
### 206a7fe7f21343d89ca83e03a0e4dc7e670cd381:tools/v0_s3_native_broker.py
00001 """The Windows side broker of the parent owned native custody.
00002 
00003 The broker is the only creator of the native services of one round.  The
00004 parent starts it, keeps its original handle and talks to it over a private
00005 channel; the broker creates the services itself, holds their original
00006 handles and stops them again.
00007 
00008 Four properties of the broker are the reason it exists at all.
00009 
00010 * **Suspend first, adopt second.**  Every service is created with
00011   ``CREATE_SUSPENDED``.  The broker holds the original process and thread
00012   handle, it assigns the process to the job object of the round and only
00013   then it resumes the thread.  A service is never started first and
00014   adopted afterwards: a process that already ran could have broken away
00015   from the job before the adoption.
00016 
00017 * **The job belongs to the round.**  One job object is created per round
00018   with ``JOB_OBJECT_LIMIT_KILL_ON_JOB_CLOSE`` and without
00019   ``JOB_OBJECT_LIMIT_BREAKAWAY_OK``.  The children of the services inherit
00020   that constraint.  When the broker closes the job handle, the round is
00021   gone.  The broker is the only owner of that handle and of the stop.
00022 
00023 * **Nothing else is inherited.**  The services get their standard output
00024   and their standard error and the null device as their standard input.
00025   They never get the control channel of the broker, the job handle or any
00026   other handle of the broker.  On Windows the inheritance set is not
00027   trusted to the default: it is passed explicitly as the handle list of
00028   the process attributes, and every handle of that list is created as
00029   inheritable while every other handle is not.
00030 
00031 * **Only what was created is stopped.**  The broker has no notion of a
00032   process name, of a pid or of a record of another owner.  It stops the
00033   handles that it holds and the job that it created, and nothing else.
00034 
00035 Nothing in this module has ever run on Windows.  Every native claim of
00036 this package therefore stays ``NOT_PROVEN``: the Windows path is written
00037 from the documented behaviour of the API and it is not a measurement.
00038 """
00039 import collections
00040 import hashlib
00041 import json
00042 import math
00043 import os
00044 import queue
00045 import re
00046 import select
00047 import subprocess
00048 import sys
00049 import threading
00050 import time
00051 from pathlib import Path
00052 
00053 PACK_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
00054 if str(PACK_ROOT) not in sys.path:
00055     sys.path.insert(0, str(PACK_ROOT))

00057 from tools.v0_s3_native_custody import (  # noqa: E402 - the path is set above
00058     CHANNEL_SCHEMA, CustodyError, MAX_DESCENDANTS, MAX_FRAME_BYTES, MAX_FRAMES,

00061 # The bounded limits of the broker.  They are internal limits of this
00062 # foundation; they are not a claim about any native Windows behaviour.
00063 WAIT_MILLISECONDS = 5000
00064 HEARTBEAT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 120
00065 CLOSE_JOIN_SECONDS = 5.0
00066 READER_JOIN_SECONDS = 2.0
00067 # The ONE cleanup budget of a round.  The stop and the cleanup of a round
00068 # share ONE absolute deadline: the deadline is generated once, at the
00069 # first stop or at the first failure of the cleanup, and it is the same
00070 # value in every layer of the cleanup.  No layer adds a budget of its own
00071 # and no layer starts a new wait with a fresh timeout.
00072 CLEANUP_BUDGET_SECONDS = 5.0
00073 # The number of the startup results that the in-flight owner of a startup
00074 # step may carry.  The channel is bounded: a startup owner that produced
00075 # more than one result at a time would be an unbounded queue.
00076 STARTUP_INFLIGHT_SLOTS = 1
00077 # The bounded wait for a startup step that did not end before the
00078 # deadline of the round.  A step that is still running after this wait
00079 # keeps its owner and the round stays unknown: the round never claims a
00080 # cleanup that it cannot prove.
00081 STARTUP_SETTLE_SECONDS = 1.0
00082 MAX_PLAN_BYTES = 64 * 1024
00083 MAX_RECEIPT_BYTES = 1024 * 1024
00084 POLL_SECONDS = 0.25
00085 
00086 # The bounded waits of the private channel.  The pipe is polled in slices
00087 # of at most 50 milliseconds, so that the cancel flag and the absolute
00088 # deadline of the round are observed even while a peer is silent.
00089 POLL_SLICE_SECONDS = 0.05
00090 # The bounded wait of the **put** of the reader of the channel.  The
00091 # put is cropped to the time that is really left until the current
00092 # effective deadline of the round and it never asks for more than the
00093 # round allows.
00094 CHANNEL_QUEUE_PUT_SECONDS = 0.5
00095 PIPE_READ_BYTES = 65536
00096 # The number of the frames of the channel that is reserved for the
00097 # terminal exchange of the round.  The heartbeat may not spend them: a
00098 # round that reached its frame budget has to fail closed and it may not
00099 # reconnect and it may not renew its lease.
00100 RESERVED_TERMINAL_FRAMES = 8
00101 
00102 # The frozen environment of every child.  The variables that the existing
00103 # authorised launch wrappers remove are removed here as well, so that a
00104 # child never inherits a proxy, a user site directory or a start-up file.
00105 REMOVED_ENVIRONMENT = ('PYTHONPATH', 'PYTHONHOME', 'PYTHONSTARTUP', 'PYTHONINSPECT',
00106                         'PYTHONUSERBASE', 'PYTHONOPTIMIZE', 'HTTP_PROXY', 'HTTPS_PROXY',
00107                         'ALL_PROXY')
00108 
00109 #: The one lock that guards the creation of the lock of the admission of
00110 #: an endpoint.  It is a lock of the **module** and it is never the lock
00111 #: of an endpoint: two endpoints never share an admission.
00112 _ADMISSION_LOCK_INIT = threading.Lock()

00117

00119         super().__init__(code)
00120         self.code = code
00121         self.detail = detail

00124 # -- the one context of a round ---------------------------------------------
00125 
00126 class _RoundContext:
00127     """The ONE finite startup and cleanup context of one round.
00128 
00129     The context holds the four things that every layer of a round has to
00130     share and that may never be copied from one layer into the next one.
00131 
00132     * The **clock** of the round.  Every deadline of the round is
00133       expressed in it, so that a deadline of one domain is never
00134       compared with a clock of another one.
00135 
00136     * The **absolute deadline** of the round.  It is bound once and it
00137       may only be shortened.
00138 
00139     * The **one cleanup deadline**.  It is published **once**, by the
00140       first caller that enters the cleanup, and every later caller
00141       reuses that very value: two callers that entered the cleanup at the
00142       same moment always see the same number, and the publication is one
00143       atomic step under the lock of the context.
00144 
00145     * The three **one way latches** of the round: the cancel, the stop
00146       and the unknown.  A latch that was set is never cleared, and a
00147       late event may never overwrite the unknown of the round.
00148 
00149     Every layer of the round holds this **one object**: the core, the
00150     loop, the transport of the channel and the endpoints of the channel.
00151     A layer that only copied the number of a deadline would be a layer
00152     with a deadline of its own, and two layers with two deadlines are
00153     two budgets.
00154     """
00155 
00156     def __init__(self, *, clock=None, wall=None):
00157         self.clock = clock or time.monotonic
00158         self.wall = wall or time.time
00159         self._lock = threading.RLock()
00160         self._absolute_deadline = None
00161         self._cleanup_deadline = None
00162         self._inflight = []
00163         self._inflight_lock = threading.Lock()
00164         # The one way latches of the round.
00165         self.cancel = threading.Event()
00166         self.stopping = threading.Event()
00167         self.unknown = threading.Event()
00168 
00169     # -- the deadlines ----------------------------------------------------
00170     def bind_absolute_deadline(self, deadline):
00171         """Bind the absolute deadline of the round.  It may only shorten."""
00172         value = _require_deadline(deadline)
00173         if value is None:
00174             return self._absolute_deadline
00175         with self._lock:
00176             if self._absolute_deadline is None or value < self._absolute_deadline:
00177                 self._absolute_deadline = value
00178             return self._absolute_deadline
00179 
00180     @property
00181     def absolute_deadline(self):
00182         with self._lock:
00183             return self._absolute_deadline
00184 
00185     def cleanup_deadline_value(self):
00186         """The published cleanup deadline, or ``None`` before the first."""
00187         with self._lock:
00188             return self._cleanup_deadline
00189 
00190     def cleanup_deadline(self, deadline=None):
00191         """Publish the ONE cleanup deadline of the round, atomically.
00192 
00193         The deadline is the earlier of the absolute deadline of the
00194         round, the fixed cleanup budget of the round and the deadline
00195         that the caller brought.  It is generated **once**: every later
00196         caller receives the very same value and may never extend it and
00197         may never replace it.
00198         """
00199         with self._lock:
00200             if self._cleanup_deadline is None:
00201                 value = self.clock() + CLEANUP_BUDGET_SECONDS
00202                 if self._absolute_deadline is not None:
00203                     value = min(value, self._absolute_deadline)
00204                 candidate = _require_deadline(deadline)
00205                 if candidate is not None:
00206                     value = min(value, candidate)
00207                 self._cleanup_deadline = value
00208             return self._cleanup_deadline
00209 
00210     def remaining_cleanup(self, deadline=None):
00211         """The seconds that are left until the cleanup deadline.
00212 
00213         The value is recomputed on every call: a wait that already
00214         consumed part of the budget never receives the whole budget
00215         again.
00216         """
00217         value = self.cleanup_deadline(deadline)
00218         return max(0.0, value - self.clock())
00219 
00220     def cleanup_expired(self):
00221         """Whether the shared cleanup deadline of the round arrived."""
00222         with self._lock:
00223             value = self._cleanup_deadline
00224         if value is None:
00225             return False
00226         return self.clock() >= value
00227 
00228     def remaining_absolute(self):
00229         """The seconds that are left until the absolute deadline.
00230 
00231         The value is recomputed on every call and it is read from the
00232         **one** context of the round: a layer that copied the number of
00233         the deadline would have a deadline of its own.  ``None`` means
00234         that the round did not bind an absolute deadline at all.
00235         """
00236         with self._lock:
00237             value = self._absolute_deadline
00238         if value is None:
00239             return None
00240         return max(0.0, value - self.clock())
00241 
00242     def effective_deadline(self, call=None, bound=None):
00243         """The ONE deadline that a call of a layer really uses.
00244 
00245         The deadline of the call, the deadline that is bound to the
00246         layer, the absolute deadline of the round and the **published**
00247         cleanup deadline of the round are one value: the earliest of
00248         the four.
00249 
00250         The published cleanup deadline is part of it.  A round that
00251         entered its cleanup publishes one deadline, and every layer that
00252         reads the context afterwards is bound by it: a transport that
00253         still waited with the long lease of the round would outlive the
00254         only cleanup budget of the round.
00255 
00256         The value is **recomputed on every call**: a deadline that was
00257         published after the call started still shortens the call that is
00258         running.
00259         """
00260         value = _require_deadline(call)
00261         for candidate in (bound, self.absolute_deadline,
00262                           self.cleanup_deadline_value()):
00263             if candidate is None:
00264                 continue
00265             if value is None or candidate < value:
00266                 value = candidate
00267         return value
00268 
00269     # -- the one way latches ----------------------------------------------
00270     def request_cancel(self):
00271         """Ask for the cancellation of the round.  One way."""
00272         self.cancel.set()
00273 
00274     def request_stop(self):
00275         """Ask for the stop of the round.  One way."""
00276         self.stopping.set()
00277 
00278     def latch_unknown(self):
00279         """Latch the unknown state of the round.  One way.
00280 
00281         The first caller learns that it latched the state and every later
00282         caller learns that the state was already latched.  A late event
00283         may never clear the latch and may never replace it.
00284         """
00285         if self.unknown.is_set():
00286             return False
00287         self.unknown.set()
00288         return True
00289 
00290     def is_unknown(self):
00291         """Whether the unknown state of the round was latched."""
00292         return self.unknown.is_set()
00293 
00294     # -- the owners of the operations in flight ---------------------------
00295     def register_inflight(self, owner):
00296         """Take the ownership of one operation that is in flight."""
00297         with self._inflight_lock:
00298             if owner not in self._inflight:
00299                 self._inflight.append(owner)
00300         return owner
00301 
00302     def release_inflight(self, owner):
00303         """Give the ownership of one operation back."""
00304         with self._inflight_lock:
00305             if owner in self._inflight:
00306                 self._inflight.remove(owner)
00307 
00308     def inflight(self):
00309         """The owners of the operations that are in flight."""
00310         with self._inflight_lock:
00311             return list(self._inflight)

00314 # -- the Windows primitive table -------------------------------------------
00315 
00316 def _windows_command_line(executable, arguments):
00317     """Build the command line of one child.
00318 
00319     The quoting is the quoting of the Microsoft C runtime: a backslash run
00320     in front of a quote is doubled and a trailing backslash run is doubled
00321     as well, so that a path with a backslash is never truncated by the
00322     parser of the child.
00323     """
00324     parts = [_quote_windows_argument(executable)]
00325     parts.extend(_quote_windows_argument(item) for item in arguments)
00326     return ' '.join(parts)
00327 
00328 
00329 def _quote_windows_argument(value):
00330     text = str(value)
00331     if text and not any(character in text for character in ' \t"'):
00332         return text
00333     quoted = ['"']
00334     backslashes = 0
00335     for character in text:
00336         if character == '\\':
00337             backslashes += 1
00338             continue
00339         if character == '"':
00340             quoted.append('\\' * (backslashes * 2 + 1))
00341             quoted.append('"')
00342             backslashes = 0
00343             continue
00344         if backslashes:
00345             quoted.append('\\' * backslashes)
00346             backslashes = 0
00347         quoted.append(character)
00348     quoted.append('\\' * (backslashes * 2))
00349     quoted.append('"')
00350     return ''.join(quoted)
00351 
00352 
00353 def _environment_block(environment):
00354     """Build the Unicode environment block of one child."""
00355     if environment is None:
00356         return None
00357     import ctypes
00358     items = sorted(environment.items(), key=lambda item: item[0].casefold())
00359     raw = ''.join('%s=%s\0' % (key, value) for key, value in items) + '\0'
00360     return ctypes.create_unicode_buffer(raw)

00362 
00363 def _windows_api_types():
00364     """The pure declaration of the Windows structures of the broker.
00365 
00366     The declaration is built **without** a native library.  It declares
00367     the layout of the structures and it returns them together with the
00368     ``ctypes`` module that built them, so that the layout can be measured
00369     and checked off Windows and so that the loaded primitive table uses
00370     exactly this declaration.
00371 
00372     The fixed width types are declared **explicitly**.  ``ctypes.wintypes``
00373     is a module of the host: its ``BOOL`` is a ``c_long`` and its
00374     ``DWORD`` is a ``c_uint``, and the size of a ``c_long`` is not the
00375     size of a Windows ``BOOL`` on every host.  A layout that was built
00376     from the types of the host would therefore be a layout of the host
00377     and not the layout of Windows.  Here a ``DWORD`` and a ``BOOL`` are
00378     always 32 bit, a ``WORD`` is always 16 bit and a handle and a
00379     ``SIZE_T`` have the width of a pointer.
00380 
00381     A name of a class body is resolved in the class namespace and in the
00382     global namespace only.  A class body therefore never assigns a name
00383     that shadows an enclosing local: ``kernel32 = kernel32`` would raise
00384     a ``NameError`` instead of copying the value, which is why the table
00385     of the broker is built from an explicit namespace and never from the
00386     body of a class.
00387     """
00388     import ctypes
00389 
00390     DWORD = ctypes.c_uint32
00391     BOOL = ctypes.c_int32
00392     WORD = ctypes.c_uint16
00393     UINT = ctypes.c_uint32
00394     LONG = ctypes.c_int32
00395     ULONG = ctypes.c_uint32
00396     HANDLE = ctypes.c_void_p
00397     SIZE_T = ctypes.c_size_t
00398     ULONG_PTR = ctypes.c_size_t
00399     LPCWSTR = ctypes.c_wchar_p
00400     LPWSTR = ctypes.c_wchar_p
00401 
00402     class STARTUPINFOW(ctypes.Structure):
00403         _fields_ = [
00404             ('cb', DWORD),
00405             ('lpReserved', LPWSTR),
00406             ('lpDesktop', LPWSTR),
00407             ('lpTitle', LPWSTR),
00408             ('dwX', DWORD),
00409             ('dwY', DWORD),
00410             ('dwXSize', DWORD),
00411             ('dwYSize', DWORD),
00412             ('dwXCountChars', DWORD),
00413             ('dwYCountChars', DWORD),
00414             ('dwFillAttribute', DWORD),
00415             ('dwFlags', DWORD),
00416             ('wShowWindow', WORD),
00417             ('cbReserved2', WORD),
00418             ('lpReserved2', ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_byte)),
00419             ('hStdInput', HANDLE),
00420             ('hStdOutput', HANDLE),
00421             ('hStdError', HANDLE),
00422         ]
00423 
00424     class STARTUPINFOEXW(ctypes.Structure):
00425         _fields_ = [('StartupInfo', STARTUPINFOW),
00426                     ('lpAttributeList', ctypes.c_void_p)]
00427 
00428     class PROCESS_INFORMATION(ctypes.Structure):
00429         _fields_ = [('hProcess', HANDLE),
00430                     ('hThread', HANDLE),
00431                     ('dwProcessId', DWORD),
00432                     ('dwThreadId', DWORD)]
00433 
00434     class SECURITY_ATTRIBUTES(ctypes.Structure):
00435         """The security attributes of one inheritable handle.
00436 
00437         ``bInheritHandle`` is a ``BOOL`` and it is a 32 bit value: a
00438         handle that is created with ``NULL`` security attributes can
00439         never be inherited at all.
00440         """
00441         _fields_ = [('nLength', DWORD),
00442                     ('lpSecurityDescriptor', ctypes.c_void_p),
00443                     ('bInheritHandle', BOOL)]
00444 
00445     class FILETIME(ctypes.Structure):
00446         _fields_ = [('dwLowDateTime', DWORD),
00447                     ('dwHighDateTime', DWORD)]
00448 
00449     class OVERLAPPED(ctypes.Structure):
00450         """The overlapped structure of one asynchronous operation.
00451 
00452         The structure owns the event of the completion and the offset
00453         of the operation.  It is released only after the completion of
00454         the operation was proven.
00455         """
00456         _fields_ = [('Internal', SIZE_T),
00457                     ('InternalHigh', SIZE_T),
00458                     ('Offset', DWORD),
00459                     ('OffsetHigh', DWORD),
00460                     ('hEvent', HANDLE)]
00461 
00462     class IO_COUNTERS(ctypes.Structure):
00463         _fields_ = [('ReadOperationCount', ctypes.c_ulonglong),
00464                     ('WriteOperationCount', ctypes.c_ulonglong),
00465                     ('OtherOperationCount', ctypes.c_ulonglong),
00466                     ('ReadTransferCount', ctypes.c_ulonglong),
00467                     ('WriteTransferCount', ctypes.c_ulonglong),
00468                     ('OtherTransferCount', ctypes.c_ulonglong)]
00469 
00470     class JOBOBJECT_BASIC_LIMIT_INFORMATION(ctypes.Structure):
00471         _fields_ = [('PerProcessUserTimeLimit', ctypes.c_longlong),
00472                     ('PerJobUserTimeLimit', ctypes.c_longlong),
00473                     ('LimitFlags', DWORD),
00474                     ('MinimumWorkingSetSize', SIZE_T),
00475                     ('MaximumWorkingSetSize', SIZE_T),
00476                     ('ActiveProcessLimit', DWORD),
00477                     ('Affinity', SIZE_T),
00478                     ('PriorityClass', DWORD),
00479                     ('SchedulingClass', DWORD)]
00480 
00481     class JOBOBJECT_EXTENDED_LIMIT_INFORMATION(ctypes.Structure):
00482         _fields_ = [('BasicLimitInformation', JOBOBJECT_BASIC_LIMIT_INFORMATION),
00483                     ('IoInfo', IO_COUNTERS),
00484                     ('ProcessMemoryLimit', SIZE_T),
00485                     ('JobMemoryLimit', SIZE_T),
00486                     ('PeakProcessMemoryUsed', SIZE_T),
00487                     ('PeakJobMemoryUsed', SIZE_T)]
00488 
00489     class JOBOBJECT_BASIC_PROCESS_ID_LIST(ctypes.Structure):
00490         """The process id list of a job object.
00491 
00492         The list is an **inline** array of ``ULONG_PTR`` and not a
00493         pointer: the first process id is written at the offset of the
00494         array and every other id follows it.  A structure that declared
00495         a pointer field here would read the first id as an address and
00496         would dereference it.
00497         """
00498         _fields_ = [('NumberOfAssignedProcesses', DWORD),
00499                     ('NumberOfProcessIdsInList', DWORD),
00500                     ('ProcessIdList', ULONG_PTR * 1)]
00501 
00502     return {
00503         'ctypes': ctypes,
00504         'DWORD': DWORD,
00505         'BOOL': BOOL,
00506         'WORD': WORD,
00507         'UINT': UINT,
00508         'LONG': LONG,
00509         'ULONG': ULONG,
00510         'HANDLE': HANDLE,
00511         'SIZE_T': SIZE_T,
00512         'ULONG_PTR': ULONG_PTR,
00513         'LPCWSTR': LPCWSTR,
00514         'LPWSTR': LPWSTR,
00515         'STARTUPINFOW': STARTUPINFOW,
00516         'STARTUPINFOEXW': STARTUPINFOEXW,
00517         'PROCESS_INFORMATION': PROCESS_INFORMATION,
00518         'SECURITY_ATTRIBUTES': SECURITY_ATTRIBUTES,
00519         'FILETIME': FILETIME,
00520         'OVERLAPPED': OVERLAPPED,
00521         'IO_COUNTERS': IO_COUNTERS,
00522         'JOBOBJECT_BASIC_LIMIT_INFORMATION': JOBOBJECT_BASIC_LIMIT_INFORMATION,
00523         'JOBOBJECT_EXTENDED_LIMIT_INFORMATION': JOBOBJECT_EXTENDED_LIMIT_INFORMATION,
00524         'JOBOBJECT_BASIC_PROCESS_ID_LIST': JOBOBJECT_BASIC_PROCESS_ID_LIST,
00525     }

00528 def _declare_windows_primitives(kernel32, types):
00529     """Declare the prototypes of the Windows primitives of the broker.
00530 
00531     Every prototype is explicit.  A handle is a ``HANDLE``, a pointer is
00532     an explicit ``ctypes`` pointer, a size is a ``SIZE_T`` and every
00533     value that Windows defines as a 32 bit number is a ``DWORD`` or a
00534     ``BOOL``.  A primitive without a declared prototype takes its
00535     arguments as ``c_int`` and would truncate a 64 bit handle.
00536 
00537     The declaration is applied to the primitive table that is handed in,
00538     so the very same code declares the table of a real run and the table
00539     of an offline inspection.
00540     """
00541     ctypes = types['ctypes']
00542     void_p = ctypes.c_void_p
00543     size_t = types['SIZE_T']
00544     handle = types['HANDLE']
00545     boolean = types['BOOL']
00546     dword = types['DWORD']
00547     uint = types['UINT']
00548     ulong = types['ULONG']
00549 
00550     kernel32.CreateJobObjectW.argtypes = [ctypes.POINTER(types['SECURITY_ATTRIBUTES']),
00551                                           types['LPCWSTR']]
00552     kernel32.CreateJobObjectW.restype = handle
00553     kernel32.SetInformationJobObject.argtypes = [handle, ctypes.c_int, void_p, dword]
00554     kernel32.SetInformationJobObject.restype = boolean
00555     kernel32.CreateProcessW.argtypes = [types['LPCWSTR'], types['LPWSTR'],
00556                                         ctypes.POINTER(types['SECURITY_ATTRIBUTES']),
00557                                         ctypes.POINTER(types['SECURITY_ATTRIBUTES']),
00558                                         boolean, dword, void_p, types['LPCWSTR'],
00559                                         ctypes.POINTER(types['STARTUPINFOW']),
00560                                         ctypes.POINTER(types['PROCESS_INFORMATION'])]
00561     kernel32.CreateProcessW.restype = boolean
00562     kernel32.AssignProcessToJobObject.argtypes = [handle, handle]
00563     kernel32.AssignProcessToJobObject.restype = boolean
00564     kernel32.ResumeThread.argtypes = [handle]
00565     kernel32.ResumeThread.restype = dword
00566     kernel32.TerminateProcess.argtypes = [handle, uint]
00567     kernel32.TerminateProcess.restype = boolean
00568     kernel32.TerminateJobObject.argtypes = [handle, uint]
00569     kernel32.TerminateJobObject.restype = boolean
00570     kernel32.WaitForSingleObject.argtypes = [handle, dword]
00571     kernel32.WaitForSingleObject.restype = dword
00572     kernel32.GetExitCodeProcess.argtypes = [handle, ctypes.POINTER(dword)]
00573     kernel32.GetExitCodeProcess.restype = boolean
00574     kernel32.GetProcessTimes.argtypes = [handle,
