02084 
02085     A handle is an integer in the offline validation and an opaque
02086     object in a real run: the evidence of the round carries a printable
02087     name of it and it never carries the object itself.  A callable is
02088     never serialisable either and it is named instead.
02089     """
02090     if value is None or isinstance(value, (bool, int, float, str)):
02091         return value
02092     return repr(value)
02093 
02094 
02095 class _PendingResources:
02096     """The pending resource table of one round.
02097 
02098     Every handle that this round creates enters this table in the moment
02099     of its successful creation.  A handle **stays** in the table until it
02100     is closed with a confirmed close: the handover of a handle to its
02101     owner changes the owner and nothing else.  The handle is a resource
02102     that this round owns and it is named until it is really closed.
02103 
02104     A handle that could not be closed stays in the table with its owner:
02105     it is a resource that this round still owns and it is named in the
02106     evidence of the round.
02107 
02108     A handle of another owner is never in this table and it is never
02109     touched.
02110 
02111     The table carries the **owner object** of a handle and not only its
02112     name: a handle that is owned by a live reader is protected from the
02113     pending pass by the fact that the reader is alive and not by a
02114     string that somebody may write.  The owner object is the reader
02115     itself and the table asks it.
02116     """
02117 
02118     def __init__(self, win32):
02119         self._win32 = win32
02120         self._items = []
02121         self._lock = threading.RLock()
02122 
02123     def add(self, handle, kind, *, role=None, owner=None, owner_ref=None,
02124             closeable=None):
02125         """Enter one handle into the table of the round.
02126 
02127         The ``closeable`` predicate is the **close eligibility** of the
02128         handle.  It is asked by every close of the table and it is the
02129         authority of the question "may this handle be closed now?": a
02130         control handle of a process whose exit was not confirmed is
02131         never closed, and the predicate is the **owner** of that fact
02132         and not a name of it.
02133 
02134         The ``owner_ref`` is the object of the owner of the handle: the
02135         pending pass asks it before it closes the handle.
02136         """
02137         item = {'handle': handle, 'kind': kind, 'role': role,
02138                 'owner': owner or 'PENDING', 'owner_ref': owner_ref,
02139                 'closeable': closeable, 'closed': False}
02140         with self._lock:
02141             self._items.append(item)
02142         return item
02143 
02144     def take(self, handle):
02145         """Remove one handle from the table: its owner holds it now."""
02146         with self._lock:
02147             for index, item in enumerate(self._items):
02148                 if item['handle'] == handle:
02149                     del self._items[index]
02150                     return item
02151         return None
02152 
02153     def find(self, handle):
02154         """The entry of one handle, without removing it from the table.
02155 
02156         The entry carries the **owner** of the handle and its close
02157         eligibility.  A close that is confirmed needs the owner of the
02158         handle in order to notify it: a notification that names a copy
02159         of the entry would notify nobody.
02160         """
02161         with self._lock:
02162             for item in self._items:
02163                 if item['handle'] == handle:
02164                     return item
02165         return None
02166 
02167     def transfer(self, handle, owner, *, owner_ref=None):
02168         """Hand one pending handle over to its owner.
02169 
02170         The owner of the handle is only changed after the handover
02171         succeeded.  The handle stays in the table until it is closed: it
02172         is a resource that this round owns and it is named until then.
02173         A handle that could not be handed over keeps the owner
02174         ``PENDING`` and it is closed by the round.
02175 
02176         The ``owner_ref`` is the **object** of the new owner.  It is
02177         what the pending pass asks before it closes a handle: a read end
02178         that a live reader still reads from is never closed under it.
02179         """
02180         with self._lock:
02181             for item in self._items:
02182                 if item['handle'] == handle:
02183                     item['owner'] = owner
02184                     if owner_ref is not None:
02185                         item['owner_ref'] = owner_ref
02186                     return item
02187         return None
02188 
02189     def pending(self):
02190         """The handles that this round still owns and has not closed."""
02191         with self._lock:
02192             return [dict(item) for item in self._items]
02193 
02194     def pending_facts(self):
02195         """The pending handles of the round as **serialisable** facts.
02196 
02197         The close eligibility of a handle is a **callable** over the
02198         owner of the handle and a callable is never serialised.  The
02199         facts name the eligibility as a name and they keep the
02200         **original** reference of the handle: a resource of the round is
02201         never replaced by a copy of a message.
02202         """
02203         facts = []
02204         for item in self.pending():
02205             predicate = item.get('closeable')
02206             if predicate is None:
02207                 eligibility = 'NONE'
02208             elif callable(predicate):
02209                 eligibility = 'CALLABLE'
02210             else:
02211                 eligibility = 'FROZEN'
02212             facts.append({'handle': _json_fact(item.get('handle')),
02213                           'kind': item.get('kind'),
02214                           'role': item.get('role'),
02215                           'owner': item.get('owner'),
02216                           'closed': bool(item.get('closed')),
02217                           'eligibility': eligibility})
02218         return facts
02219 
02220     def close_all(self, failures, on_closed=None, protect=None):
02221         """Close every handle that is still pending and name the failures.
02222 
02223         A handle that could not be closed stays in the table: it is a
02224         resource that this round still owns and it is named in the
02225         evidence of the round.  It is never left to the garbage
02226         collector and it is never closed twice.
02227 
02228         Every handle is closed on its own: a cancellation in the middle
02229         of the group never skips the handles that follow it.  The
02230         cancellation is raised after every handle was coordinated and it
02231         is never turned into an ordinary evidence.
02232 
02233         A handle that was closed with a **confirmed** close is reported
02234         to the owner of the table through ``on_closed``: the core has to
02235         learn that the handle is really closed, so that it never names a
02236         closed handle any more.  The report happens before the
02237         cancellation is raised: the bookkeeping of the core is never
02238         left behind the error that left the cleanup.
02239 
02240         The ``protect`` predicate is the **real** guard of the pass: a
02241         handle whose owner is still using it is never closed under it,
02242         whatever the branch that reached the pass did before.  The
02243         guard is asked about the owner and not about a name of an owner.
02244 
02245         The **close eligibility** of a handle is asked here as well: a
02246         handle that may not be closed yet - the control handle of a
02247         process whose exit was not confirmed - is never closed by the
02248         pass.  The eligibility comes from the **same** owner that the
02249         release of the records asks: the pass may not decide the
02250         question on its own and it may not close behind the back of the
02251         owner of the handle.
02252         """
02253         remaining = []
02254         cancellations = []
02255         with self._lock:
02256             items = list(self._items)
02257         for item in items:
02258             if protect is not None and protect(item):
02259                 # The handle is in use by a live owner: it is never
02260                 # closed under it and it keeps its owner.
02261                 remaining.append(item)
02262                 failures.append({'stage': 'close_pending', 'kind': item['kind'],
02263                                  'role': item['role'],
02264                                  'code': 'BROKER_PENDING_HANDLE_IN_USE'})
02265                 continue
02266             if not self._is_closeable(item):
02267                 # The handle may not be closed yet: it keeps its owner
02268                 # and the round keeps the fact.
02269                 remaining.append(item)
02270                 failures.append({'stage': 'close_pending', 'kind': item['kind'],
02271                                  'role': item['role'],
02272                                  'code': 'BROKER_PENDING_CLOSE_NOT_ELIGIBLE'})
02273                 continue
02274             try:
02275                 self._win32.close_handle(item['handle'])
02276             except BrokerError as error:
02277                 failures.append({'stage': 'close_pending', 'kind': item['kind'],
02278                                  'role': item['role'], 'code': error.code})
02279                 remaining.append(item)
02280             except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as error:
02281                 failures.append({'stage': 'close_pending', 'kind': item['kind'],
02282                                  'role': item['role'],
02283                                  'error_type': type(error).__name__,
02284                                  'cancellation': True})
02285                 remaining.append(item)
02286                 cancellations.append(error)
02287             except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
02288                 failures.append({'stage': 'close_pending', 'kind': item['kind'],
02289                                  'role': item['role'],
02290                                  'error_type': type(error).__name__})
02291                 remaining.append(item)
02292             else:
02293                 item['closed'] = True
02294                 if on_closed is not None:
02295                     on_closed(item)
02296         with self._lock:
02297             self._items = remaining
02298         if cancellations:
02299             raise cancellations[0]
02300         return remaining
02301 
02302     @staticmethod
02303     def _is_closeable(item):
02304         """Whether one pending handle may be closed now.
02305 
02306         The predicate of the entry is the authority of the question.  A
02307         predicate that cannot answer protects the handle: an unknown
02308         fact is never a permission to close.
02309 
02310         The predicate is a **callable** over the original owner of the
02311         handle.  A frozen answer - a boolean that was taken at the
02312         moment of the creation of the handle - is **not** an
02313         eligibility: it protects the handle, because the current fact
02314         of the owner is the only authority of the question.
02315         """
02316         predicate = item.get('closeable')
02317         if predicate is None:
02318             return True
02319         if not callable(predicate):
02320             # A frozen answer is not an eligibility.
02321             return False
02322         try:
02323             return bool(predicate())
02324         except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
02325             return False
02326 
02327 
02328 # -- the core of the broker ------------------------------------------------
02329 
02330 class BrokerCore:
02331     """The job and the handle custody of one native custody round.
02332 
02333     The core owns exactly one job object and the original handles of the
02334     processes of the round.  Every process of the round is created
02335     suspended, it is assigned to the job of the round and only then it is
02336     resumed.
02337 
02338     A failure never falls back to a weaker behaviour.  There is no retry
02339     without the job, there is no adoption of a process that was not
02340     created here and there is no stop of a process that this core does not
02341     hold a handle of.
02342     """
02343 
02344     def __init__(self, *, win32, plan, run_root, clock=None, wall=None,
02345                  context=None):
02346         self.win32 = win32
02347         self.plan = plan
02348         self.run_root = Path(run_root)
02349         self._clock = clock or time.monotonic
02350         self._wall = wall or time.time
02351         # The ONE context of the round.  The core, the loop, the
02352         # transport of the channel and the endpoints of the channel hold
02353         # this very object: a layer that only copied the number of a
02354         # deadline would be a layer with a deadline of its own.
02355         self.context = context if context is not None else _RoundContext(
02356             clock=self._clock, wall=self._wall)
02357         # The façade of the primitives reads the absolute deadline and
02358         # the published cleanup deadline of the round from the **same**
02359         # context: a recovery of the façade may not open a budget of
02360         # its own.
02361         binder = getattr(self.win32, 'bind_context', None)
02362         if callable(binder):
02363             try:
02364                 binder(self.context)
02365             except BaseException:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
02366                 pass
02367         self.state = 'NEW'
02368         self.job = None
02369         self.job_kill_on_close = False
02370         self.job_closed = False
02371         self.job_terminated = False
02372         self.job_active = None
02373         self.job_active_known = False
02374         self.job_process_ids = []
02375         self.records = []
02376         self.failures = []
02377         self.readers = []
02378         self.reader_states = []
02379         self.new_text_bytes = 0
02380         self.stop_reason = None
02381         self._stream_failure_reported = False
02382         self._text_failure_reported = False
02383         self._pending = _PendingResources(win32)
02384         # The close eligibility of the control handle of every record of
02385         # the round.  The map is keyed by the identity of the record and
02386         # it holds the **callable** over that record: the pending table
02387         # and the release of the records ask the very same callable and
02388         # they never disagree about the current exit fact.
02389         self._process_eligibility = {}
02390         # The resources that this core could not release.  A resource
02391         # that could not be closed keeps its original reference and its
02392         # owner here: it is named in the evidence of the round and it is
02393         # never replaced by a copy of an error message.
02394         self._retained_resources = []
02395         # The cancellations that were observed during the coordination of
02396         # the readers.  They are raised after every reader was
02397         # coordinated and they never replace an ordinary failure.
02398         self._readers_cancelled = []
02399         # The cancellations that were observed during the wait of the
02400         # original processes.  They are raised after every process was
02401         # coordinated.
02402         self._processes_cancelled = []
02403         # The absolute deadline of the round.  It is bound by the loop
02404         # before the job of the round exists and it is the upper bound of
02405         # the one cleanup deadline of the round.
02406         self._absolute_deadline = None
02407         # The ONE cleanup deadline of the round.  It is generated at the
02408         # first stop or at the first failure of the cleanup and it is the
02409         # same value in every step of the cleanup.
02410         self._cleanup_deadline_value = None
02411         self._released = False
02412         self._abort_reported = False
02413         self.broker_identity = {
02414             'pid': os.getpid(),
02415             'executable': sys.executable,
02416             'reported_by_the_broker': True,
02417             'identity_source': 'CHILD_CLAIM',
02418         }
02419 
02420     # -- the deadlines of the round ---------------------------------------
02421     def bind_clock(self, clock):
02422         """Bind the clock of the round to this core.
02423 
02424         The core, the loop and the context of the round read the same
02425         clock: the absolute deadline of the round and the ONE cleanup
02426         deadline are in one domain and a deadline of one domain is never
02427         compared with a clock of another one.
02428         """
02429         if clock is not None:
02430             self._clock = clock
02431             self.context.clock = clock
02432         return self._clock
02433 
02434     def bind_absolute_deadline(self, deadline):
02435         """Bind the absolute deadline of the round to this core.
02436 
02437         The deadline may only shorten the deadline that is already
02438         bound.  It is the upper bound of the one cleanup deadline of the
02439         round: the cleanup never outlives the round itself.
02440         """
02441         value = self.context.bind_absolute_deadline(deadline)
02442         self._absolute_deadline = value
02443         # The façade of the primitives is bound to the same deadline: its
02444         # waits are bounded by the deadline of the round and they never
02445         # start a wait with a fresh timeout of their own.
02446         binder = getattr(self.win32, 'bind_deadline', None)
02447         if callable(binder):
02448             try:
02449                 binder(value, self._clock)
02450             except BaseException:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
02451                 pass
02452         return value
02453 
02454     def cleanup_deadline(self, deadline=None):
02455         """The ONE absolute deadline of the cleanup of this round.
02456 
02457         The deadline is generated once, at the first stop or at the first
02458         failure of the cleanup, and it is published **atomically** by the
02459         context of the round.  Two callers that entered the cleanup at
02460         the same moment therefore see the very same value and the
02461         deadline is never generated twice.
02462 
02463         It is the earlier of the absolute deadline of the round and the
02464         fixed cleanup budget, measured from the moment of the first
02465         cleanup.  Every later call returns the very same value: no layer
02466         of the cleanup adds a budget of its own and no layer starts a
02467         new wait with a fresh timeout.
02468         """
02469         value = self.context.cleanup_deadline(deadline)
02470         self._cleanup_deadline_value = value
02471         return value
02472 
02473     def remaining_cleanup_seconds(self, deadline=None):
02474         """The seconds that are left until the cleanup deadline.
02475 
02476         The value is recomputed on every call: a wait that already
02477         consumed part of the budget never gets the whole budget again.
02478         """
02479         return self.context.remaining_cleanup(deadline)
02480 
02481     def check_startup_budget(self, stage):
02482         """Check the shared budget of the startup **before** a step.
02483 
02484         A step of the startup of the round - the creation of a process,
02485         the assignment of a process to the job, the resume of its
02486         thread, the start of a reader and the creation of the next role
02487         - checks the shared cancel and the shared deadline of the round
02488         before it happens and after it happened.
02489 
02490         A step that runs after the deadline arrived may only clean up:
02491         it never resumes a thread and it never creates the next role.
02492         """
02493         context = self.context
02494         if context.cancel.is_set() or context.is_unknown():
02495             raise BrokerError('BROKER_STARTUP_CANCELLED', stage)
02496         deadline = context.absolute_deadline
02497         if deadline is not None and self._clock() >= deadline:
02498             raise BrokerError('BROKER_STARTUP_DEADLINE_EXCEEDED', stage)
02499         return True
02500 
02501     def _retain_resource(self, kind, handle, code=None, owner='CORE_RETAINED'):
02502         """Keep the original reference of a resource that was not released."""
02503         for item in self._retained_resources:
02504             if item['kind'] == kind and item['handle'] is handle:
02505                 return item
02506         item = {'kind': kind, 'handle': handle, 'owner': owner, 'code': code}
02507         self._retained_resources.append(item)
02508         return item
02509 
02510     def _release_resource(self, kind, handle):
02511         """Forget a resource that was released with a confirmed close.
02512 
02513         The **original reference** of a resource is its identity: a
02514         handle that was really closed is forgotten whatever name it was
02515         retained under.  A resource that was not closed keeps its owner
02516         and it stays named: the retained list is never cleared to make a
02517         check green.
02518         """
02519         self._retained_resources = [item for item in self._retained_resources
02520                                    if item['handle'] is not handle]
02521 
02522     def _adopt_from_api(self, entries, role):
02523         """Take the named handles over from the façade of the primitives.
02524 
02525         The receiver registers the handles and the façade forgets them
02526         in **one** atomic step.  A handle that both of them names would
02527         have two independent owners and the recovery of the façade could
02528         close a handle that a live reader of the round is reading from.
02529         A handle that left the façade before its receiver registered it
02530         would have no owner at all.
02531 
02532         The façade that does not offer the atomic handover is asked to
02533         forget the handles after the registration: the receiver is
02534         always registered first, so that a failure in between leaves the
02535         handle with **two** owners and never with none.
02536         """
02537         items = list(entries)
02538 
02539         def register(handles):
02540             for handle, kind in handles:
02541                 self._pending.add(handle, kind, role=role)
02542 
02543         handover = getattr(self.win32, 'handover', None)
02544         if callable(handover):
02545             handover(items, owner='CORE', register=register)
02546             return
02547         register(items)
02548         forget = getattr(self.win32, 'forget_handle', None)
02549         if callable(forget):
02550             for handle, _kind in items:
02551                 forget(handle)
02552 
02553     def _api_retained(self):
02554         """The original handles that the Windows façade still holds.
02555 
02556         The façade is the owner of a handle that it could not hand over
02557         and it is the owner of the recovery of that handle.  The core
02558         keeps the façade, so the core can always reach these handles.
02559 
02560         The names of the handles are the printable ones: the evidence of
02561         a round is a document and it never carries a raw handle value.
02562         """
02563         getter = getattr(self.win32, 'retained_handles', None)
02564         if not callable(getter):
02565             return []
02566         try:
02567             entries = list(getter())
02568         except BaseException:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
02569             return []
02570         return [{'kind': entry.get('kind'), 'pid': entry.get('pid'),
02571                  'stage': entry.get('stage'), 'owner': entry.get('owner'),
02572                  'failures': list(entry.get('failures', ())),
02573                  'handle_repr': repr(entry.get('handle'))}
02574                 for entry in entries]
02575 
02576     # -- the arming of the round ------------------------------------------
02577     def arm(self):
02578         """Create the job object of the round.
02579 
02580         The job is created before any process of the round exists, so that
02581         every process of the round can be assigned to it before it runs.
02582 
02583         The shared budget of the startup is checked **before** the
02584         creation of the job and **after** it: a job that was created
02585         after the deadline of the round arrived is only cleaned up and it
02586         is never armed.
02587         """
02588         if self.state != 'NEW':
02589             raise BrokerError('BROKER_STATE_INVALID', self.state)
02590         self.state = 'VALIDATED'
02591         self.check_startup_budget('ARM:JOB')
02592         try:
02593             self.job = self.win32.create_job()
02594         except BrokerError as error:
02595             self.failures.append({'stage': 'create_job', 'code': error.code})
02596             self.state = 'UNKNOWN_RETAINED'
02597             raise
02598         self._pending.add(self.job, 'job')
02599         self._pending.transfer(self.job, 'CORE')
02600         try:
02601             self.win32.set_job_kill_on_close(self.job)
02602         except BrokerError as error:
02603             self.failures.append({'stage': 'set_job_limits', 'code': error.code})
02604             closed = self._close_handle('job', self.job)
02605             if closed:
02606                 # The job handle was closed with a confirmed close: it may
02607                 # be forgotten.
02608                 self.job = None
02609             else:
02610                 # The job handle was not closed.  The original reference
02611                 # stays with its owner and it is named: a handle that may
02612                 # not be closed is never forgotten and it is never
02613                 # replaced by a copy of an error message.
02614                 self._retain_resource('job', self.job, error.code)
02615             self.state = 'UNKNOWN_RETAINED'
02616             raise
02617         self.job_kill_on_close = True
02618         self.state = 'ARMED'
02619         # The budget is checked after the creation of the job: a job that
02620         # was created after the deadline arrived is only cleaned up.
02621         self.check_startup_budget('ARM:DONE')
02622         return self.evidence()
02623 
02624     # -- the launch of the roles ------------------------------------------
02625     def launch(self):
02626         """Create the roles of the round, one after the other.
02627 
02628         The launch has an independent ``finally``: a failure of a role,
02629         a refusal of the broker and a cancellation all release the
02630         resources of the round before the failure leaves this method.
02631         The cancellation itself is not swallowed: it continues to
02632         propagate after the cleanup.
02633         """
02634         if self.state != 'ARMED':
02635             raise BrokerError('BROKER_STATE_INVALID', self.state)
02636         try:
02637             for role in self.plan.roles:
02638                 # The shared budget of the startup is checked before
02639                 # every role: a role that is created after the deadline
02640                 # of the round arrived is a role that nobody may stop.
02641                 self.check_startup_budget('ROLE:' + role['role'])
02642                 self._launch_role(role)
02643         except BaseException:
02644             # Any failure of the launch, a cancellation included, ends
02645             # the round closed: the job, the pipes and the original
02646             # handles of this round are released here.
02647             self._abort_round('LAUNCH_ABORTED')
02648             raise
02649         self.state = 'RUNNING'
02650         return self.evidence()
02651 
02652     def _launch_role(self, role):
02653         name = role['role']
02654         owned = [record['pid'] for record in self.records if record['pid']]
02655         command = role_command(role, self.run_root, owned)
02656         record = {'role': name,
02657                   'executable': command['executable'],
02658                   'executable_sha256': role['tools'][0]['sha256'],
02659                   'arguments': list(command['arguments']),
02660                   'working_directory': command['working_directory'],
02661                   'log': role['log'],
02662                   'created': False, 'assigned': False, 'resumed': False,
02663                   'pid': None, 'creation_time': None,
02664                   'terminated': False, 'exited': False, 'exit_code': None,
02665                   'process_handle_closed': False, 'thread_handle_closed': False,
02666                   'stdout_bytes': 0, 'stderr_bytes': 0,
02667                   'stdout_overflow': False, 'stderr_overflow': False,
02668                   'failure': None,
02669                   'pending_handles': []}
02670         self.records.append(record)
02671         try:
02672             self._prepare_directories(role)
02673         except OSError as error:
02674             record['failure'] = 'BROKER_ROLE_DIRECTORY_FAILED'
02675             self.failures.append({'stage': 'role_directory', 'role': name,
02676                                   'error_type': type(error).__name__})
02677             self._abort_round('ROLE_DIRECTORY_FAILED')
02678             raise BrokerError('BROKER_ROLE_DIRECTORY_FAILED', name)
02679         try:
02680             # The shared budget of the startup is checked **before** every
02681             # pipe: a pipe that is created after the deadline of the round
02682             # arrived is a pipe that nobody may close.
02683             self.check_startup_budget('PIPE:stdout:' + name)
02684             stdout_read, stdout_write = self.win32.create_pipe('stdout')
02685             # The two ends are handed over to the core in one step: the
02686             # façade forgets them and the core registers them, so that
02687             # the two tables never name the same handle.
02688             self._adopt_from_api([(stdout_read, 'pipe_read'),
02689                                   (stdout_write, 'pipe_write')], name)
02690             self.check_startup_budget('PIPE:stderr:' + name)
02691             stderr_read, stderr_write = self.win32.create_pipe('stderr')
02692             self._adopt_from_api([(stderr_read, 'pipe_read'),
02693                                   (stderr_write, 'pipe_write')], name)
02694             self.check_startup_budget('PIPE_DONE:' + name)
02695         except BrokerError as error:
02696             record['failure'] = error.code
02697             self.failures.append({'stage': 'create_pipe', 'role': name, 'code': error.code})
02698             self._abort_round('PIPE_FAILED')
02699             raise
02700         try:
02701             # The shared budget of the startup is checked **before** the
02702             # creation: a role that is created after the deadline of the
02703             # round arrived would be a role that nobody may stop.
02704             self.check_startup_budget('CREATE:' + name)
02705             created = self.win32.create_process_suspended(
02706                 executable=command['executable'], arguments=command['arguments'],
02707                 working_directory=command['working_directory'],
02708                 stdout_handle=stdout_write, stderr_handle=stderr_write,
02709                 environment=command['environment'])
02710         except BrokerError as error:
02711             record['failure'] = error.code
02712             self.failures.append({'stage': 'create_process', 'role': name,
02713                                   'code': error.code})
02714             # The façade keeps the original handles of a process that it
02715             # could not hand over.  They are named here: the core holds
02716             # the façade and the recovery of the façade can reach them.
02717             retained = self._api_retained()
02718             if retained:
02719                 self.failures.append({'stage': 'create_process_retained',
02720                                       'role': name, 'retained': retained})
02721             self._abort_round('CREATE_FAILED')
02722             raise
02723         # The original handles of the process enter the pending table of
02724         # the round in the moment of their creation and the core takes
02725         # them right away: it is their owner from now on and it closes
02726         # them when it releases the records.
02727         #
02728         # The **eligibility** of the control handle is a callable over
02729         # the ORIGINAL record: the pending pass asks it at the moment
02730         # of its close and it reads the current exit fact of the
02731         # record.  The release of the records asks the very same
02732         # callable, so that the record and the pending table never
02733         # disagree about the question "may this handle be closed now?".
02734         #
02735         # The registration happens **before** the check of the shared
02736         # budget that follows the creation and before every other step
02737         # that may raise.  A process that was created after the deadline
02738         # arrived is only cleaned up and it is never owned by nobody:
02739         # the lease of the round may end, the ownership of the handles
02740         # may not.
02741         eligibility = self._process_closeable(record)
02742         self._process_eligibility[id(record)] = eligibility
02743         self._pending.add(created.process, 'process', role=name,
02744                           closeable=eligibility)
02745         self._pending.add(created.thread, 'thread', role=name)
02746         self._pending.transfer(created.process, 'CORE')
02747         self._pending.transfer(created.thread, 'CORE')
02748         record['pending_handles'].append({'kind': 'process', 'owner': 'CORE'})
02749         record['pending_handles'].append({'kind': 'thread', 'owner': 'CORE'})
02750         record['created'] = True
02751         record['pid'] = created.pid
02752         record['creation_time'] = created.creation_time
02753         record['process_handle'] = created.process
02754         record['thread_handle'] = created.thread
02755         for failure in getattr(created, 'failures', ()):
02756             self.failures.append({'stage': 'create_process', 'role': name,
02757                                   'code': failure})
02758         try:
02759             # The budget is checked again after the creation: a process
02760             # that was created after the deadline arrived is only
02761             # cleaned up, it is never assigned and it is never resumed.
02762             self.check_startup_budget('CREATED:' + name)
02763         except BrokerError as error:
02764             record['failure'] = error.code
02765             self.failures.append({'stage': 'create_process', 'role': name,
02766                                   'code': error.code})
02767             # The façade keeps the original handles of a process that it
02768             # could not hand over.  They are named here: the core holds
02769             # the façade and the recovery of the façade can reach them.
02770             retained = self._api_retained()
02771             if retained:
02772                 self.failures.append({'stage': 'create_process_retained',
02773                                       'role': name, 'retained': retained})
02774             self._abort_round('CREATE_FAILED')
02775             raise
02776         # The write ends belong to the child now; the broker keeps the read
02777         # ends only.  The copies of the broker are closed and they leave
02778         # the pending table: they are not pending any more.
02779         self._release_pending(stdout_write)
02780         self._release_pending(stderr_write)
02781         try:
02782             # The shared budget is checked before the assignment and
02783             # before the resume: a thread that is resumed after the
02784             # deadline of the round arrived is a service that runs
02785             # outside of the round.
02786             self.check_startup_budget('ASSIGN:' + name)
02787             self.win32.assign_process_to_job(self.job, created.process)
02788         except BrokerError as error:
02789             record['failure'] = error.code
02790             self.failures.append({'stage': 'assign', 'role': name, 'code': error.code})
02791             # The process is still suspended: it never ran.  It is stopped
02792             # through its own original handle and its exit is confirmed.
02793             self._terminate_owned(created.process, record, 'ASSIGN_FAILED')
02794             self._abort_round('ASSIGN_FAILED')
02795             raise
02796         record['assigned'] = True
02797         try:
02798             self.check_startup_budget('RESUME:' + name)
02799             self.win32.resume_thread(created.thread)
02800         except BrokerError as error:
02801             record['failure'] = error.code
02802             self.failures.append({'stage': 'resume', 'role': name, 'code': error.code})
02803             self._abort_round('RESUME_FAILED')
02804             raise
02805         record['resumed'] = True
02806         try:
02807             self.check_startup_budget('READER:' + name)
02808             self._start_reader(record, stdout_read, 'stdout')
02809             self.check_startup_budget('READER:' + name)
02810             self._start_reader(record, stderr_read, 'stderr')
02811         except BrokerError as error:
02812             record['failure'] = error.code
02813             self.failures.append({'stage': 'reader', 'role': name, 'code': error.code})
02814             self._abort_round('READER_FAILED')
02815             raise
02816 
02817     def _release_pending(self, handle):
02818         """Release one pending handle that the child took over."""
02819         item = self._pending.take(handle)
02820         if item is None:
02821             return False
02822         try:
02823             self.win32.close_handle(handle)
02824         except BrokerError as error:
02825             self.failures.append({'stage': 'close_stream', 'kind': item['kind'],
02826                                   'role': item['role'], 'code': error.code})
02827             self._pending.add(handle, item['kind'], role=item['role'])
02828             return False
02829         except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as error:
02830             # A cancellation never becomes an ordinary evidence: it is
02831             # named and it is raised, so that the cleanups that follow
02832             # it still run.
02833             self.failures.append({'stage': 'close_stream', 'kind': item['kind'],
02834                                   'role': item['role'],
02835                                   'error_type': type(error).__name__,
02836                                   'cancellation': True})
02837             self._pending.add(handle, item['kind'], role=item['role'])
02838             raise
02839         except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
02840             self.failures.append({'stage': 'close_stream', 'kind': item['kind'],
02841                                   'role': item['role'],
02842                                   'error_type': type(error).__name__})
02843             self._pending.add(handle, item['kind'], role=item['role'])
02844             return False
02845         return True
02846 
02847     def _prepare_directories(self, role):
02848         """Create the fresh directories of a role.
02849 
02850         A directory that already exists is refused: a stale log, a stale
02851         pid lease or a stale sampler output of an earlier round may never
02852         be read as the output of this round.
02853 
02854         The user directory of the ComfyUI role is the directory that the
02855         plan closed and not a fixed name: the round creates the
02856         directory that the command of the role really uses.
02857         """
02858         name = role['role']
02859         if name == 'COMFY':
02860             (Path(self.run_root) / 'temp' / 'service-os').mkdir(parents=True,
02861                                                                 exist_ok=True)
02862             (Path(self.run_root) / role['data']['user_directory']).mkdir(
02863                 parents=True, exist_ok=True)
02864         elif name == 'SAMPLER':
02865             (Path(self.run_root) / 'temp' / 'resource-sampler').mkdir(
02866                 parents=True, exist_ok=False)
02867 
02868     def _start_reader(self, record, handle, stream):
02869         """Hand the read end of a pipe over to its reader.
02870 
02871         The reader is the owner of the read end from now on: the
02872         handover changes the owner of the handle and nothing else.  The
02873         handle stays in the pending table of the round until it is
02874         really closed, and the reader closes it when it is stopped.
02875 
02876         The reader **object** is registered as the owner of the handle
02877         and not only its name: the pending pass asks the reader whether
02878         it is still alive before it closes the read end under it.
02879         """
02880         reader = _BoundedStreamReader(self.win32, handle, limit=MAX_STREAM_BYTES,
02881                                       name=record['role'] + '-' + stream)
02882         self.readers.append(reader)
02883         self._pending.transfer(handle, 'READER', owner_ref=reader)
02884         record['pending_handles'].append({'kind': 'pipe_read',
02885                                           'stream': stream, 'owner': 'READER'})
02886         reader.start()
02887 
02888     @staticmethod
02889     def _process_closeable(record):
02890         """The close eligibility of the control handle of a process.
02891 
02892         The control handle of a process is closed **only** when the exit
02893         of that process was really confirmed.  The fact comes from the
02894         **record** of the process and it is not a name of a state: a
02895         process whose exit was not confirmed may still be alive and its
02896         control handle is the only handle that may stop it.
02897 
02898         The eligibility is a **callable** over the original record and
02899         never a frozen copy of a fact: the pending pass asks it at the
02900         moment of its close and it reads the **current** exit fact of
02901         the record.  A pending entry that froze the answer at the
02902         moment of the creation of the process would never close the
02903         handle of a process whose exit was confirmed later.
02904 
02905         The same eligibility is asked by the release of the records and
02906         by the pending pass of the round: the pending pass may not
02907         close behind the back of the owner of the handle.
02908         """
02909         def eligible():
02910             return bool(record.get('exited'))
02911         return eligible
02912 
02913     @staticmethod
02914     def _process_exit_confirmed(record):
02915         """Whether the exit of the process of a record was confirmed.
02916 
02917         The fact is read from the **original** record and it is asked at
02918         the moment of the decision: a record whose exit was confirmed
02919         after the creation of the process is a record whose control
02920         handle may be closed.
02921         """
02922         return bool(record.get('exited'))
02923 
02924     def _process_eligible(self, record):
02925         """Whether the control handle of the process of a record may be closed.
02926 
02927         The **very same** callable that the pending table of the round
02928         holds is asked: the record and the pending table never disagree
02929         about the current exit fact of the process.  A record without
02930         an eligibility of its own is asked directly: the fact comes
02931         from the record and it is never a name of a state.
02932         """
02933         eligibility = self._process_eligibility.get(id(record))
02934         if eligibility is None:
02935             return self._process_exit_confirmed(record)
02936         return bool(eligibility())
02937 
02938     def _close_handle(self, kind, handle):
02939         """Close one owned handle and name a failure of the close.
02940 
02941         A handle that was closed leaves the pending table of the round
02942         and it leaves the retained resources of the round: a handle that
02943         is really closed is never named as a resource any more.  A
02944         handle that could not be closed stays pending: it is a resource
02945         that this round still owns and it is named.
02946 
02947         A **confirmed** close is notified through the **one** notification
02948         of the round: the job of the round, the records of the round, the
02949         reader that owns the handle and the retained resources of the
02950         round learn the very same fact, and every owner of a handle that
02951         was really closed knows that it must never close it again.
02952 
02953         A **cancellation** in the middle of a close is never turned into
02954         an ordinary evidence: it is named and it is raised, so that the
02955         finalizer that follows it still runs and the cancellation
02956         continues to propagate after the cleanup.
02957         """
02958         entry = self._pending.find(handle)
02959         try:
02960             self.win32.close_handle(handle)
02961         except BrokerError as error:
02962             self.failures.append({'stage': 'close_handle', 'kind': kind,
02963                                   'code': error.code})
02964             return False
02965         except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as error:
02966             self.failures.append({'stage': 'close_handle', 'kind': kind,
02967                                   'error_type': type(error).__name__,
02968                                   'cancellation': True})
02969             raise
02970         except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
02971             self.failures.append({'stage': 'close_handle', 'kind': kind,
02972                                   'error_type': type(error).__name__})
02973             return False
02974         self._pending.take(handle)
02975         # The handle is really closed: every owner of it is notified
02976         # through the one notification of the round.
02977         self._note_closed_handle(entry if entry is not None
02978                                  else {'handle': handle, 'kind': kind})
02979         return True
02980 
02981     def _close_readers(self):
02982         """Stop the readers of the children and close their read handles.
02983 
02984         The wait of a reader is bounded by the ONE cleanup deadline of the
02985         round and its state is recorded.  A reader that is still blocked
02986         when the wait is over is a *fact* of this round and it is
02987         reported as such: a daemon thread that is still running is not a
02988         finished thread, and the read end of such a reader is not closed
02989         under it.
02990 
02991         Every reader is independent of the others: a reader that could
02992         not be joined never skips the coordination of the readers that
02993         follow it, and a reader whose thread never started is never
02994         joined at all.  A read end that was closed with a confirmed
02995         close is never closed a second time.
02996         """
02997         for reader in self.readers:
02998             try:
02999                 self._close_one_reader(reader)
03000             except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as error:
03001                 # A cancellation never skips the readers that follow it:
03002                 # it is named and it is raised after every reader was
03003                 # coordinated.
03004                 self.failures.append({'stage': 'close_readers',
03005                                       'name': reader.name,
03006                                       'error_type': type(error).__name__,
03007                                       'cancellation': True})
03008                 self._readers_cancelled.append(error)
03009             except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
03010                 self.failures.append({'stage': 'close_readers',
03011                                       'name': reader.name,
03012                                       'error_type': type(error).__name__})
03013         if self._readers_cancelled:
03014             raise self._readers_cancelled[0]
03015 
03016     def _close_one_reader(self, reader):
03017         """Coordinate one reader of the round on its own.
03018 
03019         The read end of a reader is closed **only** when the reader
03020         really stopped.  Every branch of the coordination asks the
03021         reader whether it is still alive: a wait that was cancelled and
03022         a join that failed are not a proof that the reader ended, and
03023         the read end of a live reader is never closed under it.
03024 
03025         A cancellation in the middle of the coordination never skips the
03026         readers that follow it and it never skips the protection of the
03027         read end of the reader that is still alive.
03028         """
03029         if reader.handle is None:
03030             # The read end of this reader was closed with a confirmed
03031             # close already.
03032             return
03033         state = {'name': reader.name,
03034                  'finished': reader.finished.is_set(),
03035                  'joined': False,
03036                  'alive': reader.is_alive(),
03037                  'error': reader.error}
03038         if reader.ident is None and not reader.is_alive():
03039             # The thread of this reader never started.  A thread that
03040             # never started is never joined: the join of a thread that
03041             # was not started is an error of the caller and not a fact
03042             # of the round.  The handle of this reader is closed here,
03043             # because no thread of the round reads it.
03044             state['started'] = False
03045             state['alive'] = False
03046             state['finished'] = True
03047             self.reader_states.append(state)
03048             if self._close_handle('stream', reader.handle):
03049                 reader.handle = None
03050             else:
03051                 self._retain_resource('pipe_read', reader.handle,
03052                                       'BROKER_HANDLE_CLOSE_FAILED')
03053             return
03054         state['started'] = True
03055         cancelled = None
03056         try:
03057             remaining = self.remaining_cleanup_seconds()
03058             wait = min(READER_JOIN_SECONDS, remaining)
03059             if wait > 0:
03060                 reader.finished.wait(wait)
03061             # The remaining budget is recomputed before the join: a wait
03062             # that already consumed part of the budget never receives the
03063             # whole budget again.
03064             remaining = self.remaining_cleanup_seconds()
03065             reader.join(min(READER_JOIN_SECONDS, remaining))
03066         except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as error:
03067             # A cancellation never decides the fate of the read end: the
03068             # reader is asked again whether it is still alive.
03069             cancelled = error
03070         except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
03071             self.failures.append({'stage': 'close_reader',
03072                                   'name': reader.name,
03073                                   'error_type': type(error).__name__})
03074         alive = reader.is_alive()
03075         state['finished'] = reader.finished.is_set()
03076         state['joined'] = not alive
03077         state['alive'] = alive
03078         self.reader_states.append(state)
03079         if alive:
03080             # The reader still holds the read end.  The handle is not
03081             # closed under a running reader: it leaves the pending
03082             # table with its owner and the round keeps the fact.
03083             self._pending.take(reader.handle)
03084             self.reader_states[-1]['handle_retained'] = True
03085             self.failures.append({'stage': 'reader_not_stopped',
03086                                   'name': reader.name,
03087                                   'code': 'BROKER_READER_NOT_STOPPED'})
03088             if cancelled is not None:
03089                 raise cancelled
03090             return
03091         if self._close_handle('stream', reader.handle):
03092             reader.handle = None
03093         else:
03094             self._retain_resource('pipe_read', reader.handle,
03095                                   'BROKER_HANDLE_CLOSE_FAILED')
03096         if cancelled is not None:
03097             raise cancelled
03098 
03099     def _terminate_owned(self, handle, record, reason):
03100         """Stop one owned process through its own original handle.
03101 
03102         No pid is used and no name is used: only the handle that this core
03103         created is passed to the operating system.  The wait is bounded
03104         by the ONE cleanup deadline of the round: an expired deadline
03105         never starts a new wait.
03106 
03107         The termination and the **confirmation of the exit** are two
03108         independent facts.  A termination that failed is named and it
03109         is preserved, and the **bounded wait** of the exit still runs
03110         when the original deadline of the round still permits it: a
03111         process whose termination failed may have exited anyway, and
03112         the exit is the only fact that may release its control handle.
03113         A failure of the termination never skips the wait and it never
03114         replaces it.
03115 
03116         The control handle of a process is never closed because the
03117         termination was **requested**: only a confirmed exit may close
03118         it, and an expired cleanup keeps the control and it never opens
03119         a new budget of its own.
03120         """
03121         terminated = False
03122         try:
03123             self.win32.terminate_process(handle, 1)
03124             record['terminated'] = True
03125             terminated = True
03126         except BrokerError as error:
03127             # The original failure of the round is named and it stays
03128             # named: the wait of the exit below is independent of it.
03129             self.failures.append({'stage': 'terminate', 'reason': reason,
03130                                   'code': error.code})
03131         except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit):
03132             # A cancellation is never swallowed: it leaves the method
03133             # and the finalizers that follow it still run.
03134             raise
03135         except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
03136             self.failures.append({'stage': 'terminate', 'reason': reason,
03137                                   'error_type': type(error).__name__})
03138         timeout = self._wait_timeout_ms()
03139         if timeout <= 0:
03140             self.failures.append({'stage': 'wait', 'reason': reason,
03141                                   'code': 'BROKER_CLEANUP_DEADLINE_EXCEEDED'})
03142             return terminated
03143         try:
03144             record['exited'] = bool(self.win32.wait_process(handle, timeout))
03145         except BrokerError as error:
03146             self.failures.append({'stage': 'wait', 'reason': reason, 'code': error.code})
03147         if record['exited']:
03148             try:
03149                 record['exit_code'] = self.win32.exit_code(handle)
03150             except BrokerError as error:
03151                 self.failures.append({'stage': 'exit_code', 'reason': reason,
03152                                       'code': error.code})
03153         return terminated
03154 
03155     # -- the stop of the round ---------------------------------------------
03156     def stop(self, reason='STOP', *, force_unknown=False, deadline=None):
03157         """Stop the round and release every handle that this core holds.
03158 
03159         The stop is bounded and it is idempotent: a repeated stop does not
03160         terminate anything twice and it does not change the evidence of
03161         the round.  Every handle is reported on its own, so a handle that
03162         could not be closed stays visible and the round becomes unknown
03163         instead of a silent success.
03164 
03165         The steps of the stop are **independent bounded finalizers**.  The
03166         termination of the job, the wait for the original processes, the
03167         stop of the readers, the query of the job, the release of the
03168         original handles, the release of the job, the release of the
03169         pending resources and the diagnosis of the round each run in
03170         their own guard: a failure of one of them never skips the others.
03171         The first failure of the round is the one that leaves the method
03172         and the later failures are named next to it.
03173 
03174         A stop that did not release everything does **not** latch the
03175         round as released: the same owner may try the remaining work
03176         again, inside the ONE cleanup deadline of the round.  A handle
03177         that was closed with a confirmed close is never closed a second
03178         time.
03179         """
03180         if self._released:
03181             return self.evidence()
03182         if self.state == 'STOPPING' and self._cleanup_deadline_value is not None:
03183             # A stop that is already running: the owner of the round does
03184             # not start a second one.
03185             return self.evidence()
03186         self.state = 'STOPPING'
03187         if self.stop_reason is None:
03188             self.stop_reason = reason
03189         # The ONE cleanup deadline of the round.  It is generated here,
03190         # at the first stop, and every step below uses this very value.
03191         deadline = self.cleanup_deadline(deadline)
03192         primary = None
03193 
03194         def step(stage, action):
03195             """Run one bounded finalizer of the stop.
03196 
03197             A finalizer that fails never skips the finalizers that follow
03198             it.  A cancellation is named as a cancellation and it is
03199             never turned into an ordinary evidence: it becomes the
03200             primary failure of the round when nothing else failed, and
03201             it is raised after every finalizer was executed.
03202             """
03203             nonlocal primary
03204             try:
03205                 action()
03206             except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the cleanup still runs
03207                 cancelled = isinstance(error, (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit))
03208                 if primary is None:
03209                     primary = error
03210                 self.failures.append({'stage': stage,
03211                                       'error_type': type(error).__name__,
03212                                       'cancellation': cancelled})
03213 
03214         step('diagnostic', self._collect_stream_counts)
03215         step('terminate_job', self._terminate_the_job)
03216         step('wait_processes', self._wait_the_processes)
03217         step('close_readers', self._close_readers)
03218         step('query_job', self._query_job)
03219         step('release_records', self._release_the_records)
03220         step('release_job', self._release_the_job)
03221         step('pending_resources', self._close_pending)
03222 
03223         released = self._is_released()
03224         # The latch of a completed release is only set when the release
03225         # was really proven.  An unfinished cleanup never blocks the
03226         # recovery of the same owner.
03227         self._released = released
03228         self.state = 'UNKNOWN_RETAINED' if (force_unknown or not released) else 'RELEASED'
03229         if primary is not None:
03230             # The first failure of the round is not swallowed: the caller
03231             # sees it after the round was cleaned up.
03232             raise primary
03233         return self.evidence()
03234 
03235     def _pending_handle_in_use(self, item):
03236         """Whether the owner of a pending handle is still using it.
03237 
03238         The owner of a handle is an **object** and not a name: the
03239         reader that owns a read end is asked whether it is still alive.
03240         A read end that a live reader still reads from is never closed
03241         under it, whatever the branch that reached the pending pass did
03242         before.
03243         """
03244         owner = item.get('owner_ref')
03245         if owner is None:
03246             return False
03247         alive = getattr(owner, 'is_alive', None)
03248         if not callable(alive):
03249             return False
03250         try:
03251             return bool(alive())
03252         except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
03253             # A reader that cannot answer is a reader that may still be
03254             # alive: the handle is protected.
03255             return True
03256 
03257     def _close_pending(self):
03258         """Close the handles that are still pending and name the failures.
03259 
03260         The pending pass is the **last** close of the round and it may
03261         close a handle that the release of the records or the release of
03262         the job could not close.  Every handle that it really closes is
03263         reported to the core: the job of the round, the records of the
03264         round and the retained resources of the round never name a
03265         handle that is closed.
03266 
03267         The pass never closes a handle whose owner is still using it:
03268         the guard is the fact of the owner and not the name of an
03269         owner.
03270         """
03271         retained = self._pending.close_all(self.failures,
03272                                            on_closed=self._note_closed_handle,
03273                                            protect=self._pending_handle_in_use)
03274         if retained:
03275             self.failures.append({'stage': 'pending_resources',
03276                                   'count': len(retained),
03277                                   'code': 'BROKER_PENDING_RESOURCES_RETAINED'})
03278 
03279     def _note_closed_handle(self, item):
03280         """Learn that one handle was really closed.
03281 
03282         The bookkeeping of the core has to agree with the fact of the
03283         close: a handle that is closed may never be named by the job of
03284         the round, by the records of the round or by the retained
03285         resources of the round.  Only the handle that was really closed
03286         is forgotten: a handle that could not be closed keeps its owner
03287         and it stays named.
03288 
03289         The **owner object** of the handle is notified as well.  A
03290         reader whose read end was closed by the pending pass learns that
03291         the handle is gone: a later stop never closes the same original
03292         handle a second time and it never closes a handle that the
03293         operating system may have reused in the meantime.
03294         """
03295         handle = item['handle']
03296         kind = item.get('kind')
03297         if self.job is handle:
03298             self.job = None
03299             self.job_closed = True
03300         for record in self.records:
03301             if record.get('process_handle') is handle:
03302                 record['process_handle_closed'] = True
03303                 record['process_handle'] = None
03304             if record.get('thread_handle') is handle:
03305                 record['thread_handle_closed'] = True
03306                 record['thread_handle'] = None
03307         owner = item.get('owner_ref')
03308         if owner is not None and getattr(owner, 'handle', None) is handle:
03309             # The owner of the handle learns that it is closed.  The
03310             # handle of the owner is the **original** handle and it is
03311             # never closed twice.
03312             try:
03313                 owner.handle = None
03314                 owner.closed = True
03315             except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
03316                 pass
03317         self._release_resource(kind, handle)
03318 
03319     def _terminate_the_job(self):
03320         """Terminate the job of the round.
03321 
03322         The termination happens once.  A repeated stop does not terminate
03323         the job of the round a second time.
03324         """
03325         if self.job is None or self.job_terminated:
03326             return
03327         try:
03328             self.win32.terminate_job(self.job, 1)
03329             self.job_terminated = True
03330         except BrokerError as error:
03331             self.failures.append({'stage': 'terminate_job', 'code': error.code})
03332 
03333     def _wait_the_processes(self):
03334         """Wait for the original processes of the round.
03335 
03336         Every process is independent of the others: a process that could
03337         not be waited for does not skip the wait of the next one, and an
03338         ordinary failure of the wait of one process does not interrupt
03339         the group.  The wait is bounded by the ONE cleanup deadline of
03340         the round and the remaining budget is recomputed before every
03341         single wait: an expired deadline never starts a new wait.
03342         """
03343         for record in self.records:
03344             try:
03345                 self._wait_one_process(record)
03346             except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as error:
03347                 # A cancellation never skips the processes that follow it:
03348                 # it is named and it is raised after every process was
03349                 # coordinated.
03350                 self.failures.append({'stage': 'wait', 'role': record['role'],
03351                                       'error_type': type(error).__name__,
03352                                       'cancellation': True})
03353                 self._processes_cancelled.append(error)
03354             except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
03355                 self.failures.append({'stage': 'wait', 'role': record['role'],
03356                                       'error_type': type(error).__name__})
03357         if self._processes_cancelled:
03358             raise self._processes_cancelled[0]
03359 
03360     def _wait_one_process(self, record):
03361         """Wait for one original process of the round, on its own."""
03362         handle = record.get('process_handle')
03363         if handle is None or record['exited']:
03364             return
03365         if not record['resumed']:
03366             # The process never ran: it is stopped through its own
03367             # suspended handle.
03368             self._terminate_owned(handle, record, 'SUSPENDED_AT_STOP')
03369             return
03370         timeout = self._wait_timeout_ms()
03371         if timeout <= 0:
03372             self.failures.append({'stage': 'wait', 'role': record['role'],
03373                                   'code': 'BROKER_CLEANUP_DEADLINE_EXCEEDED'})
03374             return
03375         try:
03376             record['exited'] = bool(self.win32.wait_process(handle, timeout))
03377         except BrokerError as error:
03378             self.failures.append({'stage': 'wait', 'role': record['role'],
03379                                   'code': error.code})
03380         except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit):
03381             # A cancellation is never turned into an ordinary evidence:
03382             # it leaves the wait of this process and it is raised after
03383             # every other process was coordinated.
03384             raise
03385         except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
03386             self.failures.append({'stage': 'wait', 'role': record['role'],
03387                                   'error_type': type(error).__name__})
03388             return
03389         if record['exited']:
03390             try:
03391                 record['exit_code'] = self.win32.exit_code(handle)
03392             except BrokerError as error:
03393                 self.failures.append({'stage': 'exit_code', 'role': record['role'],
03394                                       'code': error.code})
03395             except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit):
03396                 # The cancellation of the exit code is a cancellation of
03397                 # the round and it is never swallowed by the evidence.
03398                 raise
03399             except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
03400                 self.failures.append({'stage': 'exit_code', 'role': record['role'],
03401                                       'error_type': type(error).__name__})
03402 
03403     def _wait_timeout_ms(self):
03404         """The milliseconds that are left until the cleanup deadline.
03405 
03406         The milliseconds are **floored** and they are never rounded
03407         **up**: a remainder that is smaller than the representable unit
03408         is zero and the wait asks for nothing.  A wait that asks for
03409         more than the remaining of the round outlives the round and it
03410         may never be used to infer that the process ended.
03411         """
03412         remaining = self.remaining_cleanup_seconds()
03413         if remaining <= 0:
03414             return 0
03415         return min(WAIT_MILLISECONDS, int(remaining * 1000))
03416 
03417     def _release_the_records(self):
03418         """Close the original handles of the processes of the round.
03419 
03420         A handle that was closed with a confirmed close is forgotten and
03421         it is never closed a second time.  A handle that could not be
03422         closed keeps its original reference and its owner.
03423 
03424         The **control handle of a process** is closed **only** when the
03425         exit of that process was really confirmed.  A process whose exit
03426         was not confirmed - a suspended child that was never assigned to
03427         the job of the round, a process whose wait timed out or whose
03428         wait failed - may still be alive, and its control handle is the
03429         only handle that may stop it.  The handle therefore stays with
03430         the record of the process and with the pending table of the
03431         round, and the same owner may confirm the exit and close it
03432         later, inside the same cleanup deadline of the round.
03433 
03434         The thread handle and the pipe handles are independent of the
03435         exit of the process: they are closed on their own and they never
03436         wait for the process.
03437 
03438         Every handle of every record is closed on its own: a failure of
03439         one of them never skips the handles that follow it, and a
03440         cancellation is raised after every handle was coordinated.
03441         """
03442         cancellations = []
03443         for record in self.records:
03444             for key, kind in (('thread_handle', 'thread'),
03445                               ('process_handle', 'process')):
03446                 handle = record.get(key)
03447                 if handle is None:
03448                     continue
03449                 if kind == 'process' and not self._process_eligible(record):
03450                     # The exit of the process was not confirmed: the
03451                     # control handle stays with its owner and the round
03452                     # keeps the fact.  The handle is never closed under
03453                     # a process that nobody may stop any more.
03454                     record[kind + '_handle_closed'] = False
03455                     self._retain_resource(
03456                         kind, handle, 'BROKER_PROCESS_EXIT_NOT_CONFIRMED')
03457                     self.failures.append({'stage': 'close_handle',
03458                                           'kind': kind,
03459                                           'role': record.get('role'),
03460                                           'code': 'BROKER_PROCESS_EXIT_NOT_CONFIRMED'})
03461                     continue
03462                 try:
03463                     closed = self._close_handle(kind, handle)
03464                 except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as error:
03465                     # A cancellation never skips the handles that follow
03466                     # it: it is named and it is raised after every
03467                     # handle was coordinated.
03468                     record[kind + '_handle_closed'] = False
03469                     self.failures.append({'stage': 'close_handle', 'kind': kind,
03470                                           'role': record.get('role'),
03471                                           'error_type': type(error).__name__,
03472                                           'cancellation': True})
03473                     self._retain_resource(kind, handle, 'BROKER_HANDLE_CLOSE_FAILED')
03474                     cancellations.append(error)
03475                     continue
03476                 record[kind + '_handle_closed'] = closed
03477                 if closed:
03478                     record[key] = None
03479                 else:
03480                     self._retain_resource(kind, handle, 'BROKER_HANDLE_CLOSE_FAILED')
03481         if cancellations:
03482             raise cancellations[0]
03483 
03484     def _release_the_job(self):
03485         """Close the original handle of the job of the round.
03486 
03487         A job handle that could not be closed is **not** forgotten: the
03488         core keeps the original reference and the round stays unknown.
03489         """
03490         if self.job is None:
03491             return
03492         handle = self.job
03493         closed = self._close_handle('job', handle)
03494         self.job_closed = closed
03495         if closed:
03496             self.job = None
03497             self._release_resource('job', handle)
03498         else:
03499             self._retain_resource('job', handle, 'BROKER_HANDLE_CLOSE_FAILED')
03500 
03501     def _is_released(self):
03502         """Whether the round is a proven release.
03503 
03504         A release needs every fact and not only the absence of a failure:
03505         every owned process has to have exited, every handle has to have
03506         been closed, every reader has to have been joined without a read
03507         error and the job has to have been queried and found empty.
03508         """
03509         if self.failures:
03510             return False
03511         if not all(record['exited'] for record in self.records if record['created']):
03512             return False
03513         if not all(record['process_handle_closed'] and record['thread_handle_closed']
03514                    for record in self.records):
03515             return False
03516         if not self.job_closed:
03517             return False
03518         if not (self.job_active_known and self.job_active == 0):
03519             return False
03520         if not self.reader_states:
03521             return False
03522         for state in self.reader_states:
03523             if not state['finished'] or state['alive'] or state['error']:
03524                 return False
03525         if self._pending.pending():
03526             return False
03527         return True
03528 
03529     def _query_job(self):
03530         """Ask the job how many processes it still holds.
03531 
03532         The answer is bounded.  An unknown answer and an answer that is
03533         over the cap are both *unknown*: neither of them is a zero.
03534 
03535         The query only runs while the job handle is still open.  A
03536         handle that was already closed keeps the answer that it produced:
03537         a second stop never destroys the proof of the first one.
03538         """
03539         if self.job is None:
03540             return
03541         try:
03542             identifiers = self.win32.job_process_ids(self.job)
03543         except BrokerError as error:
03544             self.failures.append({'stage': 'query_job', 'code': error.code})
03545             self.job_active_known = False
03546             self.job_active = None
03547             return
03548         if len(identifiers) > MAX_DESCENDANTS:
03549             self.failures.append({'stage': 'query_job', 'code': 'BROKER_DESCENDANT_LIMIT'})
03550             self.job_active_known = False
03551             self.job_active = None
03552             return
03553         self.job_active_known = True
03554         self.job_active = len(identifiers)
03555         self.job_process_ids = list(identifiers)
03556 
03557     def _collect_stream_counts(self):
03558         total = 0
03559         for record in self.records:
03560             for stream, key in (('stdout', 'stdout_bytes'), ('stderr', 'stderr_bytes')):
03561                 for reader in self.readers:
03562                     if reader.name.endswith(record['role'] + '-' + stream):
03563                         record[key] = reader.observed
03564                         record[stream + '_overflow'] = reader.overflow
03565                         if reader.overflow and not self._stream_failure_reported:
03566                             self._stream_failure_reported = True
03567                             self.failures.append({'stage': 'stream_limit',
03568                                                   'role': record['role'],
03569                                                   'stream': stream,
