03570                                                   'code': 'BROKER_STREAM_LIMIT_EXCEEDED'})
03571         for reader in self.readers:
03572             total += reader.kept
03573         self.new_text_bytes = total
03574         if total > MAX_NEW_TEXT_BYTES and not self._text_failure_reported:
03575             self._text_failure_reported = True
03576             self.failures.append({'stage': 'new_text_limit',
03577                                   'code': 'BROKER_NEW_TEXT_LIMIT_EXCEEDED'})
03578 
03579     def overflowed(self):
03580         """Whether a bound of the text of the children was exceeded."""
03581         self._collect_stream_counts()
03582         if self.new_text_bytes > MAX_NEW_TEXT_BYTES:
03583             return True
03584         return any(record['stdout_overflow'] or record['stderr_overflow']
03585                    for record in self.records)
03586 
03587     def _abort_round(self, reason):
03588         """Fail the round closed.
03589 
03590         The stop of the round is bounded and it only touches the handles
03591         and the job of this round.  The state of the round is unknown
03592         afterwards, whatever the stop itself proved.  The abort is
03593         idempotent: a failure that already aborted the round does not
03594         abort it a second time and it does not report it twice.
03595         """
03596         if not self._abort_reported:
03597             self._abort_reported = True
03598             self.failures.append({'stage': 'round', 'code': reason})
03599         try:
03600             self.stop(reason, force_unknown=True)
03601         except BrokerError as error:
03602             self.failures.append({'stage': 'abort', 'code': error.code})
03603         except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
03604             self.failures.append({'stage': 'abort', 'error_type': type(error).__name__})
03605         self.state = 'UNKNOWN_RETAINED'
03606 
03607     # -- the evidence ------------------------------------------------------
03608     def summary(self):
03609         """The bounded summary of the round that fits into one frame."""
03610         return {'state': self.state,
03611                 'roles': [{'role': record['role'], 'pid': record['pid'],
03612                            'created': record['created'], 'assigned': record['assigned'],
03613                            'resumed': record['resumed'], 'exited': record['exited']}
03614                           for record in self.records],
03615                 'job_active_known': self.job_active_known,
03616                 'job_active': self.job_active,
03617                 'failures': len(self.failures)}
03618 
03619     def evidence(self):
03620         return {
03621             'kind': 'lvw.s3-native-broker-evidence/1',
03622             'state': self.state,
03623             'run_id': self.plan.run_id,
03624             'candidate_sha': self.plan.candidate_sha,
03625             'authorization_sha256': self.plan.authorization_sha256,
03626             'grant_authority': 'NOT_VERIFIED',
03627             'stop_reason': self.stop_reason,
03628             'broker': self.broker_identity,
03629             'job': {'created': self.job is not None or self.job_closed,
03630                     'kill_on_job_close': self.job_kill_on_close,
03631                     'breakaway_allowed': False,
03632                     'terminated': self.job_terminated,
03633                     'active_process_count': self.job_active,
03634                     'active_process_count_known': self.job_active_known,
03635                     'process_ids': list(self.job_process_ids),
03636                     'handle_closed': self.job_closed},
03637             'roles': [{key: value for key, value in record.items()
03638                        if key not in ('process_handle', 'thread_handle')}
03639                       for record in self.records],
03640             'new_text_bytes': self.new_text_bytes,
03641             'readers': list(self.reader_states),
03642             'pending_resources': self._pending.pending_facts(),
03643             # The resources that this round could not release and the
03644             # original handles that the Windows façade still holds.  They
03645             # are named with their original references: a resource that
03646             # was not released is never replaced by a copy of a message.
03647             'retained_resources': [{'kind': item['kind'], 'owner': item['owner'],
03648                                     'code': item['code'],
03649                                     'handle_repr': repr(item['handle'])}
03650                                    for item in self._retained_resources],
03651             'api_retained': self._api_retained(),
03652             'cleanup_deadline': self._cleanup_deadline_value,
03653             'failures': list(self.failures),
03654             # The claims that this package does not make.
03655             'native_execution_proven': False,
03656             'native_cleanup_proven': False,
03657             'gpu_released_proven': False,
03658             'port_released_proven': False,
03659             'sampler_proven': False,
03660             'parent_crash_recovery_proven': False,
03661             'windows_stdin_eof_proven': False,
03662             'admission_produced': False,
03663             'stage_pass_claim': False,
03664             'product_gate0': False,
03665         }
03666 
03667 
03668 # -- the bounded private pipe ----------------------------------------------
03669 
03670 def _require_deadline(deadline, code='BROKER_DEADLINE_INVALID'):
03671     """Check one absolute deadline.
03672 
03673     A deadline is a finite number of seconds of the monotonic clock of
03674     the host.  A boolean, a string, an infinite value and a value that
03675     is not a number at all are refused: a deadline that was understood
03676     as something else would silently unbound the round.
03677     """
03678     if deadline is None:
03679         return None
03680     if isinstance(deadline, bool) or not isinstance(deadline, (int, float)):
03681         raise BrokerError(code)
03682     value = float(deadline)
03683     if not math.isfinite(value):
03684         raise BrokerError(code)
03685     return value
03686 
03687 
03688 class _PosixSyscalls:
03689     """The POSIX system calls of the private pipe.
03690 
03691     The table is a narrow and explicit interface.  It is the only place
03692     where the private channel touches the operating system, and it can
03693     be replaced by a synthetic table in the offline validation: the
03694     production logic of the channel is then driven by a table that
03695     records the calls instead of by the kernel.
03696     """
03697 
03698     def __init__(self):
03699         self._pollers = {}
03700 
03701     def fileno(self, file_object):
03702         return file_object.fileno()
03703 
03704     def set_blocking(self, descriptor, blocking):
03705         os.set_blocking(descriptor, blocking)
03706 
03707     def _poller(self, descriptor):
03708         poller = self._pollers.get(descriptor)
03709         if poller is None:
03710             poller = select.poll()
03711             poller.register(descriptor,
03712                             select.POLLIN | select.POLLOUT
03713                             | select.POLLERR | select.POLLHUP)
03714             self._pollers[descriptor] = poller
03715         return poller
03716 
03717     def poll(self, descriptor, mask, timeout):
03718         """Whether the descriptor is ready for the requested operation."""
03719         ready = self._poller(descriptor).poll(max(0.0, timeout) * 1000)
03720         for number, event in ready:
03721             if event & select.POLLNVAL:
03722                 raise BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_DESCRIPTOR_INVALID')
03723             if mask == 'read' and event & (select.POLLIN | select.POLLHUP
03724                                            | select.POLLERR):
03725                 return True
03726             if mask == 'write' and event & (select.POLLOUT | select.POLLHUP
03727                                             | select.POLLERR):
03728                 return True
03729         return False
03730 
03731     def read(self, descriptor, size):
03732         return os.read(descriptor, size)
03733 
03734     def write(self, descriptor, data):
03735         return os.write(descriptor, data)
03736 
03737     def close(self, file_object):
03738         file_object.close()
03739 
03740 
03741 class _PosixPipeEndpoint:
03742     """One end of a private pipe on a POSIX host.
03743 
03744     The endpoint owns the original descriptor of the pipe and it closes
03745     it itself.  The descriptor is set to non blocking exactly once, by
03746     the owner that created it, and the read and the write poll it in
03747     slices of at most 50 milliseconds.  Every slice checks the cancel
03748     flag and the absolute deadline, so that a peer that never reads can
03749     never hold the supervisor of the round.
03750 
03751     The endpoint never changes the blocking mode of a descriptor that it
03752     does not own and it never closes a descriptor that it does not own.
03753     It never relies on the lock of a buffered object either: the close
03754     of a buffered object may wait for a reader that holds its lock, and
03755     the close of this endpoint is the close of the owner of the
03756     descriptor.
03757     """
03758 
03759     def __init__(self, file_object, *, name, syscalls=None, clock=None):
03760         self._file = file_object
03761         self._name = name
03762         self._syscalls = syscalls or _PosixSyscalls()
03763         self._clock = clock or time.monotonic
03764         self._descriptor = self._syscalls.fileno(file_object)
03765         self._non_blocking = False
03766         self._closed = False
03767         # The absolute deadline that is bound to this endpoint.  It may
03768         # only shorten the deadline that is already bound and it is read
03769         # again on every slice: a deadline that was bound after a read
03770         # started still tightens that read.
03771         self._deadline = None
03772         self.facts = {'name': name, 'owner': 'POSIX_ENDPOINT',
03773                       'descriptor': self._descriptor}
03774 
03775     def bind_deadline(self, deadline):
03776         """Bind an absolute deadline to this endpoint.
03777 
03778         The deadline may only shorten the deadline that is already
03779         bound.  It is a property of the endpoint and not a local
03780         argument of one call: a call that passed ``None`` before the
03781         binding still observes the bound deadline.
03782         """
03783         value = _require_deadline(deadline)
03784         if value is None:
03785             return self._deadline
03786         if self._deadline is not None and value > self._deadline:
03787             return self._deadline
03788         self._deadline = value
03789         return value
03790 
03791     def _effective_deadline(self, deadline=None):
03792         """The deadline that this call really uses."""
03793         value = _require_deadline(deadline)
03794         if self._deadline is None:
03795             return value
03796         if value is None:
03797             return self._deadline
03798         return min(value, self._deadline)
03799 
03800     def prepare(self):
03801         """Set the descriptor to non blocking, once, by its owner."""
03802         if self._non_blocking or self._closed:
03803             return
03804         self._syscalls.set_blocking(self._descriptor, False)
03805         self._non_blocking = True
03806         self.facts['non_blocking'] = True
03807 
03808     def _check(self, deadline, cancel):
03809         if cancel is not None and cancel.is_set():
03810             raise BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_CANCELLED', self._name)
03811         if deadline is not None and self._clock() >= deadline:
03812             raise BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_TIMEOUT', self._name)
03813 
03814     def read(self, deadline=None, cancel=None):
03815         """Read one block of the pipe, bounded by the deadline."""
03816         self.prepare()
03817         while True:
03818             self._check(self._effective_deadline(deadline), cancel)
03819             if not self._syscalls.poll(self._descriptor, 'read',
03820                                        POLL_SLICE_SECONDS):
03821                 continue
03822             try:
03823                 data = self._syscalls.read(self._descriptor, PIPE_READ_BYTES)
03824             except BlockingIOError:
03825                 # The pipe was not ready after all: the slice is repeated.
03826                 continue
03827             except OSError as error:
03828                 raise BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_READ_FAILED',
03829                                   '%s:%s' % (self._name, type(error).__name__))
03830             if not data:
03831                 return b''
03832             return data
03833 
03834     def write(self, data, deadline=None, cancel=None):
03835         """Write the whole block, bounded by the deadline."""
03836         self.prepare()
03837         view = memoryview(bytes(data))
03838         total = 0
03839         while total < len(view):
03840             self._check(self._effective_deadline(deadline), cancel)
03841             if not self._syscalls.poll(self._descriptor, 'write',
03842                                        POLL_SLICE_SECONDS):
03843                 continue
03844             try:
03845                 count = self._syscalls.write(self._descriptor, view[total:])
03846             except BlockingIOError:
03847                 # The pipe was full after all: the slice is repeated.
03848                 continue
03849             except OSError as error:
03850                 raise BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_WRITE_FAILED',
03851                                   '%s:%s' % (self._name, type(error).__name__))
03852             if count <= 0:
03853                 raise BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_WRITE_FAILED', self._name)
03854             total += count
03855         return total
03856 
03857     def close(self):
03858         """Close the original descriptor of this endpoint, exactly once.
03859 
03860         The endpoint is closed **only** when the close really happened.
03861         A close that failed keeps the descriptor and its owner: the
03862         endpoint may try it again and it is never marked as closed
03863         irreversibly.
03864 
03865         A close of an endpoint that is already closed keeps the fact
03866         that it is closed: the second close is idempotent and it never
03867         turns a proven close into a missing one.
03868         """
03869         if self._closed:
03870             self.facts['closed'] = True
03871             self.facts['already_closed'] = True
03872             self.facts['retained_workers'] = 0
03873             self.facts['worker_thread_handle_released'] = True
03874             return self.facts
03875         try:
03876             self._syscalls.close(self._file)
03877         except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
03878             self.facts['closed'] = False
03879             self.facts['error_type'] = type(error).__name__
03880             self.facts['retained_workers'] = 0
03881             self.facts['worker_thread_handle_released'] = True
03882             return self.facts
03883         self._closed = True
03884         self.facts['closed'] = True
03885         # This host has no worker thread for an end of the pipe: the
03886         # blocking calls are made by the reader of the channel itself.
03887         # There is therefore no original worker handle that could be
03888         # outstanding, and the fact says so.
03889         self.facts['retained_workers'] = 0
03890         self.facts['worker_thread_handle_released'] = True
03891         return self.facts
03892 
03893 
03894 class _WindowsPipeWorker(threading.Thread):
03895     """One worker of one end of a private pipe on a Windows host.
03896 
03897     The worker owns one blocking operation at a time.  It takes the
03898     request from its queue, it performs the blocking call on the
03899     original handle of the pipe and it reports the real completion of
03900     the call.  The thread is created once for the endpoint and it is
03901     never created for a frame.
03902 
03903     The handle of the thread is the handle of the thread itself and
03904     never a self reported identifier: ``CancelSynchronousIo`` needs the
03905     original handle of the thread that performs the operation.
03906 
03907     The lifecycle of the worker is **explicit**.  The states are
03908     ``IDLE``, ``QUEUED``, ``IN_IO``, ``CANCELLING``, ``DONE`` and
03909     ``STOPPED``:
03910 
03911     * ``IDLE``      the worker waits for a request;
03912     * ``QUEUED``    a request waits for the worker;
03913     * ``IN_IO``     the worker is inside the blocking call;
03914     * ``CANCELLING`` a cancellation was asked for;
03915     * ``DONE``      the request of the worker completed;
03916     * ``STOPPED``   the thread of the worker really ended.
03917 
03918     ``DONE`` is not ``STOPPED``: the completion of a request says
03919     nothing about the thread of the worker, and the handle of the
03920     thread may only be closed after the thread really ended.
03921 
03922     The worker is the **only** owner of the handle of its own thread:
03923     it duplicated that handle and it closes it when its thread really
03924     ended.  A worker that did not stop keeps its handle and its owner:
03925     the handle is never closed under a live worker and it is never left
03926     to the garbage collector.
03927     """
03928 
03929     STATES = ('IDLE', 'QUEUED', 'IN_IO', 'CANCELLING', 'DONE', 'STOPPED')
03930 
03931     def __init__(self, api, handle, *, kind, action, name, clock=None,
03932                  context=None):
03933         super().__init__(daemon=True, name=name)
03934         self.api = api
03935         self.handle = handle
03936         self.kind = kind
03937         self.action = action
03938         self._clock = clock or time.monotonic
03939         self._requests = queue.Queue(maxsize=1)
03940         # The event of the in-flight operation.  It is set while the
03941         # worker is inside the call and it is cleared again when the
03942         # call ended: an event that is never cleared would report a
03943         # permanent in-flight state.
03944         self.inside = threading.Event()
03945         self.done = threading.Event()
03946         self.stopped = threading.Event()
03947         self.ready = threading.Event()
03948         self.result = None
03949         self.error = None
03950         # The **original** error object of the last request.  The code
03951         # of the error is a name; the object is the failure.  It is kept
03952         # so that the endpoint may raise the very object that the
03953         # primitive raised instead of a copy of its name.
03954         self._error_object = None
03955         self.thread_handle = None
03956         self.thread_handle_released = False
03957         self.joined = False
03958         self.state = 'IDLE'
03959         # The number of the requests that were submitted and the number
03960         # of the requests that completed.  The two numbers are the real
03961         # completion of a request: the event alone could report the
03962         # completion of an older request.
03963         self._condition = threading.Condition()
03964         self._submitted = 0
03965         self._completed = 0
03966         # The generations of the requests that were handed over, in the
03967         # order in which they were handed over.  The worker takes the
03968         # request and its generation together: a worker that would read
03969         # the number of the submissions would name the generation of a
03970         # later request.
03971         self._generations = collections.deque()
03972         # The **outcome of every generation**.  The outcome carries the
03973         # result, the error, the **original error object**, the source
03974         # of the failure and the order of the completion.  A result
03975         # that was really published is never replaced by a later one and
03976         # a cancellation never rewrites the outcome of a generation.
03977         self._outcomes = {}
03978         # The **admission guard of every generation**: the cancel flag
03979         # and the absolute deadline that the request of the generation
03980         # carries.  The guard is the admission of the worker itself: a
03981         # request whose cancel was published or whose deadline arrived
03982         # **before** the worker entered the operation performs **no**
03983         # primitive call at all.  The guard travels with the request
03984         # and it is never a deadline of the worker.
03985         self._guards = {}
03986         # The explicit notification that a **failure** of a generation
03987         # was really published.  It is set under the lock of the worker
03988         # and it is set **after** the outcome was recorded: an owner
03989         # that waits for it knows that the failure of the round is
03990         # published and that a later cancellation may not replace it.
03991         self.outcome_published = threading.Event()
03992         # The order of the events of this worker.  Every completion and
03993         # every cancellation takes a number of it, under the lock of the
03994         # worker: the number tells whether a failure was published
03995         # **before** the cancellation of the round or after it.
03996         self._sequence = 0
03997         self._cancel_sequence = None
03998         # The ONE context of the round.  The worker reads the absolute
03999         # deadline and the published cleanup deadline of the round from
04000         # it and it never keeps a budget of its own.
04001         self._context = context
04002         # The flag of the cancellation.  It stops the worker from
04003         # accepting a new task before the cancellation is requested.
04004         self._cancelled = False
04005         # The name of the cancel flag is not ``_stop``: the base class of
04006         # the thread uses that name for its own method and a shadow of it
04007         # would break the join of the thread.
04008         self._stop_event = threading.Event()
04009         # The pause of the retry of a cancellation.  It is its **own**
04010         # event and it is never set: the retry of a cancellation may not
04011         # wait on the stop event of the worker, because the worker set
04012         # that event itself and the wait would return at once.
04013         self._pause = threading.Event()
04014 
04015     # -- the lifecycle ----------------------------------------------------
04016     def run(self):
04017         try:
04018             self.thread_handle = self.api.duplicate_current_thread_handle()
04019         except BrokerError as error:
04020             self.error = error.code
04021             self.done.set()
04022             self.ready.set()
04023             self.state = 'DONE'
04024             self.stopped.set()
04025             return
04026         except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
04027             self.error = type(error).__name__
04028             self.done.set()
04029             self.ready.set()
04030             self.state = 'DONE'
04031             self.stopped.set()
04032             return
04033         self.ready.set()
04034         try:
04035             while not self._stop_event.is_set():
04036                 try:
04037                     request = self._requests.get(timeout=0.1)
04038                 except queue.Empty:
04039                     continue
04040                 with self._condition:
04041                     generation = (self._generations.popleft()
04042                                   if self._generations else self._submitted)
04043                 self.error = None
04044                 self.result = None
04045                 self._error_object = None
04046                 # The check of the cancellation and the publication of
04047                 # the intent to enter the operation are **one** atomic
04048                 # step under the lock of the worker.  A cancellation
04049                 # that arrives between the check and the operation would
04050                 # otherwise be answered by a worker that performs the
04051                 # operation anyway: the cancellation and the operation
04052                 # would be two uncoordinated facts.
04053                 #
04054                 # The **admission guard of the generation** is asked in
04055                 # the same atomic step: the cancel flag and the
04056                 # deadline of the request travel with the request and
04057                 # the worker never enters the operation of a request
04058                 # whose admission was already refused.
04059                 with self._condition:
04060                     cancelled = self._cancelled
04061                     if not cancelled:
04062                         guard = self._guards.get(generation)
04063                         if guard is not None:
04064                             guard_cancel, guard_deadline = guard
04065                             # The **current** original context of the
04066                             # round is consulted at the moment of the
04067                             # decision of the dispatch.  A cancellation
04068                             # or an expiry that was published **before**
04069                             # this decision refuses the admission and
04070                             # the primitive is never called at all: the
04071                             # captured request of the call is never the
04072                             # authority of the decision.
04073                             context = self._context
04074                             if context is not None:
04075                                 if context.cancel.is_set():
04076                                     cancelled = True
04077                                     self.error = 'BROKER_PIPE_CANCELLED'
04078                                     self._error_object = _cancel_source_error(
04079                                         BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_CANCELLED',
04080                                                     self.kind), context.cancel)
04081                                 else:
04082                                     # The effective minimum is the one
04083                                     # of the context: the bound of the
04084                                     # call, the **current** absolute
04085                                     # deadline of the round and the
04086                                     # **published** cleanup deadline.
04087                                     current = self._effective_deadline(
04088                                         guard_deadline)
04089                                     if (current is not None
04090                                             and self._clock() >= current):
04091                                         cancelled = True
04092                                         self.error = 'BROKER_PIPE_TIMEOUT'
04093                                         self._error_object = BrokerError(
04094                                             'BROKER_PIPE_TIMEOUT', self.kind)
04095                             if (not cancelled and guard_cancel is not None
04096                                     and guard_cancel.is_set()):
04097                                 cancelled = True
04098                                 self.error = 'BROKER_PIPE_CANCELLED'
04099                                 # The error of the refusal carries the
04100                                 # owner of the cancel flag, exactly like
04101                                 # the refusal of the endpoint: a
04102                                 # cancellation that the close of the
04103                                 # channel initiated is a named fact of
04104                                 # that close and it is never the error
04105                                 # of the reader.
04106                                 self._error_object = _cancel_source_error(
04107                                     BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_CANCELLED',
04108                                                 self.kind), guard_cancel)
04109                             elif (not cancelled and guard_deadline is not None
04110                                   and self._clock() >= guard_deadline):
04111                                 cancelled = True
04112                                 self.error = 'BROKER_PIPE_TIMEOUT'
04113                                 self._error_object = BrokerError(
04114                                     'BROKER_PIPE_TIMEOUT', self.kind)
04115                     if not cancelled:
04116                         self.state = 'IN_IO'
04117                         self.inside.set()
04118                 if cancelled:
04119                     # The cancellation reached the worker before the
04120                     # operation started: the operation is not performed
04121                     # at all and the request is answered as finished.
04122                     self._complete(generation)
04123                     continue
04124                 try:
04125                     self.result = self.action(request)
04126                 except BrokerError as error:
04127                     self.error = error.code
04128                     self._error_object = error
04129                 except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
04130                     self.error = type(error).__name__
04131                     self._error_object = error
04132                 finally:
04133                     self.inside.clear()
04134                     self._complete(generation)
04135         finally:
04136             self.state = 'STOPPED'
04137             self.stopped.set()
04138             # The worker is the only owner of the handle of its own
04139             # thread and it closes it here, when its thread really ended.
04140             self.release_thread_handle()
04141 
04142     def _complete(self, generation=None):
04143         """Report the real completion of the current request.
04144 
04145         The outcome of the generation is **recorded** here, under the
04146         lock of the worker: the result, the code of the error, the
04147         **original error object** and the order of the completion.  The
04148         record is the history of the request and it is never replaced
04149         by a later one.
04150 
04151         The order of the completion is the order of the events of the
04152         worker: it tells whether a failure was published **before** the
04153         cancellation of the round or after it.  A failure that was
04154         published before the cancellation is a fault of the round and it
04155         stays the first error; a failure that was published after the
04156         cancellation is the consequence of the cancellation.
04157         """
04158         with self._condition:
04159             self._completed += 1
04160             self._sequence += 1
04161             if generation is not None:
04162                 record = {
04163                     'generation': generation,
04164                     'result': self.result,
04165                     'error': self.error,
04166                     'error_object': self._error_object,
04167                     'source': 'WORKER',
04168                     'sequence': self._sequence,
04169                     'cancel_sequence': self._cancel_sequence,
04170                 }
04171                 self._outcomes[generation] = record
04172                 if record['error'] is not None:
04173                     # The failure of this generation is published: a
04174                     # later cancellation of the round may not replace
04175                     # it.
04176                     self.outcome_published.set()
04177             self._condition.notify_all()
04178         self.done.set()
04179         self.state = 'DONE'
04180 
04181     def outcome(self, generation):
04182         """The recorded outcome of one generation of this worker.
04183 
04184         The outcome is the **original** outcome of the request: the
04185         result, the error and the error object of the request that
04186         really ran.  It is ``None`` when the request did not complete.
04187         """
04188         with self._condition:
04189             record = self._outcomes.get(generation)
04190             return dict(record) if record is not None else None
04191 
04192     def published_before_cancel(self, generation):
04193         """Whether the outcome of this generation was published first.
04194 
04195         The comparison of the two orders is the only proof that a
04196         failure of the round happened **before** the cancellation of the
04197         round.  A cancellation that was requested first may not rewrite
04198         the failure of a request that was still running, and a failure
04199         that was published first may never be replaced by a
04200         cancellation.
04201 
04202         A worker that was never cancelled proves the order by itself:
04203         the failure was published before any cancellation of it.
04204         """
04205         with self._condition:
04206             record = self._outcomes.get(generation)
04207             if record is None or record.get('error') is None:
04208                 return False
04209             if self._cancel_sequence is None:
04210                 return True
04211             return record['sequence'] < self._cancel_sequence
04212 
04213     def _request_cancellation(self):
04214         """Take the order of the cancellation of this worker.
04215 
04216         The order is taken under the lock of the worker, so that the
04217         comparison with the order of a completion is a real order and
04218         not a guess of the scheduler.
04219         """
04220         with self._condition:
04221             self._cancelled = True
04222             if self._cancel_sequence is None:
04223                 self._sequence += 1
04224                 self._cancel_sequence = self._sequence
04225             self.state = 'CANCELLING'
04226             self._condition.notify_all()
04227         self._stop_event.set()
04228 
04229     def wait_ready(self, seconds):
04230         """Wait for the worker to have acquired the handle of its thread."""
04231         return self.ready.wait(max(0.0, seconds))
04232 
04233     def submit(self, request, cancel=None, deadline=None):
04234         """Hand one request to the worker.
04235 
04236         The hand over is bounded: the queue of the worker holds one
04237         request and a request that does not fit is refused with a named
04238         error.  A worker that is dead and a worker whose thread handle
04239         could not be created refuse the request as well.
04240 
04241         The hand over and the cancellation share **one** lock: a
04242         request that is submitted after the cancellation of the worker
04243         is refused and it never enters the queue.  The cancellation and
04244         the submission are therefore coordinated by the same generation
04245         of the worker and a request of an older generation is never
04246         taken for the completion of this one.
04247 
04248         The **admission guard** of the request - the cancel flag and the
04249         absolute deadline of the call - travels with the request: the
04250         worker asks it before it enters the operation and a request
04251         whose admission was refused performs **no** primitive call.
04252 
04253         The completion of a request is the number of the completed
04254         requests and not the event alone: the completion of an older
04255         request is never the completion of this one.
04256         """
04257         if not self.is_alive():
04258             raise BrokerError('BROKER_WORKER_NOT_ALIVE', self.kind)
04259         if self.thread_handle is None:
04260             raise BrokerError('BROKER_WORKER_NOT_READY',
04261                                '%s:%s' % (self.kind, self.error))
04262         with self._condition:
04263             if self._cancelled:
04264                 # The cancellation of this worker arrived first: the
04265                 # request is refused and it never enters the queue.
04266                 raise BrokerError('BROKER_WORKER_CANCELLED', self.kind)
04267             self._submitted += 1
04268             generation = self._submitted
04269             # The generation travels with the request: the worker takes
04270             # the request and the generation of **that** request.  The
04271             # generation is registered **before** the request is handed
04272             # over: a worker that takes the request never finds an
04273             # empty list of generations.
04274             self._generations.append(generation)
04275             self._guards[generation] = (cancel, deadline)
04276             try:
04277                 self._requests.put(request, timeout=POLL_SLICE_SECONDS)
04278             except queue.Full:
04279                 self._submitted -= 1
04280                 self._guards.pop(generation, None)
04281                 if self._generations and self._generations[-1] == generation:
04282                     self._generations.pop()
04283                 self._condition.notify_all()
04284                 raise BrokerError('BROKER_WORKER_QUEUE_FULL', self.kind)
04285             self.done.clear()
04286             self.state = 'QUEUED'
04287             return generation
04288 
04289     def wait_done(self, generation, timeout):
04290         """Wait for the completion of one request, bounded."""
04291         with self._condition:
04292             return self._condition.wait_for(
04293                 lambda: self._completed >= generation, max(0.0, timeout))
04294 
04295     def request_cancel(self, deadline=None):
04296         """Ask for the cancellation of the operation of this worker.
04297 
04298         The request is not a completion.  The caller has to wait for the
04299         real completion of the worker and to join it.
04300 
04301         The cancellation stops the worker from accepting a new task
04302         **first** and it drives the worker to its end: the worker that
04303         is inside an operation is asked to leave it and the worker that
04304         waits for a request is asked to stop waiting.  A cancellation
04305         that reports ``NOT_FOUND`` is repeated, bounded by the shared
04306         deadline, until the worker reports that **this** generation is
04307         done or that the thread really stopped: a cancellation that was
04308         sent once and dropped is not a cancellation.
04309 
04310         The retry never waits on the stop event of the worker: the
04311         worker set that event itself, a moment before, and a wait that
04312         returns at once would end the retry after a single
04313         ``NOT_FOUND``.  The retry waits on its own pause instead, so
04314         that the request of the operating system is really repeated.
04315 
04316         The completion of an **older** generation never releases the
04317         retry: the real completion of the request of this generation is
04318         the number of the completed requests.
04319 
04320         The deadline of the retry is **recomputed on every round** of
04321         the loop: a deadline that was published after the retry started
04322         still shortens it, and the pause of the retry is cropped to the
04323         time that is really left.  A retry that used the number of the
04324         deadline that it found when it started would wait longer than
04325         the round allows.
04326         """
04327         self._request_cancellation()
04328         if self.thread_handle is None:
04329             return 'NO_HANDLE'
04330         # The bound of the retry is fixed **once**: a retry that used a
04331         # deadline that moved with every round would never expire.
04332         bound = self._effective_deadline(deadline)
04333         if bound is None:
04334             # The retry of a cancellation is **always** bounded: a
04335             # worker that never ends may not hold the caller for ever.
04336             # The bound of the package is used only when neither the
04337             # caller nor the context of the round brought a deadline.
04338             bound = self._clock() + CLOSE_JOIN_SECONDS
04339         while True:
04340             try:
04341                 state = self.api.cancel_synchronous_io(self.thread_handle)
04342             except BrokerError as error:
04343                 self.error = error.code
04344                 return 'FAILED'
04345             except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
04346                 self.error = type(error).__name__
04347                 return 'FAILED'
04348             if state != 'NOT_FOUND':
04349                 return state
04350             # The operation was not found: it had not started yet or it
04351             # had just finished.  The worker is asked again until the
04352             # request of this generation completed or until the thread
04353             # of the worker really stopped.
04354             with self._condition:
04355                 completed = self._completed >= self._submitted
04356             if completed or self.stopped.is_set():
04357                 return 'NOT_FOUND'
04358             # The deadline of this round of the retry is read **again**
04359             # from the call and from the context of the round: a
04360             # deadline that was published after the retry started still
04361             # shortens the retry that is running.
04362             effective = self._effective_deadline(deadline)
04363             if effective is None or bound < effective:
04364                 effective = bound
04365             if self._clock() >= effective:
04366                 return 'NOT_FOUND'
04367             # The pause of the retry is its own event: the stop event of
04368             # the worker was set by this very method and it may never be
04369             # used as the sleep of the retry.  The pause is cropped to
04370             # the time that is really left.
04371             pause = POLL_SLICE_SECONDS
04372             if effective is not None:
04373                 pause = max(0.0, min(pause, effective - self._clock()))
04374             if pause <= 0:
04375                 return 'NOT_FOUND'
04376             self._pause.wait(pause)
04377 
04378     def _effective_deadline(self, call=None):
04379         """The ONE deadline that a wait of this worker really uses.
04380 
04381         The deadline of the call, the absolute deadline of the round and
04382         the **published** cleanup deadline of the round are one value:
04383         the earliest of them.  The value is recomputed on every call.
04384         """
04385         value = _require_deadline(call)
04386         context = self._context
04387         if context is not None:
04388             absolute = context.absolute_deadline
04389             if absolute is not None and (value is None or absolute < value):
04390                 value = absolute
04391             cleanup = context.cleanup_deadline_value()
04392             if cleanup is not None and (value is None or cleanup < value):
04393                 value = cleanup
04394         return value
04395 
04396     def stop(self):
04397         """Ask the worker to end its loop."""
04398         self._stop_event.set()
04399 
04400     def stop_accepting(self):
04401         """Stop the worker from accepting a new task.
04402 
04403         The flag is written under the **same** lock that the worker of
04404         the endpoint reads it under: the cancellation and the intent to
04405         enter the operation are one atomic step.
04406 
04407         The **order** of the cancellation is taken here as well: a
04408         failure that was published before this call is a fault of the
04409         round and a cancellation may never replace it.
04410         """
04411         self._request_cancellation()
04412 
04413     def release_thread_handle(self):
04414         """Close the handle of the thread of this worker.
04415 
04416         The worker is the only owner of that handle.  It is closed here
04417         and only here, and only after the thread of the worker really
04418         ended.  A worker that did not stop keeps its handle.
04419         """
04420         if self.thread_handle is None:
04421             return True
04422         handle = self.thread_handle
04423         try:
04424             self.api.close_handle(handle)
04425         except BrokerError as error:
04426             self.error = error.code
04427             return False
04428         except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
04429             self.error = type(error).__name__
04430             return False
04431         self.thread_handle = None
04432         self.thread_handle_released = True
04433         return True
04434 
04435     def join_bounded(self, seconds):
04436         """Join the worker and report whether it really stopped.
04437 
04438         A worker whose thread was never started cannot be joined: the
04439         join of such a thread is an error of the caller and not a fact
04440         of the round.  The join reports that the worker did not stop
04441         and it never pretends that the thread ended.
04442         """
04443         try:
04444             self.join(max(0.0, seconds))
04445         except RuntimeError:
04446             # The thread was never started: it is not a thread of the
04447             # round that ended, it is a thread that never came up.
04448             self.joined = False
04449             return False
04450         self.joined = not self.is_alive()
04451         return self.joined
04452 
04453 
04454 class _WindowsPipeEndpoint:
04455     """One end of a private pipe on a Windows host.
04456 
04457     The read and the write of the endpoint each own exactly one worker
04458     thread and there is never more than one of them: the number of the
04459     threads of a channel is fixed and it never grows with the number of
04460     the frames.
04461 
04462     The supervisor never performs the blocking call itself.  It waits
04463     for the completion of the worker in bounded slices, it checks the
04464     cancel flag and the deadline on every slice and it cancels the
04465     worker through the original handle of its thread when the deadline
04466     arrives.  The worker that is inside the call is the one that may
04467     block; the supervisor never does.
04468 
04469     The cancellation of a synchronous operation is a request and not a
04470     completion: ``CancelSynchronousIo`` may report that the operation
04471     was not found because it had not started yet or because it had just
04472     finished.  The worker therefore reports the real state of its
04473     operation, the supervisor joins it with a bounded wait, and a worker
04474     that did not stop keeps its owner reference and makes the round
04475     unknown.
04476 
04477     A write is a **complete** write: the endpoint writes the whole
04478     block, in as many partial writes as Windows needs, and it reports
04479     the number of the bytes that really went into the pipe.  A partial
04480     write is never reported as a success.
04481     """
04482 
04483     #: The bound absolute deadline of this endpoint.  It is a class
04484     #: attribute so that an endpoint that was built without its
04485     #: constructor still has a real deadline and never a missing one.
04486     _deadline = None
04487     #: The cancel flag and the deadline of the write that is in flight.
04488     #: They are class attributes for the same reason as the deadline
04489     #: above: an endpoint that was built without its constructor still
04490     #: has them and never a missing one.
04491     _write_cancel = None
04492     _write_deadline = None
04493     #: The context of the round.  It is a class attribute for the same
04494     #: reason: an endpoint that was built without its constructor still
04495     #: reads the absolute deadline of the round from it and it never
04496     #: treats a missing context as an unlimited budget.
04497     _context = None
04498     #: The lock of the **admission** of the endpoint.  It is a class
04499     #: attribute for the same reason: an endpoint that was built without
04500     #: its constructor still has a **real** admission and it never has a
04501     #: permissive one.  The lock is created **per instance** on its
04502     #: first use and it is never shared between two endpoints.
04503     _admission_lock = None
04504 
04505     def __init__(self, file_object, *, name, api=None, clock=None,
04506                  handle=None, context=None):
04507         self._file = file_object
04508         self._name = name
04509         self._api = api
04510         self._clock = clock or time.monotonic
04511         self._closed = False
04512         self._reader = None
04513         self._writer = None
04514         self._retained = []
04515         self._deadline = None
04516         self._write_cancel = None
04517         self._write_deadline = None
04518         # The lock of the **admission** of the endpoint.  The check of
04519         # the cancel flag, of the deadline of the round and the entry of
04520         # the request into the worker are one atomic step under it.
04521         self._admission_lock = threading.RLock()
04522         # The ONE context of the round.  The endpoint reads the absolute
04523         # deadline of the round from it and it never keeps a budget of
04524         # its own.
04525         self._context = context
04526         if handle is None:
04527             import msvcrt
04528             handle = msvcrt.get_osfhandle(file_object.fileno())
04529         self._handle = handle
04530         self.facts = {'name': name, 'owner': 'WINDOWS_ENDPOINT'}
04531 
04532     def bind_deadline(self, deadline):
04533         """Bind an absolute deadline to this endpoint.
04534 
04535         The deadline may only shorten the deadline that is already
04536         bound and it is read again on every slice.
04537         """
04538         value = _require_deadline(deadline)
04539         if value is None:
04540             return self._deadline
04541         if self._deadline is not None and value > self._deadline:
04542             return self._deadline
04543         self._deadline = value
04544         return value
04545 
04546     def bind_context(self, context):
04547         """Bind the ONE context of the round to this endpoint."""
04548         if context is not None:
04549             self._context = context
04550         return self._context
04551 
04552     def _effective_deadline(self, deadline=None):
04553         """The deadline that this call really uses.
04554 
04555         The deadline of the call, the deadline that is bound to the
04556         endpoint, the absolute deadline of the round and the **published**
04557         cleanup deadline of the round are **one** value: the earliest of
04558         the four.  The endpoint never keeps a deadline of its own and it
04559         never treats a missing context as an unlimited budget.
04560 
04561         The published cleanup deadline is part of it: a round that
04562         entered its cleanup publishes one deadline and the endpoint
04563         that reads the context afterwards is bound by it.  An endpoint
04564         that still waited with the long lease of the round would
04565         outlive the only cleanup budget of the round.
04566         """
04567         value = _require_deadline(deadline)
04568         bound = self._deadline
04569         if bound is not None and (value is None or bound < value):
04570             value = bound
04571         context = self._context
04572         if context is not None:
04573             absolute = context.absolute_deadline
04574             if absolute is not None and (value is None or absolute < value):
04575                 value = absolute
04576             cleanup = context.cleanup_deadline_value()
04577             if cleanup is not None and (value is None or cleanup < value):
04578                 value = cleanup
04579         return value
04580 
04581     def _admission_lock_for(self):
04582         """The lock of the admission of **this** endpoint.
04583 
04584         The lock is created **per instance** and it is created once.
04585         An endpoint that was built without its constructor - a fixture
04586         of the offline validation - therefore still has a **real**
04587         admission: the check of the cancel flag and of the deadline is
04588         never skipped and it is never permissive.  The creation of the
04589         lock is guarded by one lock of the module, so that two threads
04590         that arrive at the same time never build two locks for the same
04591         endpoint.
04592         """
04593         lock = self.__dict__.get('_admission_lock')
04594         if lock is not None:
04595             return lock
04596         with _ADMISSION_LOCK_INIT:
04597             lock = self.__dict__.get('_admission_lock')
04598             if lock is None:
04599                 lock = threading.RLock()
04600                 self.__dict__['_admission_lock'] = lock
04601         return lock
04602 
04603     def _admission(self, deadline=None, cancel=None):
04604         """The admission of one generation of this endpoint.
04605 
04606         The admission is asked **before** every side effect: before the
04607         worker of the endpoint is started, before a request is
04608         submitted and before the primitive is called.  A call that
04609         entered when the round was already expired or already cancelled
04610         is refused there: it never starts a worker, it never submits a
04611         request and it never calls the primitive.
04612 
04613         The check and the admission of the generation are **one atomic
04614         step** under the lock of the endpoint: a cancellation that
04615         arrives between the check and the submit may not be answered by
04616         a request that was submitted anyway.
04617         """
04618         with self._admission_lock_for():
04619             return self._admission_locked(deadline, cancel)
04620 
04621     def _admission_locked(self, deadline=None, cancel=None):
04622         """The admission of one generation, under the lock of the endpoint.
04623 
04624         The caller holds the lock of the admission: the check of the
04625         cancel flag, of the deadline of the round and of the context of
04626         the round and the decision of the admission are **one** atomic
04627         step.  The **final** admission of a generation is asked here,
04628         immediately before the submission of the request: a
04629         cancellation or an expiry that was published **before** that
04630         admission causes **zero** primitive calls.
04631         """
04632         effective = self._effective_deadline(deadline)
04633         if cancel is not None and cancel.is_set():
04634             # The cancellation of the round arrived first: the
04635             # request is refused and no side effect happens.
04636             raise _cancel_source_error(
04637                 BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_CANCELLED', self._name), cancel)
04638         context = self._context
04639         if context is not None and context.cancel.is_set():
04640             raise BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_CANCELLED', self._name)
04641         if effective is not None and self._clock() >= effective:
04642             raise BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_TIMEOUT', self._name)
04643         return effective
04644 
04645     def _remaining(self, deadline=None):
04646         """The seconds that are left until the deadline of this endpoint."""
04647         value = self._effective_deadline(deadline)
04648         if value is None:
04649             return CLOSE_JOIN_SECONDS
04650         return max(0.0, value - self._clock())
04651 
04652     def _wait_slice(self, effective=None):
04653         """The seconds that the next wait of this endpoint may ask for.
04654 
04655         The wait is the ordinary poll slice of the package, cropped to
04656         the time that is really left until the **current** effective
04657         deadline of the round.  A wait never asks for more than the
04658         round allows and it never asks for a positive floor beyond it:
04659         an expired round is refused before the wait and a round that is
04660         about to expire waits only for the time that is left.
04661         """
04662         value = POLL_SLICE_SECONDS
04663         if effective is None:
04664             effective = self._effective_deadline()
04665         if effective is not None:
04666             value = max(0.0, min(value, effective - self._clock()))
04667         return value
04668 
04669     def _start(self, kind, action, deadline=None):
04670         """Create and start one worker of this endpoint.
04671 
04672         The worker is registered with the endpoint **before** it is
04673         started.  An endpoint that owns a worker only after the start
04674         returned has no owner at all for a worker whose start did not
04675         return, and the close of the endpoint could close the file
04676         under a worker that is about to read from it.
04677 
04678         The wait for the handle of the thread of the worker is bounded
04679         by the **current** remaining budget of the round.  It never
04680         opens a budget of its own and it never waits a fixed number of
04681         seconds.
04682         """
04683         worker = _WindowsPipeWorker(self._api, self._handle, kind=kind,
04684                                     action=action, clock=self._clock,
04685                                     context=self._context,
04686                                     name='lvw-native-custody-pipe-' + kind)
04687         # The pre-registration: the endpoint is the owner of the worker
04688         # before the thread of the worker exists.
04689         if kind == 'read':
04690             self._reader = worker
04691         else:
04692             self._writer = worker
04693         try:
04694             worker.start()
04695         except BaseException:
04696             # The start of the worker did not return.  The worker keeps
04697             # its owner and it may still come up: it is never forgotten
04698             # and it is never closed under.
04699             self._retained.append(worker)
04700             self.facts['retained_workers'] = len(self._retained)
04701             self.facts['worker_alive'] = True
04702             raise
04703         # The worker acquires the handle of its own thread before it may
04704         # be used.  The wait for it is bounded by the remaining budget of
04705         # the round.
04706         worker.wait_ready(self._remaining(deadline))
04707         return worker
04708 
04709     def _await(self, worker, request, deadline, cancel):
04710         """Wait for the completion of one worker, bounded and cancellable.
04711 
04712         The **final** admission of the generation happens here: it is
04713         asked after the worker is ready and it is asked **immediately
04714         before** the submission of the request, under the lock of the
04715         admission.  That is the **linearization point** of the request:
04716         a cancellation or an expiry that was published **before** it
04717         causes **zero** primitive calls and a cancellation that arrives
04718         **after** it follows the bounded cancellation below.  No claim
04719         is made that an operating system call that already began may be
04720         undone atomically.
04721 
04722         The cancel flag and the deadline of the call travel with the
04723         request: the worker asks them itself before it enters the
04724         operation and a request whose admission was refused never calls
04725         the primitive.
04726 
04727         The deadline that this wait really uses is **recomputed on every
04728         slice**: a deadline that was bound after the wait started still
04729         tightens the wait that is running.  A wait that carries no
04730         deadline yet observes the deadline as soon as it is bound, and
04731         the cancel flag ends it in the meantime.
04732 
04733         The **result of the generation** wins over the cancellation and
04734         over a new timeout.  A fault that the worker really published
04735         before the cancellation of the round is the first error of the
04736         round: the cancellation of a later close may not replace it and
04737         it may not turn it into a benign cancellation.  The order of the
04738         two facts is the order of the events of the worker and it is
04739         never a guess of the scheduler.
04740         """
04741         with self._admission_lock_for():
04742             # The final admission of the generation and the submission
04743             # of the request are **one** atomic step.
04744             effective = self._admission_locked(deadline, cancel)
04745             generation = worker.submit(request, cancel=cancel,
04746                                        deadline=effective)
04747         while True:
04748             effective = self._effective_deadline(deadline)
04749             if cancel is not None and cancel.is_set():
04750                 self._raise_cancellation(worker, generation, effective, cancel)
04751             context = self._context
04752             if context is not None and context.cancel.is_set():
04753                 # The **original** context of the round is noticed here
04754                 # as well: the flag of the call and the flag of the
04755                 # channel are not the only latches of the round.
04756                 self._raise_cancellation(worker, generation, effective,
04757                                         context.cancel)
04758             if effective is not None and self._clock() >= effective:
04759                 # Before a **new** timeout is decided, the published
04760                 # original error of the **same** generation is
04761                 # inspected: it remains the primary error of the round
04762                 # and the timeout is a **parallel** fact.
04763                 outcome = worker.outcome(generation)
04764                 if outcome is not None and outcome.get('error') is not None:
04765                     raise self._parallel_timeout(outcome)
04766                 try:
04767                     self._cancel(worker, effective)
04768                 except BrokerError as error:
04769                     # The coordination failed: the published original
04770                     # error of the generation is inspected **again**
04771                     # before the failure of the coordination is
04772                     # decided, and the failure of the coordination
04773                     # stays a parallel fact.
04774                     outcome = worker.outcome(generation)
04775                     if outcome is not None and outcome.get('error') is not None:
04776                         raise self._parallel_timeout(outcome, error)
04777                     raise
04778                 # Again after the coordination: the outcome of the
04779                 # generation is immutable and it is never rewritten by
04780                 # the timeout.
04781                 outcome = worker.outcome(generation)
04782                 if outcome is not None and outcome.get('error') is not None:
04783                     raise self._parallel_timeout(outcome)
04784                 raise BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_TIMEOUT', self._name)
04785             if worker.wait_done(generation, self._wait_slice(effective)):
04786                 break
04787         outcome = worker.outcome(generation)
04788         if outcome is not None and outcome.get('error') is not None:
04789             raise self._outcome_error(outcome)
04790         if worker.error:
04791             raise BrokerError(worker.error, self._name)
04792         return worker.result
04793 
04794     @staticmethod
04795     def _parallel_timeout(outcome, parallel=None):
04796         """The published failure of a generation with the timeout beside it.
04797 
04798         The **original** error object is the error of the round: a
04799         timeout that arrives after the failure was really published is
04800         a **parallel** fact and it never replaces the failure.
04801         """
04802         error = _WindowsPipeEndpoint._outcome_error(outcome)
04803         try:
04804             error.lvw_parallel_timeout = {
04805                 'code': 'BROKER_PIPE_TIMEOUT',
04806                 'generation': outcome.get('generation'),
04807                 'sequence': outcome.get('sequence'),
04808                 'cancel_sequence': outcome.get('cancel_sequence'),
04809                 'coordination_error': (type(parallel).__name__
04810                                        if parallel is not None else None),
04811             }
04812         except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
04813             pass
04814         return error
04815 
04816     @staticmethod
04817     def _outcome_error(outcome):
04818         """The error of one published outcome of a worker.
04819 
04820         The **original** error object is raised when the worker kept
04821         it: the object is the failure and the code is only its name.  A
04822         worker that kept only a code raises the stable code of the
04823         broker.
04824         """
04825         original = outcome.get('error_object')
04826         if original is not None:
04827             return original
04828         return BrokerError(outcome.get('error'), 'WORKER_OUTCOME')
04829 
04830     def _raise_cancellation(self, worker, generation, effective, cancel):
04831         """Raise the cancellation of this generation of the worker.
04832 
04833         The outcome of the generation is asked **before** the worker is
04834         cancelled and it is asked again after the worker really stopped.
04835         A failure of this generation is the **first error** of the
04836         round: a cancellation that arrives afterwards may never replace
04837         it and it may never turn it into a benign cancellation.  The
04838         cancellation stays a **parallel** fact and it is named next to
04839         the failure.
04840 
04841         A **failed** coordination - a cancellation that could not be
04842         confirmed or a join that did not end - is a parallel fact as
04843         well: it never replaces the published failure of the
04844         generation and it never hides it.
04845 
04846         The error of the cancellation carries the owner of the flag: the
04847         origin of the cancellation is named at the place where the error
04848         is really raised and never by a caller of it.
04849         """
04850         outcome = worker.outcome(generation)
04851         if outcome is not None and outcome.get('error') is not None:
04852             raise self._parallel_fault(outcome, cancel)
04853         coordination_error = None
04854         try:
04855             self._cancel(worker, effective)
04856         except BrokerError as error:
04857             coordination_error = error
04858         outcome = worker.outcome(generation)
04859         if outcome is not None and outcome.get('error') is not None:
04860             raise self._parallel_fault(outcome, cancel, coordination_error)
04861         if coordination_error is not None:
04862             raise coordination_error
04863         raise _cancel_source_error(
04864             BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_CANCELLED', self._name), cancel)
04865 
04866     @staticmethod
04867     def _parallel_fault(outcome, cancel, coordination_error=None):
04868         """The failure of a generation with the cancellation beside it.
04869 
04870         The failure is the error of the round.  The cancellation that
04871         was requested at the same moment is a **parallel** fact: it is
04872         named next to the failure and it never replaces it.  A
04873         coordination that failed is a parallel fact as well.
04874         """
04875         error = _WindowsPipeEndpoint._outcome_error(outcome)
04876         if cancel is not None and cancel.is_set():
04877             source = None
04878             owner = getattr(cancel, 'owner', None)
04879             if callable(owner):
04880                 try:
04881                     source = getattr(owner(), 'owner', None)
04882                 except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
04883                     source = None
04884             try:
04885                 error.lvw_parallel_cancellation = {
04886                     'code': 'BROKER_PIPE_CANCELLED',
04887                     'source': source,
04888                     'generation': outcome.get('generation'),
04889                     'sequence': outcome.get('sequence'),
04890                     'cancel_sequence': outcome.get('cancel_sequence'),
04891                     'coordination_error': (type(coordination_error).__name__
04892                                            if coordination_error is not None
04893                                            else None),
04894                 }
04895             except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
04896                 pass
04897         return error
04898 
04899     def _cancel(self, worker, deadline=None):
04900         """Cancel one worker and prove that it really stopped."""
04901         state = worker.request_cancel(self._effective_deadline(deadline))
04902         if not worker.join_bounded(self._remaining(deadline)):
04903             # The worker did not stop: it keeps its owner reference and
04904             # the round becomes unknown.  It is never terminated blindly
04905             # and it is never waited for without a bound.
04906             self._retained.append(worker)
04907             self.facts['retained_workers'] = len(self._retained)
04908             self.facts['worker_alive'] = True
04909             raise BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_CANCEL_NOT_CONFIRMED',
04910                               '%s:%s' % (self._name, state))
04911         if not worker.joined:
04912             self._retained.append(worker)
04913             self.facts['retained_workers'] = len(self._retained)
04914             raise BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_CANCEL_NOT_CONFIRMED', self._name)
04915 
04916     def read(self, deadline=None, cancel=None):
04917         """Read one block of the pipe, bounded by the deadline.
04918 
04919         The admission of the generation is asked **before** the worker is
04920         started and before the request is submitted: a read that enters
04921         when the round is already expired or already cancelled performs
04922         **no** primitive call at all.
04923         """
04924         effective = self._admission(deadline, cancel)
04925         if self._reader is None:
04926             self._start('read', self._read_block, effective)
04927         return self._await(self._reader, None, deadline, cancel)
04928 
04929     def write(self, data, deadline=None, cancel=None):
04930         """Write the whole block, bounded by the deadline.
04931 
04932         The admission of the generation is asked **before** the worker is
04933         started and before the request is submitted: a write that enters
04934         when the round is already expired or already cancelled performs
04935         **no** primitive call at all.
04936         """
04937         effective = self._admission(deadline, cancel)
04938         if self._writer is None:
04939             self._start('write', self._write_block, effective)
04940         # The cancel flag and the deadline of this write are published
04941         # for the partial write: every segment of a partial write checks
04942         # the cancel flag and the deadline of the round.  They are not
04943         # cleared when the write ends: a segment that is still in flight
04944         # inside the worker has to observe the cancel of this write.
04945         self._write_cancel = cancel
04946         self._write_deadline = effective
04947         return self._await(self._writer, bytes(data), deadline, cancel)
04948 
04949     def _read_block(self, request):
04950         return self._api.read_stream(self._handle, PIPE_READ_BYTES)
04951 
04952     def _current_write_deadline(self):
04953         """The deadline that the next segment of a write really uses.
04954 
04955         The value is read **again** for every segment of a partial
04956         write.  A copy of the deadline that was taken when the write
04957         started would let the write continue past a deadline that the
04958         round published in the meantime, and the shared context of the
04959         round is the only authority of the deadline.
04960 
04961         The **published cleanup deadline** is part of it: a write that
04962         is still running when the round entered its cleanup is bounded by
04963         the cleanup budget of the round and not by the long lease.
04964         """
04965         value = self._write_deadline
04966         bound = self._deadline
04967         if bound is not None and (value is None or bound < value):
04968             value = bound
04969         context = self._context
04970         if context is not None:
04971             absolute = context.absolute_deadline
04972             if absolute is not None and (value is None or absolute < value):
04973                 value = absolute
04974             cleanup = context.cleanup_deadline_value()
04975             if cleanup is not None and (value is None or cleanup < value):
04976                 value = cleanup
04977         return value
04978 
04979     def _write_block(self, request):
04980         """Write the whole block, report the number of the written bytes.
04981 
04982         Windows may write less than the block that it was given.  The
04983         endpoint repeats the write until the whole block was written or
04984         until the write fails with a named error.  **Every segment** of
04985         the partial write checks the cancel flag and the deadline of the
04986         round: a write that is not bounded is not a write.
04987         """
04988         data = bytes(request)
04989         total = 0
04990         while total < len(data):
04991             cancel = self._write_cancel
04992             if cancel is not None and cancel.is_set():
04993                 raise BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_CANCELLED', self._name)
04994             deadline = self._current_write_deadline()
04995             if deadline is not None and self._clock() >= deadline:
04996                 raise BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_TIMEOUT', self._name)
04997             count = self._api.write_stream(self._handle, data[total:])
04998             if count is None or count <= 0:
04999                 raise BrokerError('BROKER_STREAM_WRITE_FAILED',
05000                                   '%s:written=%d' % (self._name, total))
05001             total += count
05002         return total
05003 
05004     def close(self):
05005         """Close the original handle of this endpoint, exactly once.
05006 
05007         The close stops the workers from accepting a new task first, it
05008         asks for the cancellation of the operation that is in flight and
05009         it joins the workers with a bounded wait.  A worker that did not
05010         stop keeps its owner reference: the file is never closed under a
05011         worker that is still inside its operation.
05012 
05013         The close is **committed** only when it really happened.  A
05014         close that failed keeps the file and its owner: the endpoint
05015         may try it again inside the deadline of the round and it is
05016         never marked as closed irreversibly.  A close of an endpoint
05017         that is already closed keeps the fact that it is closed: the
05018         second close is idempotent and it never turns a proven close
05019         into a missing one.
05020 
05021         The state of the workers is **recomputed** on every close: a
05022         worker that really ended and whose handle of its thread was
05023         released is not retained any more, and a worker whose handle of
05024         its thread could not be closed is retained again and it may be
05025         released by a later close.  A closed file never locks the
05026         recovery of the handle of a thread.
05027         """
05028         deadline = self._effective_deadline()
05029         alive = False
05030         retained = []
05031         for worker in (self._reader, self._writer):
05032             if worker is None:
05033                 continue
05034             worker.stop_accepting()
05035             worker.request_cancel(deadline)
05036             if not worker.join_bounded(self._remaining(deadline)):
05037                 # The worker did not stop: it keeps its owner and the
05038                 # file is never closed under it.
05039                 alive = True
05040                 retained.append(worker)
05041                 continue
05042             # The thread of the worker really ended: the original handle
05043             # of the thread may be released now.  A handle that could
05044             # not be closed keeps its owner and a later close may try it
05045             # again.
05046             if not worker.release_thread_handle():
05047                 retained.append(worker)
05048         # The state of the workers is **recomputed** on every close: a
05049         # worker that really ended and whose handle of its thread was
05050         # released is not retained any more.  A closed file never locks
05051         # the recovery of the handle of a thread.
05052         self._retained = retained
05053         self.facts['retained_workers'] = len(self._retained)
05054         self.facts['worker_alive'] = alive
05055         self.facts['worker_thread_handle_released'] = all(
05056             worker.thread_handle_released
05057             for worker in (self._reader, self._writer) if worker is not None)
05058         if alive:
05059             # A live worker keeps the file: the file is never closed
05060             # under a worker that is still inside its operation.
05061             self.facts['closed'] = False
05062             return self.facts
05063         if self._closed:
05064             self.facts['closed'] = True
