05065             self.facts['already_closed'] = True
05066             return self.facts
05067         try:
05068             self._file.close()
05069         except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
05070             self.facts['closed'] = False
05071             self.facts['error_type'] = type(error).__name__
05072             return self.facts
05073         self._closed = True
05074         self.facts['closed'] = True
05075         return self.facts
05076 
05077 
05078 def _make_endpoint(file_object, name, *, api=None, clock=None, context=None):
05079     """The endpoint of one end of the private pipe of this host.
05080 
05081     The Windows host gets the supervised and cancellable endpoint and
05082     every other host gets the non blocking endpoint of the original
05083     descriptor.  The choice is made by the host and never by a string
05084     that a caller could set: a platform that is not Windows never loads
05085     the Windows table.
05086 
05087     The Windows endpoint needs the primitive table of the host, because
05088     the cancellation of a synchronous operation needs the original
05089     handle of the thread that performs it.  The table is loaded only on
05090     Windows.
05091 
05092     The ONE context of the round is handed to the endpoint: the
05093     endpoint reads the absolute deadline of the round from it and it
05094     never keeps a budget of its own.  The context is bound to the
05095     **primitive table** as well when the endpoint owns the table: a
05096     table that does not know the context of the round would open a
05097     budget of its own.
05098     """
05099     if os.name == 'nt':
05100         if api is None:
05101             api = WindowsApi()
05102         binder = getattr(api, 'bind_context', None)
05103         if callable(binder) and context is not None:
05104             try:
05105                 binder(context)
05106             except BaseException:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
05107                 pass
05108         return _WindowsPipeEndpoint(file_object, name=name, api=api, clock=clock,
05109                                     context=context)
05110     return _PosixPipeEndpoint(file_object, name=name, clock=clock)
05111 
05112 
05113 # -- the private identity of a cancellation of the round --------------------
05114 
05115 class _CloseSource:
05116     """The private identity of one close of one channel.
05117 
05118     The identity is an **object**: two closes of the same channel never
05119     share it and the owner of a cancellation is compared by identity
05120     and never by the name of a code.  The sequence is the order of the
05121     closes of the channel and it is part of the evidence.
05122     """
05123 
05124     def __init__(self, sequence):
05125         self.sequence = sequence
05126         self.owner = 'CHANNEL_CLOSE'
05127 
05128 
05129 class _ChannelCancel:
05130     """The cancel flag of one channel, with the owner that set it.
05131 
05132     The flag names the **owner** that set it: a cancellation that the
05133     close of this channel initiated is provable and a cancellation that
05134     another owner initiated is not.  The owner is registered in the
05135     same atomic step as the setting of the flag and it is registered
05136     **before** the flag is set, so that a reader can never see the flag
05137     set without its owner.
05138 
05139     The **first** owner wins: a flag that another owner already set is
05140     never re-labelled by a later one.  A cancellation that this channel
05141     did not initiate is therefore never taken for a cancellation of the
05142     close of this channel.
05143     """
05144 
05145     def __init__(self):
05146         self._event = threading.Event()
05147         self._lock = threading.Lock()
05148         self._owner = None
05149 
05150     def is_set(self):
05151         return self._event.is_set()
05152 
05153     def set(self, owner=None):
05154         """Set the flag and name the owner that set it.
05155 
05156         The owner is written under the lock of the flag and it is
05157         written **before** the flag is set: the registration of the
05158         owner is never later than the cancellation itself.
05159         """
05160         with self._lock:
05161             if not self._event.is_set():
05162                 if owner is not None:
05163                     self._owner = owner
05164                 self._event.set()
05165 
05166     def owner(self):
05167         """The owner that set the flag, or None when it named none."""
05168         with self._lock:
05169             return self._owner
05170 
05171     def wait(self, timeout=None):
05172         return self._event.wait(timeout)
05173 
05174 
05175 def _cancel_source_error(error, cancel):
05176     """Stamp the owner of a cancel flag on the error of a cancellation.
05177 
05178     The owner is read from the flag **at the origin of the error**: the
05179     error itself carries the identity of the owner that really set the
05180     flag and it never carries a claim of a caller.  A flag that names
05181     no owner leaves the error untouched: an unknown source is never
05182     taken for a known one.
05183     """
05184     owner = getattr(cancel, 'owner', None)
05185     if not callable(owner):
05186         return error
05187     try:
05188         source = owner()
05189     except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
05190         return error
05191     if source is None:
05192         return error
05193     try:
05194         error.lvw_cancel_source = source
05195     except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
05196         pass
05197     return error
05198 
05199 
05200 # -- the private control channel -------------------------------------------
05201 
05202 class StreamChannel:
05203     """The private control channel of the broker.
05204 
05205     The channel is the broker's own standard input and output.  It is
05206     bounded three times: one frame is at most 32 KiB, the channel carries
05207     at most 1024 frames and the text of the children is bounded by the
05208     core.
05209 
05210     The read side is drained by one background thread, so the broker never
05211     blocks on the read.  A parent that stops talking therefore cannot hold
05212     the deadline of the round: the deadline is checked by the broker
05213     itself.
05214 
05215     The channel owns the original handles of its two ends.  A read and a
05216     write are bounded by an absolute deadline and by a cancel flag, so a
05217     peer that never reads can never hold the supervisor of the round.
05218     """
05219 
05220     def __init__(self, reader, writer, *, max_frames=MAX_FRAMES,
05221                  max_frame_bytes=MAX_FRAME_BYTES, name='channel',
05222                  read_endpoint=None, write_endpoint=None, clock=None, api=None,
05223                  context=None, custody=None):
05224         self._queue = queue.Queue(maxsize=max_frames)
05225         self._max_frames = max_frames
05226         self._max_frame_bytes = max_frame_bytes
05227         self._count = 0
05228         self._overflow = False
05229         self._overflow_facts = {}
05230         self._eof = False
05231         self._error = None
05232         self._buffer = b''
05233         self._deadline = None
05234         self._clock = clock or time.monotonic
05235         # The cancel flag of the channel names the owner that set it:
05236         # only a cancellation that the close of **this** channel
05237         # initiated is provable and only a provable one is a normal
05238         # fact of the close.
05239         self._cancel = _ChannelCancel()
05240         # The private state of the close of this channel: the identity
05241         # of the close that is running now and the named fact of the
05242         # cancellation that this close really initiated.
05243         self._close_source = None
05244         self._close_sequence = 0
05245         self._close_cancellation = None
05246         # The cancellation that arrived at the same moment as the
05247         # failure of the reader: it is a parallel fact and it never
05248         # replaces the failure.
05249         self._parallel_cancellation = None
05250         self.reader_alive = None
05251         self.reader_finished = None
05252         # The ONE context of the round.  The channel and its endpoints
05253         # read the absolute deadline of the round from it and they never
05254         # keep a budget of their own.
05255         self._context = context
05256         self._thread = threading.Thread(target=self._drain, daemon=True,
05257                                         name='lvw-native-custody-channel')
05258         # The channel registers itself with the custody of the launcher
05259         # **before** it builds anything: a channel whose construction
05260         # failed half way - an endpoint that could not be built, a
05261         # reader thread that did not start - may not leave an endpoint
05262         # and a thread without an owner.  The outer owner of the launch
05263         # finds the partial channel through this registration and it
05264         # never closes the files of the process by itself.
05265         self._custody = custody
05266         if custody is not None:
05267             custody.retain('channel', self, 'constructing')
05268         try:
05269             self._reader = read_endpoint or _make_endpoint(
05270                 reader, name + '-read', clock=clock, api=api, context=context)
05271             # The parameter of the constructor is ``write_endpoint`` and
05272             # the attribute of the channel is ``_writer``.  The two names
05273             # are never mixed: a name that does not exist raises a
05274             # ``NameError`` and every construction of a channel of this
05275             # package fails with it.
05276             self._writer = write_endpoint or _make_endpoint(
05277                 writer, name + '-write', clock=clock, api=api, context=context)
05278             self._thread.start()
05279         except BaseException:
05280             # The construction of the channel did not return: the
05281             # channel keeps its owner in the custody of the launcher and
05282             # it is never forgotten.
05283             raise
05284 
05285     def bind_context(self, context):
05286         """Bind the ONE context of the round to the channel.
05287 
05288         The context is bound to the channel **and** to its two
05289         endpoints: every layer of the round reads the same absolute
05290         deadline and none of them keeps a deadline of its own.
05291         """
05292         if context is not None:
05293             self._context = context
05294             for endpoint in (self._reader, self._writer):
05295                 binder = getattr(endpoint, 'bind_context', None)
05296                 if callable(binder):
05297                     binder(context)
05298         return self._context
05299 
05300     def bind_deadline(self, deadline):
05301         """Bind the absolute deadline of the round to the channel.
05302 
05303         The deadline is bound to the channel **and** to its two
05304         endpoints.  A binding that happens after a read started still
05305         tightens that read: the deadline is a property of the endpoint
05306         and not a local argument of one call.
05307         """
05308         value = _require_deadline(deadline)
05309         if value is None:
05310             return self._deadline
05311         if self._deadline is not None and value > self._deadline:
05312             return self._deadline
05313         self._deadline = value
05314         for endpoint in (self._reader, self._writer):
05315             binder = getattr(endpoint, 'bind_deadline', None)
05316             if callable(binder):
05317                 try:
05318                     binder(value)
05319                 except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
05320                     pass
05321         return value
05322 
05323     def _effective_deadline(self, deadline=None):
05324         """The ONE deadline that a call of this channel really uses.
05325 
05326         The deadline of the call, the deadline that is bound to the
05327         channel, the absolute deadline of the round and the **published**
05328         cleanup deadline of the round are one value: the earliest of the
05329         four.
05330 
05331         The published cleanup deadline is part of it.  A round that
05332         entered its cleanup publishes one deadline, and the transport of
05333         the round - the write of the terminal reply and the close of the
05334         channel - is bound by it: a transport that still waited with the
05335         long lease of the round would outlive the only cleanup budget
05336         of the round.
05337         """
05338         value = _require_deadline(deadline)
05339         bound = self._deadline
05340         if bound is not None and (value is None or bound < value):
05341             value = bound
05342         context = self._context
05343         if context is not None:
05344             absolute = context.absolute_deadline
05345             if absolute is not None and (value is None or absolute < value):
05346                 value = absolute
05347             cleanup = context.cleanup_deadline_value()
05348             if cleanup is not None and (value is None or cleanup < value):
05349                 value = cleanup
05350         return value
05351 
05352     def _remaining(self, deadline=None):
05353         """The seconds that are left until the deadline of the channel."""
05354         value = self._effective_deadline(deadline)
05355         if value is None:
05356             return None
05357         return max(0.0, value - (self._clock or time.monotonic)())
05358 
05359     def _drain(self):
05360         try:
05361             while True:
05362                 block = self._reader.read(self._deadline, self._cancel)
05363                 if not block:
05364                     break
05365                 buffer = self._buffer + block
05366                 self._buffer = b''
05367                 while True:
05368                     index = buffer.find(b'\n')
05369                     if index < 0:
05370                         break
05371                     raw = buffer[:index + 1]
05372                     buffer = buffer[index + 1:]
05373                     if len(raw) > self._max_frame_bytes:
05374                         self._record_overflow('FRAME_TOO_LARGE',
05375                                               {'frame_bytes': len(raw),
05376                                                'limit': self._max_frame_bytes})
05377                         return
05378                     self._count += 1
05379                     if self._count > self._max_frames:
05380                         self._record_overflow('TOO_MANY_FRAMES',
05381                                               {'frames': self._count,
05382                                                'limit': self._max_frames})
05383                         return
05384                     while True:
05385                         self._check_wait()
05386                         try:
05387                             self._queue.put(raw, timeout=self._put_wait())
05388                             break
05389                         except queue.Full:
05390                             if self._eof:
05391                                 return
05392                             continue
05393                 # The remainder of the block has no newline.  The buffer
05394                 # of the channel is bounded: it is checked on **every**
05395                 # append and an oversize buffer is refused at once.  One
05396                 # byte beyond the limit is kept, so that the overflow is
05397                 # detected and not silently trimmed.
05398                 if len(buffer) > self._max_frame_bytes:
05399                     self._buffer = buffer[:self._max_frame_bytes + 1]
05400                     self._record_overflow('BUFFER_TOO_LARGE',
05401                                           {'buffer_bytes': len(self._buffer),
05402                                            'limit': self._max_frame_bytes})
05403                     return
05404                 self._buffer = buffer
05405         except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
05406             self._record_reader_error(error)
05407         finally:
05408             self._eof = True
05409             if self._buffer and self._error is None:
05410                 # The stream ended in the middle of a frame.  An
05411                 # incomplete frame is a named failure of the channel and
05412                 # it is never dropped silently.
05413                 self._error = 'BROKER_CHANNEL_PARTIAL_FRAME'
05414 
05415     def _record_overflow(self, reason, facts):
05416         """Record the overflow of the channel at the place where it happened.
05417 
05418         The overflow is a **named** failure of the channel and it is
05419         recorded **here**, where it really happened.  A flag that only
05420         the internals of the channel know would leave the close of the
05421         channel with no error at all: the reader of a channel that
05422         overflowed would look like a reader that ended normally and the
05423         round would release its resources on a fact that never happened.
05424 
05425         The error of the channel is **sticky**: the first named failure
05426         of the reader stays the error of the channel and the overflow
05427         never replaces it.
05428         """
05429         self._overflow = True
05430         self._overflow_facts = dict(facts, reason=reason)
05431         if self._error is None:
05432             self._error = 'BROKER_CHANNEL_OVERFLOW'
05433 
05434     def _record_reader_error(self, error):
05435         """Record the error of the reader of this channel.
05436 
05437         A cancellation that **this** channel initiated with **this**
05438         close is not a failure of the reader: it is a named fact of the
05439         close.  It is kept with its code, with its source and with its
05440         order for the evidence, and it never replaces the error of the
05441         reader and it never clears a historical error of the reader.
05442 
05443         Every other error keeps the original error of the reader and the
05444         round stays unknown: an ordinary failure of the read, a timeout,
05445         an overflow, a partial frame, an error that was already there
05446         before the close and a cancellation whose owner is unknown or
05447         is not the close of this channel.
05448         """
05449         source = getattr(error, 'lvw_cancel_source', None)
05450         if source is not None and source is self._close_source:
05451             # This cancellation was initiated by the close of this
05452             # channel: it is a named fact of the close and it is not a
05453             # failure of the reader.  The historical error of the reader
05454             # is never cleared and it is never replaced by this fact.
05455             self._close_cancellation = {
05456                 'code': getattr(error, 'code', None) or type(error).__name__,
05457                 'source': getattr(source, 'owner', 'CHANNEL_CLOSE'),
05458                 'sequence': getattr(source, 'sequence', None),
05459                 'error_type': type(error).__name__,
05460             }
05461             return
05462         parallel = getattr(error, 'lvw_parallel_cancellation', None)
05463         if parallel is not None:
05464             # The cancellation that arrived at the same moment as the
05465             # failure of the reader is a **parallel** fact: it is named
05466             # next to the failure and it never replaces it.
05467             self._parallel_cancellation = dict(parallel)
05468         self._error = getattr(error, 'code', None) or type(error).__name__
05469 
05470     def _check_wait(self):
05471         """The cancel flag and the deadline of the wait of the reader."""
05472         if self._cancel.is_set():
05473             # The error carries the owner of the flag: the reader of
05474             # the channel and the close of the channel agree on the
05475             # origin of the cancellation and the evidence can prove it.
05476             raise _cancel_source_error(BrokerError('BROKER_CHANNEL_CANCELLED'),
05477                                        self._cancel)
05478         remaining = self._remaining()
05479         if remaining is not None and remaining <= 0:
05480             raise BrokerError('BROKER_CHANNEL_TIMEOUT')
05481 
05482     def _put_wait(self):
05483         """The seconds that the next put of the reader may ask for.
05484 
05485         The put is the bounded wait of the package, cropped to the
05486         time that is really left until the **current** effective
05487         deadline of the round.  There is no positive floor beyond it:
05488         an expired round is refused by the check that precedes the put
05489         and the put itself never waits longer than the round allows.
05490         """
05491         remaining = self._remaining()
05492         if remaining is None:
05493             return CHANNEL_QUEUE_PUT_SECONDS
05494         return max(0.0, min(CHANNEL_QUEUE_PUT_SECONDS, remaining))
05495 
05496     def read(self, timeout):
05497         """One raw frame, ``None`` when nothing arrived, ``b''`` at the end.
05498 
05499         The wait of the reader is cropped to the **current** effective
05500         deadline of the round: the bound of the channel, the absolute
05501         deadline of the round and the **published** cleanup deadline of
05502         the round are read **again** at the moment of the read.  There is
05503         **no positive floor** beyond them: a round whose bound arrived
05504         asks for nothing at all and the read becomes a non blocking
05505         inspection.
05506 
05507         The crop applies to **every** reader of the channel: the loop of
05508         the broker and the pump of the parent read the same channel and
05509         neither of them may wait with a lease of its own.
05510         """
05511         if self._overflow:
05512             raise BrokerError('BROKER_CHANNEL_OVERFLOW')
05513         limit = max(0.0, timeout)
05514         remaining = self._remaining()
05515         if remaining is not None:
05516             if remaining <= 0:
05517                 # The bound of the round arrived: the read asks for
05518                 # nothing and it never opens a wait of its own.
05519                 limit = 0.0
05520             elif remaining < limit:
05521                 limit = remaining
05522         try:
05523             return self._queue.get(timeout=limit)
05524         except queue.Empty:
05525             if self._error:
05526                 raise BrokerError(self._error)
05527             if self._eof:
05528                 return b''
05529             return None
05530 
05531     def write(self, raw, deadline=None):
05532         """Write one frame, bounded by the deadline of the round.
05533 
05534         The number of the bytes that really went into the pipe is
05535         checked: a partial write is never reported as a success.
05536         """
05537         if len(raw) > self._max_frame_bytes:
05538             raise BrokerError('BROKER_CHANNEL_FRAME_TOO_LARGE')
05539         target = self._effective_deadline(deadline)
05540         written = self._writer.write(raw, target, self._cancel)
05541         if written is not None and written != len(raw):
05542             raise BrokerError('BROKER_CHANNEL_WRITE_INCOMPLETE',
05543                               'written=%s:expected=%d' % (written, len(raw)))
05544         return written
05545 
05546     def close(self):
05547         """Close the channel and report whether the reader really stopped.
05548 
05549         The reader thread is joined with a bounded wait.  A reader that
05550         did not stop is reported as such: a daemon thread is not a
05551         finished thread, so the fact that the reader is still alive is
05552         kept visible instead of being hidden behind the close.  The read
05553         end is closed by its owner only when the reader really stopped.
05554 
05555         The facts of the close carry the error of the reader, the number
05556         of the workers that the endpoints retained and the fact that the
05557         original handles of those workers were released: a reader that
05558         ended with an error is not a successful end.
05559 
05560         The close may be **repeated**: a close that did not confirm its
05561         ends may try them again, and a close of an endpoint that is
05562         already closed keeps the fact that it is closed.
05563 
05564         The error of the reader of the channel is **sticky** and it is
05565         never cleared by a later close of the channel: a close that
05566         returned is not a release and the error of the reader stays.
05567 
05568         The close **initiates** the cancellation of the reader and it
05569         names itself as the owner of that cancellation before the flag
05570         is set.  A cancellation that this close really initiated is a
05571         named fact of the close and it is not a failure of the reader; a
05572         cancellation of another owner, a failure of the read that
05573         happened before the close and every other error of the reader
05574         keep the round unknown.
05575         """
05576         facts = {}
05577         # The identity of this close is published **before** the
05578         # cancellation is initiated and the flag registers the owner in
05579         # the same atomic step as the setting of the flag.
05580         self._close_sequence += 1
05581         source = _CloseSource(self._close_sequence)
05582         self._close_source = source
05583         self._cancel.set(source)
05584         try:
05585             write_facts = self._writer.close()
05586             facts['write_end_closed'] = bool(write_facts.get('closed'))
05587             facts['write_retained_workers'] = int(
05588                 write_facts.get('retained_workers') or 0)
05589             facts['write_worker_thread_handle_released'] = bool(
05590                 write_facts.get('worker_thread_handle_released'))
05591         except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
05592             facts['write_end_closed'] = False
05593             facts['write_retained_workers'] = 0
05594             facts['write_worker_thread_handle_released'] = False
05595         remaining = self._remaining()
05596         join_seconds = CLOSE_JOIN_SECONDS if remaining is None else remaining
05597         start_unconfirmed = False
05598         try:
05599             self._thread.join(join_seconds)
05600         except RuntimeError:
05601             # The join could not prove the end of the reader: the start
05602             # of the thread of the reader was **interrupted** or it was
05603             # never confirmed.  A start that did not return may still
05604             # come up and a late reader may still read from the read
05605             # end of the channel.  The state of the reader therefore
05606             # stays **unknown**: the read end is never closed underneath
05607             # a possibly late target and the round keeps the fact.
05608             self.reader_alive = True
05609             self.reader_finished = False
05610             start_unconfirmed = True
05611         else:
05612             self.reader_alive = self._thread.is_alive()
05613             self.reader_finished = not self.reader_alive
05614         facts['reader_finished'] = self.reader_finished
05615         facts['reader_error'] = self._error
05616         # The start of the reader that was never confirmed is a named
05617         # fact of the close: the state of the reader is unknown and it
05618         # is never read as a finished reader.
05619         facts['reader_start_unconfirmed'] = start_unconfirmed
05620         # The facts of the overflow are published with the close: the
05621         # overflow is a named failure of the channel and it is never
05622         # only an internal flag.
05623         facts['overflow'] = bool(self._overflow)
05624         facts['overflow_facts'] = dict(self._overflow_facts)
05625         # The parallel fact of the cancellation of the close: it never
05626         # replaces the error of the reader.
05627         facts['reader_parallel_cancellation'] = (
05628             dict(self._parallel_cancellation)
05629             if self._parallel_cancellation else None)
05630         # The named fact of the cancellation that this close really
05631         # initiated.  It is **not** the error of the reader and it does
05632         # not prove that the reader, the thread, the worker or the
05633         # handle of the round were released: the strict facts of the
05634         # close above are the ones that prove it.
05635         facts['close_cancellation'] = (dict(self._close_cancellation)
05636                                        if self._close_cancellation else None)
05637         if self.reader_alive:
05638             # The reader is still inside the pipe: the read end is not
05639             # closed under it.  It stays owned by the channel and the
05640             # round keeps the fact.  The facts of the reader endpoint are
05641             # the real ones and they are never replaced by a zero.
05642             endpoint_facts = getattr(self._reader, 'facts', None)
05643             endpoint_facts = endpoint_facts if isinstance(endpoint_facts, dict) else {}
05644             facts['read_end_closed'] = False
05645             facts['read_retained_workers'] = int(
05646                 endpoint_facts.get('retained_workers') or 0)
05647             facts['read_worker_thread_handle_released'] = bool(
05648                 endpoint_facts.get('worker_thread_handle_released'))
05649             facts['worker_thread_handle_released'] = bool(
05650                 facts.get('write_worker_thread_handle_released')
05651                 and facts.get('read_worker_thread_handle_released'))
05652             facts['channel_retained_workers'] = (int(facts.get('write_retained_workers') or 0)
05653                                                   + int(facts.get('read_retained_workers') or 0))
05654             return facts
05655         try:
05656             read_facts = self._reader.close()
05657             facts['read_end_closed'] = bool(read_facts.get('closed'))
05658             facts['read_retained_workers'] = int(
05659                 read_facts.get('retained_workers') or 0)
05660             facts['read_worker_thread_handle_released'] = bool(
05661                 read_facts.get('worker_thread_handle_released'))
05662         except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
05663             facts['read_end_closed'] = False
05664             facts['read_retained_workers'] = 0
05665             facts['read_worker_thread_handle_released'] = False
05666         facts['worker_thread_handle_released'] = bool(
05667             facts.get('write_worker_thread_handle_released')
05668             and facts.get('read_worker_thread_handle_released'))
05669         facts['channel_retained_workers'] = (int(facts.get('write_retained_workers') or 0)
05670                                               + int(facts.get('read_retained_workers') or 0))
05671         return facts
05672 
05673     @property
05674     def eof(self):
05675         return self._eof
05676 
05677     @property
05678     def overflow(self):
05679         return self._overflow
05680 
05681     @property
05682     def frames(self):
05683         return self._count
05684 
05685 
05686 # -- the bounded command loop ----------------------------------------------
05687 
05688 class _StartupOwner:
05689     """The bounded owner of one startup step of the round.
05690 
05691     A startup step may block: the creation of a job, the creation of a
05692     process and the assignment of a process may all block inside the
05693     operating system.  Such a step may not run unbounded inside the one
05694     supervision thread of the round.
05695 
05696     The owner therefore runs the step in its own thread and hands the
05697     result over through a **bounded** channel of one slot.  The
05698     supervisor waits for the result until the absolute deadline of the
05699     round.  A step that did not finish in time keeps the round unknown,
05700     keeps the thread and its owner, and a result that arrives late may
05701     only clean up: it may never continue the startup of the round.
05702     """
05703 
05704     def __init__(self, *, name, clock, deadline, context=None):
05705         self.name = name
05706         self._clock = clock
05707         self._deadline = deadline
05708         # The ONE context of the round.  The owner reads the **current**
05709         # absolute deadline and the **published** cleanup deadline of
05710         # the round from it and it never keeps a copy of a deadline of
05711         # its own.
05712         self._context = context
05713         self._results = queue.Queue(maxsize=STARTUP_INFLIGHT_SLOTS)
05714         self._thread = None
05715         self._error = None
05716         self._value = None
05717         self._finished = threading.Event()
05718         self._expired = False
05719         # Whether the start of the thread of this owner **returned**.
05720         # A start that did not return may still come up: the owner keeps
05721         # its thread and the round keeps the fact.  The flag is never
05722         # the authority of the question: the facts of the thread itself
05723         # are.
05724         self._started = False
05725 
05726     def start(self, action):
05727         """Run one startup step in the thread of this owner.
05728 
05729         The start of the thread is **one** step and it is recorded: a
05730         start that did not return keeps its owner and the thread of the
05731         step may still come up.  The owner is never released because
05732         the start failed.
05733 
05734         A start that raised **after** the thread really came up leaves
05735         the flag of the owner unset, and the facts of the thread are
05736         consulted instead: the thread of the step is a thread of the
05737         round and the owner may join it.
05738         """
05739         self._thread = threading.Thread(
05740             target=self._run, args=(action,), daemon=True,
05741             name='lvw-native-custody-startup-' + self.name)
05742         try:
05743             self._thread.start()
05744         except BaseException:
05745             # The start did not return: the thread may really have come
05746             # up and the facts of the thread decide.
05747             self._started = self._thread_facts_started()
05748             raise
05749         self._started = True
05750         return self
05751 
05752     def _thread_facts_started(self):
05753         """Whether the original facts of the thread prove that it started.
05754 
05755         The private flag of the owner is **not** the authority: it is
05756         written after the start returned and a start that raised after
05757         the thread really came up leaves it unset.  The identity of the
05758         thread and its liveness are the facts that decide whether a
05759         real join may be attempted.
05760         """
05761         thread = self._thread
05762         if thread is None:
05763             return False
05764         try:
05765             if getattr(thread, 'ident', None) is not None:
05766                 return True
05767         except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
05768             return False
05769         try:
05770             return bool(thread.is_alive())
05771         except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
05772             return False
05773 
05774     def _remaining(self):
05775         """The seconds that are left until the current bound of the step.
05776 
05777         The bound is the **current** effective deadline of the original
05778         context of the round - its absolute deadline and the published
05779         cleanup deadline - together with the deadline that the owner
05780         received.  The value is recomputed on every call: a deadline
05781         that was published while the step was running shortens the wait
05782         that is running.
05783         """
05784         candidates = []
05785         context = self._context
05786         if context is not None:
05787             absolute = context.absolute_deadline
05788             if absolute is not None:
05789                 candidates.append(absolute)
05790             cleanup = context.cleanup_deadline_value()
05791             if cleanup is not None:
05792                 candidates.append(cleanup)
05793         if self._deadline is not None:
05794             candidates.append(self._deadline)
05795         if not candidates:
05796             return None
05797         return max(0.0, min(candidates) - self._clock())
05798 
05799     def _cancelled(self):
05800         """Whether the original context of the round asked for the stop."""
05801         context = self._context
05802         return context is not None and context.cancel.is_set()
05803 
05804     def _run(self, action):
05805         try:
05806             value = action()
05807         except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
05808             self._error = error
05809             outcome = ('error', error)
05810         else:
05811             self._value = value
05812             outcome = ('value', value)
05813         # The **attempt** of the step ended.  The event of the attempt is
05814         # published here and it is never a proof that the thread of the
05815         # step ended: the thread still has to publish its result and to
05816         # leave.
05817         self._finished.set()
05818         self._put(outcome)
05819 
05820     def _put(self, item):
05821         try:
05822             self._results.put_nowait(item)
05823         except queue.Full:
05824             # The bounded channel is full: the result is dropped and the
05825             # owner keeps the error.  The channel never grows.
05826             pass
05827 
05828     def await_result(self):
05829         """Wait for the result of the step, bounded by the deadline.
05830 
05831         The result that is already published is **never** replaced by a
05832         refusal of the deadline: the original failure of the step is
05833         the error that leaves.
05834 
05835         The wait of the next slice is cropped to the time that is
05836         really left until the **current** effective deadline of the
05837         original context of the round and there is no positive floor
05838         beyond it: an expired round refuses at once.
05839         """
05840         while True:
05841             try:
05842                 kind, value = self._results.get_nowait()
05843             except queue.Empty:
05844                 kind = None
05845                 value = None
05846             if kind is not None:
05847                 if kind == 'error':
05848                     raise value
05849                 return value
05850             remaining = self._remaining()
05851             if remaining is not None and remaining <= 0:
05852                 self._expired = True
05853                 raise BrokerError('BROKER_STARTUP_DEADLINE_EXCEEDED', self.name)
05854             if self._cancelled():
05855                 raise BrokerError('BROKER_STARTUP_CANCELLED', self.name)
05856             wait = POLL_SLICE_SECONDS
05857             if remaining is not None:
05858                 wait = max(0.0, min(wait, remaining))
05859             try:
05860                 kind, value = self._results.get(timeout=wait)
05861             except queue.Empty:
05862                 continue
05863             if kind == 'error':
05864                 raise value
05865             return value
05866 
05867     def settle(self, seconds):
05868         """Wait, bounded, for the **thread** of this owner to end.
05869 
05870         The wait is bounded by the shared cleanup deadline of the round
05871         and it never adds a settle budget of its own.
05872 
05873         The end of the **attempt** of the step and the end of the
05874         **thread** that performs it are two different facts.  The event
05875         of the attempt is published before the result and before the
05876         thread leaves, and it is therefore never a proof that the
05877         thread ended.  The settle asks the thread itself: a thread that
05878         is still running keeps its owner and the round stays unknown.
05879 
05880         A **start that did not return** is unknown only when the facts
05881         of the thread do not prove that it came up.  A thread that
05882         really started is joined for real, even when the start raised
05883         afterwards: the owner of the step coordinates the thread that it
05884         really has and it never releases it without a proof.
05885         """
05886         if self._thread is None:
05887             return True
05888         if not self._started and not self._thread_facts_started():
05889             # The start of the thread did not return and the thread
05890             # really did not come up: it may still come up and the owner
05891             # is never released.
05892             return False
05893         try:
05894             self._thread.join(max(0.0, seconds))
05895         except RuntimeError:
05896             # The thread was never started: it is not a thread of the
05897             # round that ended.
05898             return False
05899         return not self._thread.is_alive()
05900 
05901     @property
05902     def expired(self):
05903         """Whether the step of this owner missed the deadline."""
05904         return self._expired
05905 
05906     @property
05907     def in_flight(self):
05908         """Whether the step of this owner is still running."""
05909         return self._thread is not None and self._thread.is_alive()
05910 
05911 
05912 class BrokerLoop:
05913     """The bounded command loop of the broker.
05914 
05915     The loop owns the absolute deadline of the round.  It is fixed once,
05916     from the lease of the plan, and it is never moved: neither a status
05917     request nor a heartbeat may extend it.
05918 
05919     The round is stopped when the parent asks for it, when the channel
05920     reaches its end, when a bound of the channel or of the text of the
05921     children is exceeded or when the absolute deadline arrives.  A stop
05922     that was not requested by the parent keeps the evidence of the round
05923     unknown: the round did not end in a proven release.
05924 
05925     The startup of the round is supervised.  The arm and the launch are
05926     **bounded startup steps** with their own owner: a step that blocks
05927     may not hold the supervision of the round and a step that returns
05928     after the absolute deadline may only clean up.
05929     """
05930 
05931     def __init__(self, *, core, transport, plan, nonce, clock=None, wall=None,
05932                  max_frames=MAX_FRAMES):
05933         self.core = core
05934         self.transport = transport
05935         self.plan = plan
05936         self.nonce = nonce
05937         self._clock = clock or time.monotonic
05938         self._wall = wall or time.time
05939         self._max_frames = max_frames
05940         self._sequence = 0
05941         self._last_sequence = None
05942         self._frames = 0
05943         self._sent_frames = 0
05944         self._deadline_wall = None
05945         self._deadline_mono = None
05946         self._last_frame_wall = None
05947         self._last_alive_wall = None
05948         self._stopped = False
05949         # The ONE context of the round.  The loop holds the very same
05950         # object as the core: the deadlines, the cancel and the unknown
05951         # of the round are one state and not one number per layer.
05952         self.context = core.context
05953         # The owner of the startup step that is in flight.  A step that
05954         # did not end keeps the round unknown and it keeps its owner.
05955         self._startup_owner = None
05956         self._startup_in_flight = None
05957 
05958     # -- the deadline ------------------------------------------------------
05959     def fix_deadline(self):
05960         """Fix the absolute deadline of the round and return it.
05961 
05962         The deadline is fixed **before** the transport of the round
05963         exists: the reader of the channel and the workers of the
05964         endpoints never start without a deadline and the child has no
05965         unbound window.
05966         """
05967         return self._fix_deadline()
05968 
05969     def bind_transport(self, transport):
05970         """Bind the transport of the round to this loop.
05971 
05972         The transport is bound after the deadline of the round was
05973         fixed: the transport of the round is never created without the
05974         deadline that bounds it.
05975         """
05976         self.transport = transport
05977         return transport
05978 
05979     def _fix_deadline(self):
05980         """Fix the absolute deadline of the round, once, before anything.
05981 
05982         The deadline is fixed before the job of the round is created,
05983         before the first reply is written and before the first role is
05984         launched.  It is the earlier of the lease of the plan and the
05985         runtime of the plan and it is never moved afterwards.
05986         """
05987         if self._deadline_mono is not None:
05988             return self._deadline_mono
05989         wall_now = self._wall()
05990         self._deadline_wall = min(self.plan.lease_expires_at,
05991                                   wall_now + self.plan.max_runtime_seconds)
05992         self._deadline_mono = self._clock() + max(0.0, self._deadline_wall - wall_now)
05993         return self._deadline_mono
05994 
05995     def remaining_seconds(self):
05996         if self._deadline_mono is None:
05997             return float(self.plan.max_runtime_seconds)
05998         return max(0.0, self._deadline_mono - self._clock())
05999 
06000     # -- the replies -------------------------------------------------------
06001     def _check_frame_budget(self, reserved=RESERVED_TERMINAL_FRAMES):
06002         """The frame budget of the channel, in both directions.
06003 
06004         The budget of the round is shared by the frames that the broker
06005         sends and by the frames that it receives: the round of six hours
06006         with its heartbeat stays inside the budget of the channel.  The
06007         frames of the terminal exchange are reserved and the budget is
06008         never widened and never renewed.
06009 
06010         The check is the **total** of both directions.  A single
06011         direction is never a substitute for the total: a channel that
06012         received a thousand frames and sent none is not inside the
06013         budget when the budget is a thousand and twenty four frames.
06014         """
06015         if self._frames + self._sent_frames + reserved + 1 > MAX_FRAMES:
06016             raise BrokerError('BROKER_FRAME_BUDGET_EXCEEDED',
06017                               'sent=%d:received=%d' % (self._sent_frames,
06018                                                        self._frames))
06019 
06020     def _reply(self, kind, state, detail, *, terminal=False):
06021         self._check_frame_budget(0 if terminal else RESERVED_TERMINAL_FRAMES)
06022         self._sent_frames += 1
06023         self._sequence += 1
06024         frame = {'schema': CHANNEL_SCHEMA, 'kind': kind, 'run_id': self.plan.run_id,
06025                  'candidate_sha': self.plan.candidate_sha,
06026                  'authorization_sha256': self.plan.authorization_sha256,
06027                  'nonce': self.nonce, 'sequence': self._sequence,
06028                  'state': state, 'detail': detail}
06029         raw = json.dumps(frame, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True,
06030                          separators=(',', ':'), allow_nan=False).encode('utf-8')
06031         if len(raw) > MAX_FRAME_BYTES:
06032             frame['detail'] = {'truncated': True}
06033             raw = json.dumps(frame, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True,
06034                              separators=(',', ':'), allow_nan=False).encode('utf-8')
06035         # The deadline of the round is already bound to the transport, so
06036         # the write of the frame is bounded by it.  The transport is
06037         # never called with an argument that it does not know.
06038         self.transport.write(raw + b'\n')
06039 
06040     # -- the supervised startup --------------------------------------------
06041     def _guarded_startup(self, action, stage):
06042         """One startup step, guarded by the shared deadline.
06043 
06044         The shared budget is checked **before** the step and **after**
06045         it returned.  A step that starts after the deadline of the round
06046         arrived never runs at all, and a step that finished after the
06047         deadline may only clean up: it may never continue the startup of
06048         the round and it may never write a successful reply.
06049 
06050         The supervision publishes the cancel of the round when the
06051         deadline arrived: the core and every later step of the startup
06052         see it and they stop.
06053         """
06054         def guarded():
06055             self._check_startup_budget(stage)
06056             action()
06057             self._check_startup_budget(stage)
06058             return None
06059         return guarded
06060 
06061     def _check_startup_budget(self, stage):
06062         """Check the shared budget of the startup of the round."""
06063         try:
06064             self.core.check_startup_budget(stage)
06065         except BrokerError:
06066             # The supervision publishes the cancel of the round: the
06067             # core and every later step of the startup see it.
06068             self.context.request_cancel()
06069             self._abort_started_round()
06070             raise
06071 
06072     def _abort_started_round(self):
06073         """Release what the round acquired, bounded by the shared budget."""
06074         try:
06075             self.core.stop('STARTUP_AFTER_DEADLINE', force_unknown=True)
06076         except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
06077             self.core.failures.append({'stage': 'startup_abort',
06078                                        'error_type': type(error).__name__})
06079 
06080     def _run_startup(self, stage, action):
06081         """Run one startup step under the absolute deadline of the round.
06082 
06083         **Every** exit of this method waits for the **real** exit of the
06084         thread of the step: the success, the timeout, the ordinary
06085         failure, the cancellation and the ``SystemExit``.  The attempt
06086         of the step and the thread that performs it are two different
06087         facts and the owner of the step is released only after the real
06088         exit of its thread.
06089 
06090         A thread that did not exit keeps its owner: it stays in the
06091         owners that are in flight and the round keeps the fact.  It is
06092         never released because the attempt ended and it is never given a
06093         new budget.
06094         """
06095         owner = _StartupOwner(name=stage, clock=self._clock,
06096                               deadline=self._deadline_mono,
06097                               context=self.context)
06098         self._startup_owner = owner
06099         self.context.register_inflight(owner)
06100         try:
06101             # The start of the step is **inside** the ownership of the
06102             # owner and inside the ``finally`` below: a start that did
06103             # not return is coordinated like every other exit and the
06104             # owner is never left in the flight without a decision.
06105             owner.start(self._guarded_startup(action, stage))
06106             value = owner.await_result()
06107         except BrokerError as error:
06108             if error.code not in ('BROKER_STARTUP_DEADLINE_EXCEEDED',
06109                                   'BROKER_STARTUP_CANCELLED'):
06110                 # An ordinary failure of the step: the thread of the
06111                 # step is still coordinated in the finally below and the
06112                 # original failure is the one that leaves this method.
06113                 raise
06114             self.core.failures.append({'stage': stage, 'code': error.code})
06115             # The step did not finish before the deadline.  The owner
06116             # keeps its thread: a late result may only clean up.
06117             raise
06118         finally:
06119             # The budget of the settle is **recomputed after the await**
06120             # and immediately before the join: a deadline that was
06121             # published while the step was running still shortens the
06122             # wait and a settle budget that was copied before the await
06123             # is never used.  There is no positive floor beyond the
06124             # time that is really left.
06125             settle_budget = self._startup_settle_seconds()
06126             # The real exit of the thread is asked on **every** exit.
06127             try:
06128                 settled = owner.settle(settle_budget)
06129             except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
06130                 self.core.failures.append({'stage': stage,
06131                                            'error_type': type(error).__name__})
06132                 settled = False
06133             if not settled:
06134                 self._startup_in_flight = owner
06135                 self.core.failures.append({'stage': stage,
06136                                            'code': 'BROKER_STARTUP_IN_FLIGHT_RETAINED'})
06137             else:
06138                 self.context.release_inflight(owner)
06139         return value
06140 
06141     def _startup_settle_seconds(self):
06142         """The bounded wait for the real exit of a startup thread.
06143 
06144         The wait is bounded by the **remaining** of the absolute
06145         deadline of the round and by the settle bound of the package.
06146         It never publishes the cleanup deadline of the round - the
06147         cleanup of a round that did not fail keeps its whole budget -
06148         and it never opens a budget of its own.
06149 
06150         The remaining is recomputed on every call and it is never
06151         rounded **up**: a settle budget that is larger than the time
06152         that is really left would wait past the deadline of the round.
06153         The published cleanup deadline of the round shortens it when the
06154         round already entered its cleanup.
06155         """
06156         remaining = self.context.remaining_absolute()
06157         cleanup = self.context.cleanup_deadline_value()
06158         if cleanup is not None:
06159             left = max(0.0, cleanup - self._clock())
06160             remaining = left if remaining is None else min(remaining, left)
06161         if remaining is None:
06162             return STARTUP_SETTLE_SECONDS
06163         return max(0.0, min(remaining, STARTUP_SETTLE_SECONDS))
06164 
06165     # -- the run -----------------------------------------------------------
06166     def run(self):
06167         """Run the round and always release what the round owns.
06168 
06169         The loop has a ``finally`` that does not depend on the diagnosis
06170         of the round and not on the success of a write: a round that
06171         could not write its first reply still releases the job and the
06172         handles that it created.
06173         """
06174         self._fix_deadline()
06175         # The core and the loop read the same clock: the deadlines of
06176         # the round and the ONE cleanup deadline are in one domain.
06177         binder_clock = getattr(self.core, 'bind_clock', None)
06178         if callable(binder_clock):
06179             binder_clock(self._clock)
06180         self.core.bind_absolute_deadline(self._deadline_mono)
06181         binder = getattr(self.transport, 'bind_deadline', None)
06182         if callable(binder):
06183             # The transport of the channel is bound to the deadline of
06184             # the round before the job of the round exists: a read or a
06185             # write of the channel can never outlive the round.
06186             binder(self._deadline_mono)
06187         try:
06188             try:
06189                 self._run_startup('ARM', self.core.arm)
06190             except BrokerError as error:
06191                 self._reply('REFUSED', self.core.state, {'code': error.code},
06192                             terminal=True)
06193                 self._write_receipt('ARM_FAILED')
06194                 return 3
06195             self._reply('READY', self.core.state, {'broker': self.core.broker_identity})
06196             try:
06197                 self._run_startup('LAUNCH', self.core.launch)
06198             except BrokerError as error:
06199                 self._reply('REFUSED', self.core.state, {'code': error.code},
06200                             terminal=True)
06201                 self._write_receipt('LAUNCH_FAILED')
06202                 return 3
06203             self._reply('STATUS', self.core.state, self.core.summary())
06204             exit_code = self._loop()
06205             self._write_receipt(self.core.stop_reason)
06206             return exit_code
06207         finally:
06208             self._ensure_stopped()
06209 
06210     def _ensure_stopped(self):
06211         """Release the resources of the round, whatever happened before.
06212 
06213         The release never raises: a failure of the release is kept by the
06214         core and it never replaces the first failure of the round.
06215         """
06216         if self._stopped:
06217             return
06218         self._stopped = True
06219         if self._startup_in_flight is not None:
06220             # A startup step is still running: the core belongs to the
06221             # in-flight owner and the round keeps the fact.  The cleanup
06222             # of the core would race with the step that is still inside
06223             # the operating system, so it is not started here.
06224             self.core.failures.append({'stage': 'loop_stop',
06225                                        'code': 'BROKER_STARTUP_IN_FLIGHT_RETAINED'})
06226             self.core.state = 'UNKNOWN_RETAINED'
06227             return
06228         try:
06229             self.core.stop(self.core.stop_reason or 'LOOP_EXIT', force_unknown=True)
06230         except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
06231             self.core.failures.append({'stage': 'loop_stop',
06232                                         'error_type': type(error).__name__})
06233             self.core.state = 'UNKNOWN_RETAINED'
06234 
06235     def _context_remaining(self):
06236         """The seconds that are left until the current bound of the round.
06237 
06238         The value is read **again** at every call: the absolute deadline
06239         of the round and the **published** cleanup deadline of the round
06240         are one minimum and the loop never keeps a copy of it.  ``None``
06241         means that the round did not bind a deadline at all and that the
06242         lease of the loop is the only bound.
06243         """
06244         context = self.context
06245         if context is None:
06246             return None
06247         value = context.effective_deadline()
06248         if value is None:
06249             return None
06250         clock = getattr(context, 'clock', None) or self._clock
06251         return max(0.0, value - clock())
06252 
06253     def _loop(self):
06254         self._last_frame_wall = self._wall()
06255         while True:
06256             now = self._clock()
06257             if now >= self._deadline_mono:
06258                 return self._stop('LEASE_EXPIRED', 4)
06259             if self.core.overflowed():
06260                 return self._stop('BOUND_EXCEEDED', 4)
06261             # The read of the transport is bounded by the time that is
06262             # really left until the absolute deadline of the round.
06263             # There is **no positive floor** beyond it: a round whose
06264             # deadline arrived asks for nothing and the loop decides
06265             # the lease that arrived.
06266             remaining = self._deadline_mono - now
06267             if remaining <= 0:
06268                 return self._stop('LEASE_EXPIRED', 4)
06269             # The **current** minimum of the original context of the
06270             # round is part of the bound of the next read: the absolute
06271             # deadline and the **published** cleanup deadline of the
06272             # round are read again at the moment of the read and the
06273             # cached lease of the loop is never the only authority.
06274             current = self._context_remaining()
06275             if current is not None:
06276                 if current <= 0:
06277                     return self._stop('LEASE_EXPIRED', 4)
06278                 if current < remaining:
06279                     remaining = current
06280             timeout = min(POLL_SECONDS, remaining)
06281             try:
06282                 raw = self.transport.read(timeout)
06283             except BrokerError as error:
06284                 if error.code == 'BROKER_CHANNEL_TIMEOUT':
06285                     # The bound of the round arrived while the read was
06286                     # admitted: the lease of the round is what expired
06287                     # and it is not a failure of the channel.
06288                     return self._stop('LEASE_EXPIRED', 4)
06289                 return self._stop('CHANNEL_ERROR', 4)
06290             if raw is None:
06291                 # A missing heartbeat is a secondary signal.  The absolute
06292                 # deadline stays the authority of the round.
06293                 if self._wall() - self._last_frame_wall > HEARTBEAT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS:
06294                     return self._stop('HEARTBEAT_MISSING', 4)
06295                 continue
06296             if raw == b'':
06297                 return self._stop('CHANNEL_EOF', 4)
06298             # The hard cap of the channel is checked before the frame is
06299             # decoded: a frame that does not fit at all is refused
06300             # without being accepted.
06301             if self._frames + self._sent_frames + 1 > MAX_FRAMES:
06302                 return self._stop('FRAME_BUDGET_EXCEEDED', 4)
06303             try:
06304                 frame = decode_command(raw, run_id=self.plan.run_id,
06305                                        candidate_sha=self.plan.candidate_sha,
06306                                        authorization_sha256=self.plan.authorization_sha256,
06307                                        nonce=self.nonce, last_sequence=self._last_sequence)
06308             except CustodyError as error:
06309                 self.core.failures.append({'stage': 'channel', 'code': error.code})
06310                 return self._stop('CHANNEL_REFUSED', 4)
06311             # The reservation of the terminal exchange is **consumed** by
06312             # the terminal frame itself.  A STOP that arrives near the
06313             # limit of the budget is a legal frame: it is never refused
06314             # because of the reservation that was kept for it.  A frame
06315             # that is not terminal never borrows the reservation and it
06316             # fails closed when the total does not fit.
06317             terminal = frame['command'] == 'STOP'
06318             try:
06319                 self._check_frame_budget(0 if terminal else RESERVED_TERMINAL_FRAMES)
06320             except BrokerError:
06321                 return self._stop('FRAME_BUDGET_EXCEEDED', 4)
06322             self._frames += 1
06323             if self._frames > self._max_frames:
06324                 return self._stop('FRAME_COUNT_EXCEEDED', 4)
06325             # Only a frame that was decoded and that carries the identity
06326             # of the round refreshes the heartbeat of the round.  A frame
06327             # that was refused never does.
06328             self._last_frame_wall = self._wall()
06329             self._last_alive_wall = self._last_frame_wall
06330             self._last_sequence = frame['sequence']
06331             if frame['command'] == 'STATUS':
06332                 self._reply('STATUS', self.core.state, self.core.summary())
06333                 continue
06334             return self._stop('STOP_COMMAND', 0)
06335 
06336     def _stop(self, reason, exit_code):
06337         unknown = reason != 'STOP_COMMAND'
06338         self.core.stop(reason, force_unknown=unknown)
06339         self._reply('STOPPED', self.core.state, self.core.summary(), terminal=True)
06340         return exit_code
06341 
06342     def _write_receipt(self, reason):
06343         """Write the terminal receipt of the round into the run root.
06344 
06345         The receipt is the evidence that survives a parent that died.  It
06346         is written once, it is bounded and it is never replaced by a
06347         successful claim.  A failure of the write never replaces the
06348         first failure of the round.
06349         """
06350         evidence = self.core.evidence()
06351         evidence['reason'] = reason
06352         evidence['channel'] = {'frames_received': self._frames,
06353                                'last_sequence_received': self._last_sequence,
06354                                'frame_limit': self._max_frames}
06355         evidence['deadline_wall'] = self._deadline_wall
06356         evidence['deadline_extended'] = False
06357         raw = json.dumps(evidence, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True,
06358                          indent=2).encode('utf-8')
06359         if len(raw) > MAX_RECEIPT_BYTES:
06360             raw = json.dumps({'kind': 'lvw.s3-native-broker-receipt/1',
06361                               'reason': reason, 'truncated': True,
06362                               'stage_pass_claim': False}).encode('utf-8')
06363         path = Path(self.core.run_root) / 'temp' / 'native' / 'custody-terminal.json'
06364         try:
06365             path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
06366             path.write_bytes(raw)
06367         except OSError:
06368             return False
06369         return True
06370 
06371 
06372 # -- the fixed entry of the broker -----------------------------------------
06373 
06374 def build_broker_command(*, plan_path, plan_sha256, run_root, nonce, python=None):
06375     """The fixed command of the broker.
06376 
06377     The command starts exactly this broker with one sealed plan document.
06378     There is no dynamic import of a caller supplied module and no arbitrary
06379     argument vector: the only arguments are the plan, its digest, the run
06380     root of the round and the nonce of the session.
06381     """
06382     script = Path(__file__).resolve()
06383     if not script.is_file():
06384         raise BrokerError('BROKER_SCRIPT_MISSING')
06385     if not re.fullmatch(r'[0-9a-f]{64}', str(plan_sha256)):
06386         raise BrokerError('BROKER_PLAN_DIGEST_INVALID')
06387     if not re.fullmatch(r'[0-9a-f]{32}', str(nonce)):
06388         raise BrokerError('BROKER_NONCE_INVALID')
06389     executable = python or sys.executable
06390     return [executable, '-I', '-B', str(script),
06391             '--plan', str(plan_path), '--plan-sha256', str(plan_sha256),
06392             '--run-root', str(run_root), '--nonce', str(nonce)]
06393 
06394 
06395 class BrokerProcess:
06396     """The original handle of the broker process of one round.
06397 
06398     The parent keeps the process handle, the pipes of the private channel
06399     and the identity of the executable.  The replies of the broker are
06400     read through one bounded background reader, so the parent never blocks
06401     on the channel and the deadline of the round is never held up by a
06402     silent broker.
06403 
06404     The handle is **deadline aware**: it says so with its own marker and
06405     it takes the absolute deadline of every write.  A handle that is not
06406     deadline aware is never assumed to be deadline aware by the parent.
06407     """
06408 
06409     #: The marker of a handle that understands an absolute deadline.
06410     channel_deadline_aware = True
06411 
06412     def __init__(self, process, executable, executable_sha256, channel,
06413                  clock=None, context=None):
06414         self._process = process
06415         self._channel = channel
06416         self._clock = clock or time.monotonic
06417         # The ONE context of the launch.  The close of the broker reads
06418         # the absolute deadline and the published cleanup deadline of
06419         # the round from it and it never opens a budget of its own.
06420         self._context = context
06421         self.pid = process.pid
06422         self.executable = executable
06423         self.executable_sha256 = executable_sha256
06424         # Whether the reader of the channel really stopped when the channel
06425         # was closed.  It is ``None`` until the close happened and it stays
06426         # ``False`` when the reader was still alive after the bounded join.
06427         self.reader_finished = None
06428         # The absolute deadline of the round, handed over by the parent.
06429         # The parent may only shorten it.
06430         self.write_deadline = None
06431         self.close_facts = None
06432 
06433     def bind_channel_deadline(self, deadline):
06434         """Bind the absolute deadline of the round to the private channel.
06435 
06436         The deadline is a finite number of seconds of the monotonic clock
06437         and it may only shorten the deadline that is already bound: a
06438         later deadline is refused.
06439         """
06440         value = _require_deadline(deadline)
06441         if value is None:
06442             raise BrokerError('BROKER_DEADLINE_INVALID')
06443         if self.write_deadline is not None and value > self.write_deadline:
06444             raise BrokerError('BROKER_DEADLINE_EXTENDED')
06445         self.write_deadline = value
06446         binder = getattr(self._channel, 'bind_deadline', None)
06447         if callable(binder):
06448             binder(value)
06449         return value
06450 
06451     def write(self, raw, deadline=None):
06452         """Write one frame of the parent, bounded by the deadline.
06453 
06454         The deadline is explicit and it is checked: a write that is not
06455         bounded by an absolute deadline would let a broker that never
06456         reads hold the supervisor of the round forever.
06457         """
06458         value = _require_deadline(deadline)
06459         if value is None:
06460             value = self.write_deadline
06461         if value is None:
06462             raise BrokerError('BROKER_DEADLINE_MISSING')
06463         if self.write_deadline is not None and value > self.write_deadline:
06464             raise BrokerError('BROKER_DEADLINE_EXTENDED')
06465         return self._channel.write(raw, value)
06466 
06467     def read(self, timeout):
06468         return self._channel.read(timeout)
06469 
06470     def close(self, deadline=None):
06471         """Close the original handle of the broker.
06472 
06473         The close happens even when the exchange of the stop failed: the
06474         resource of this round is released either way.
06475 
06476         The facts of the close are returned and they are named: a handle
06477         that was not closed, a reader that did not stop and a process that
06478         did not exit are facts of the round and they are never hidden
06479         behind a success.  The release of the original process handle is
06480         explicit and it is never left to the garbage collector.
06481 
06482         The waits of the close are bounded by **one** absolute deadline:
06483         the deadline that the parent bound before it closed the handle.
06484         The remaining time is recomputed before every wait, so that a
06485         wait that already consumed part of the budget never gets the
06486         whole budget again and no layer of the cleanup adds a budget of
06487         its own.
06488 
06489         The close may be **repeated** inside the same deadline: an item
06490         that was confirmed is never closed a second time and an item
06491         that was not confirmed is coordinated again.  The attempt that
06492         ended is not a release: only the facts of the close prove it.
06493 
06494         The error of the reader of the channel is a **history** of the
06495         round and not a fact of the last attempt: a repeated close keeps
06496         the first error that the reader really had, it publishes the
06497         source of that error and it never turns a real error into a
06498         ``None``.  The resource facts of the close may converge while
06499         the error history of the reader stays: a close that returned is
06500         not a release and a retry is not an amnesty.
06501 
06502         The **endpoint is the only owner** of the files of the channel.
06503         The broker process therefore never closes the standard input and
06504         the standard output of the process by itself: it closes the
06505         channel, and the channel closes the files that it owns.
06506         """
06507         value = self._cleanup_deadline(deadline)
06508         budget = max(0.0, value - self._clock())
06509         facts = {'channel_closed': False,
06510                  'channel_reader_finished': None,
06511                  'channel_reader_error': None,
06512                  'channel_reader_error_source': None,
06513                  'channel_reader_secondary_errors': [],
06514                  'channel_overflow': False,
06515                  'channel_overflow_facts': {},
06516                  'close_cancellation': None,
06517                  'channel_retained_workers': 0,
06518                  'write_end_closed': False,
06519                  'read_end_closed': False,
06520                  'stdin_closed': False,
06521                  'stdout_closed': False,
06522                  'process_exited': False,
06523                  'exit_code': None,
06524                  'terminated': False,
06525                  'process_handle_released': False,
06526                  'process_handle_release_method': None,
06527                  'worker_thread_handle_released': False,
06528                  'cleanup_budget_seconds': budget,
06529                  'cleanup_deadline': value}
06530         # The facts of a previous attempt are kept: an item that was
06531         # confirmed is never closed a second time and its fact stays
06532         # true.
06533         previous = self.close_facts if isinstance(self.close_facts, dict) else {}
06534         for key, confirmed in previous.items():
06535             if key in ('cleanup_budget_seconds', 'cleanup_deadline'):
06536                 continue
06537             if (confirmed is True
06538                     or (key == 'exit_code' and confirmed is not None)
06539                     or key == 'process_handle_release_method'
06540                     or key == 'close_cancellation'):
06541                 facts[key] = confirmed
06542         # The channel and its endpoints use the very same absolute
06543         # deadline: they never start a wait of their own.
06544         binder = getattr(self._channel, 'bind_deadline', None)
06545         if callable(binder):
06546             try:
06547                 binder(value)
06548             except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
06549                 pass
06550         if not facts['channel_closed']:
06551             channel_facts = self._close_channel(facts)
06552         elif not self._channel_complete(facts):
06553             # A previous attempt already called the close of the
06554             # channel, but it did not confirm **every** item of it: the
06555             # read end, the worker of an endpoint and the original
06556             # handle of its thread may still be open.  The items that
06557             # were not confirmed are coordinated again, inside the very
06558             # same deadline.  A close that returned is not a release.
06559             channel_facts = self._close_channel(facts)
06560         else:
06561             channel_facts = None
06562         if channel_facts is not None:
06563             facts.update(channel_facts)
06564         # The error of the reader of the channel is a **history** of the
06565         # round: a repeated close never turns a real error of the reader
06566         # into a ``None``.  The first error is the error of the round and
06567         # a later error is kept beside it as a secondary one.
06568         self._keep_reader_error_history(facts, previous)
06569         # The files of the channel belong to the endpoints of the
06570         # channel.  The fact of their close is the fact of the channel.
06571         facts['stdin_closed'] = facts['write_end_closed']
06572         facts['stdout_closed'] = facts['read_end_closed']
06573         if not facts['process_exited']:
06574             try:
06575                 # The bound of the cleanup is read **again** here: the
06576                 # close of the channel may have published a shorter
06577                 # cleanup deadline of the round and the wait of the
06578                 # process receives the time that is really left.
06579                 current = self._cleanup_deadline(deadline)
06580                 self._process.wait(timeout=max(0.0, current - self._clock()))
06581                 facts['process_exited'] = True
06582                 facts['exit_code'] = self._process.returncode
06583             except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
06584                 # Only the broker that this parent holds is terminated, and
06585                 # its exit is confirmed afterwards with a bounded wait.
06586                 try:
06587                     self._process.terminate()
06588                     facts['terminated'] = True
