08061     def channel_factory():
08062         """Build the channel of this launch with the context of the round.
08063 
08064         The channel registers itself with the **aggregate** of the
08065         launch before it builds anything: a channel whose reader
08066         thread did not start is found by the outer owner through the
08067         aggregate and the files of the process are never closed by the
08068         process helper.
08069 
08070         The admission of the round is asked **again** immediately
08071         before the channel is created: a stop that was published while
08072         the hash of the executable was paused refuses the channel and
08073         the process of the round keeps its owner.
08074         """
08075         _check_launch_budget(context, 'LAUNCH:BEFORE_CHANNEL')
08076         channel = StreamChannel(process.stdout, process.stdin,
08077                                 custody=launch, context=context)
08078         if deadline is not None:
08079             channel.bind_deadline(deadline)
08080         return channel
08081 
08082     return _adopt_broker_process(
08083         process,
08084         channel_factory=channel_factory,
08085         executable=str(executable), executable_sha256=executable_sha256,
08086         deadline=deadline, aggregate=launch)
08087 
08088 
08089 def read_sealed_plan(path, digest):
08090     """Read one sealed plan document and check its digest."""
08091     target = Path(path)
08092     if not target.is_file():
08093         raise BrokerError('BROKER_PLAN_MISSING')
08094     with target.open('rb') as stream:
08095         raw = stream.read(MAX_PLAN_BYTES + 1)
08096     if len(raw) > MAX_PLAN_BYTES:
08097         raise BrokerError('BROKER_PLAN_TOO_LARGE')
08098     if hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest() != digest:
08099         raise BrokerError('BROKER_PLAN_DIGEST_DRIFT')
08100     try:
08101         document = json.loads(raw.decode('utf-8'))
08102     except (UnicodeDecodeError, ValueError) as error:
08103         raise BrokerError('BROKER_PLAN_INVALID') from error
08104     return document
08105 
08106 
08107 def main(argv=None):
08108     """The production entry of the broker.
08109 
08110     The entry is fixed: one sealed plan, its digest, the run root of the
08111     round and the nonce of the session.  The plan is validated again here,
08112     because the broker never trusts a document that it did not check.
08113     """
08114     import argparse
08115     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
08116     parser.add_argument('--plan', required=True)
08117     parser.add_argument('--plan-sha256', required=True)
08118     parser.add_argument('--run-root', required=True)
08119     parser.add_argument('--nonce', required=True)
08120     arguments = parser.parse_args(argv)
08121     if not re.fullmatch(r'[0-9a-f]{32}', str(arguments.nonce)):
08122         sys.stderr.write('BROKER_REFUSED BROKER_NONCE_INVALID\n')
08123         sys.stderr.flush()
08124         return 2
08125     document = read_sealed_plan(arguments.plan, arguments.plan_sha256)
08126     try:
08127         plan = validate_launch_plan(document, now=time.time())
08128     except CustodyError as error:
08129         sys.stderr.write('BROKER_REFUSED ' + error.code + '\n')
08130         sys.stderr.flush()
08131         return 2
08132     if plan.sealed_sha256() != arguments.plan_sha256:
08133         # The seal of the plan that the broker validated is not the seal
08134         # that the parent wrote: the document drifted on its way here.
08135         sys.stderr.write('BROKER_REFUSED BROKER_PLAN_SEAL_DRIFT\n')
08136         sys.stderr.flush()
08137         return 2
08138     try:
08139         win32 = WindowsApi()
08140     except BrokerError as error:
08141         sys.stderr.write('BROKER_REFUSED ' + error.code + '\n')
08142         sys.stderr.flush()
08143         return 2
08144     core = BrokerCore(win32=win32, plan=plan, run_root=arguments.run_root)
08145     loop = BrokerLoop(core=core, transport=None, plan=plan,
08146                       nonce=arguments.nonce)
08147     # The **absolute deadline** of the round is fixed and bound to the
08148     # core **before** the transport of the round exists.  The reader of
08149     # the channel and the workers of the endpoints therefore never
08150     # start with an unbound context: the child has no window in which
08151     # a read of the channel runs without a deadline.
08152     deadline = loop.fix_deadline()
08153     core.bind_absolute_deadline(deadline)
08154     # The ONE context of the round is handed to the transport of the
08155     # channel and to its endpoints: the channel, the endpoints and the
08156     # workers of the endpoints read the same absolute deadline and none
08157     # of them keeps a budget of its own.
08158     transport = StreamChannel(sys.stdin.buffer, sys.stdout.buffer, api=win32,
08159                               context=core.context)
08160     transport.bind_deadline(deadline)
08161     loop.bind_transport(transport)
08162     try:
08163         return loop.run()
08164     finally:
08165         # The round releases its own resources in the loop; the entry only
08166         # closes the channel and never swallows a failure of the round.
08167         try:
08168             transport.close()
08169         except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
08170             pass
08171 
08172 
08173 if __name__ == '__main__':
08174     sys.exit(main())

### 206a7fe7f21343d89ca83e03a0e4dc7e670cd381:tools/v0_s3_native_custody.py
00001 """Parent-side native custody: the closed launch plan and the bounded session.
00002 
00003 This module is the parent internal half of the N1 native custody foundation.
00004 It owns exactly two things.
00005 
00006 * The **closed launch plan**.  It is a small, exactly keyed document that
00007   names the tools of one round, the absolute lease of the round and the
00008   finite role set.  The plan never carries an argument vector, a shell, a
00009   script, an environment or a PID.  The command form of a role is frozen in
00010   the broker and assembled from the already authorised tools; the plan only
00011   says *which* tools and *which* closed data.
00012 
00013 * The **bounded custody session**.  It is the parent side handle of one
00014   broker process, the private control channel to that broker and the
00015   absolute deadline of the round.
00016 
00017 Three facts are deliberate and they are repeated in every evidence that
00018 this module produces.
00019 
00020 * The hash of the plan proves that the plan did not change.  It does not
00021   prove that a grant is real.  The grant authority of this package is
00022   ``NOT_VERIFIED`` and this package produces no admission evidence at all.
00023 
00024 * The parent never stops a native service by name, by PID or by a record
00025   that somebody else wrote.  The parent stops only what it created: the
00026   broker process of this round, through its own original handle and through
00027   the private channel.  The broker, in turn, stops only what it created:
00028   the processes of this round, through their own original handles and
00029   through the job object of this round.
00030 
00031 * Nothing here is a proof of a native execution.  The Windows path of this
00032   package has never run on Windows, so every native claim of its evidence
00033   stays ``NOT_PROVEN``.
00034 
00035 The session owns the resources of one round from the moment they are
00036 created until they are released, and it never leaves a resource of the
00037 round to the garbage collector:
00038 
00039 * the plan is validated once, deep copied and presented as a copy, so
00040   that a caller that changes the presented copy changes nothing of the
00041   command that the round will really run;
00042 
00043 * every failure of the start of the round closes the original handle of
00044   the broker inside the method that failed, whether the failure was a
00045   refusal of the broker, a timeout, a parse error, an ordinary exception
00046   or a cancellation;
00047 
00048 * the read side of the private channel has exactly one owner, a bounded
00049   pump, and the round keeps a status heartbeat alive on its own, so that
00050   a long round is not stopped for silence and so that a heartbeat never
00051   extends the absolute deadline of the round.
00052 
00053 The close of the round is coordinated by **one** owner and **one**
00054 absolute deadline.
00055 
00056 * the first caller of the close owns it and every other caller waits
00057   for the close that is in flight inside that very deadline, so that a
00058   caller never returns a success before the cleanup could finish;
00059 
00060 * the end of the attempt of the close is published in a ``finally`` and
00061   the state of the close is explicit: ``NOT_STARTED``, ``IN_PROGRESS``
00062   or ``FINISHED``.  ``FINISHED`` means that the attempt ended and it
00063   never means that the resources of the round are released: only the
00064   facts of the close prove that;
00065 
00066 * no background thread of the round starts after the close began: the
00067   permission to start one is closed before the close takes its snapshot
00068   of the threads, and the stop event of the heartbeat is never rebuilt.
00069 """
00070 import copy
00071 import hashlib
00072 import inspect
00073 import json
00074 import math
00075 import os
00076 import re
00077 import threading
00078 import time
00079 from pathlib import Path
00080 from types import MappingProxyType
00081 
00082 PLAN_SCHEMA = 'lvw.s3-native-custody-plan/1'
00083 CHANNEL_SCHEMA = 'lvw.s3-native-custody-channel/1'
00084 
00085 # The closed key sets.  A document with one key too many or one key too few
00086 # is refused; a plan is never repaired and never completed.
00087 PLAN_KEYS = frozenset({'schema', 'run_id', 'candidate_sha', 'authorization_sha256',
00088                        'lease_expires_at', 'max_runtime_seconds', 'roles'})
00089 ROLE_KEYS = frozenset({'role', 'tools', 'data', 'log'})
00090 TOOL_KEYS = frozenset({'path', 'sha256'})
00091 
00092 # The finite role set.  COMFY and OLLAMA are required, SAMPLER is optional.
00093 # The order of the roles is fixed: the sampler is created last, because it
00094 # observes the two services that this broker created.
00095 ROLE_ORDER = ('COMFY', 'OLLAMA', 'SAMPLER')
00096 REQUIRED_ROLES = ('COMFY', 'OLLAMA')
00097 ROLE_DATA_KEYS = {
00098     'COMFY': frozenset({'user_directory'}),
00099     'OLLAMA': frozenset(),
00100     'SAMPLER': frozenset({'nvidia_exe', 'nvidia_sha256', 'max_seconds'}),
00101 }
00102 # The number of tools of a role is fixed as well.  The first tool of a role
00103 # is its executable; the second one, where it exists, is the script that the
00104 # executable runs.  A role never grows a third tool by accident.
00105 ROLE_TOOL_COUNTS = {'COMFY': 2, 'OLLAMA': 1, 'SAMPLER': 2}
00106 
00107 # The bounded limits of the round.  They are internal limits of this
00108 # foundation and they are not a claim about any native Windows behaviour.
00109 MAX_RUNTIME_SECONDS = 21600
00110 MAX_FRAME_BYTES = 32 * 1024
00111 MAX_FRAMES = 1024
00112 MAX_STREAM_BYTES = 256 * 1024
00113 MAX_NEW_TEXT_BYTES = 8 * 1024 * 1024
00114 MAX_DESCENDANTS = 17

00116 
00117 # The bounded waits of the parent side.  The heartbeat keeps a round alive
00118 # while the round runs; it never moves the absolute deadline of the round.
00119 # The cleanup of a stop has its own short budget and never borrows the
00120 # remaining runtime of the round: the stop and the cleanup share ONE
00121 # budget and no layer adds a budget of its own.
00122 CHANNEL_POLL_SECONDS = 0.25
00123 HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL_SECONDS = 60.0
00124 HEARTBEAT_MISSING_SECONDS = 120.0
00125 HEARTBEAT_SLICE_SECONDS = 0.25
00126 STOP_CLEANUP_SECONDS = 5.0
00127 WRITE_BUDGET_SECONDS = 5.0
00128 THREAD_JOIN_SECONDS = 2.0
00129 # The number of the frames of the channel that is reserved for the
00130 # terminal exchange of the round.  The heartbeat may not spend them: a
00131 # round that reached its frame budget fails closed and it never
00132 # reconnects and it never renews its lease.
00133 RESERVED_TERMINAL_FRAMES = 8
00134 
00135 # The closed command set of the private channel and the closed reply kinds.
00136 COMMANDS = ('STATUS', 'STOP')
00137 REPLIES = ('READY', 'STATUS', 'STOPPED', 'REFUSED')
00138 
00139 # The state machine of one round.  ARMED means that the job object of the
00140 # round exists; RUNNING means that the roles of the round were created,
00141 # assigned to that job and resumed.  UNKNOWN_RETAINED is a terminal state
00142 # that keeps the evidence of what could not be proven.
00143 STATES = ('NEW', 'VALIDATED', 'ARMED', 'RUNNING', 'STOPPING', 'RELEASED',
00144           'UNKNOWN_RETAINED')
00145 
00146 COMMAND_KEYS = frozenset({'schema', 'command', 'run_id', 'candidate_sha',
00147                           'authorization_sha256', 'nonce', 'sequence'})
00148 REPLY_KEYS = frozenset({'schema', 'kind', 'run_id', 'candidate_sha',
00149                         'authorization_sha256', 'nonce', 'sequence', 'state', 'detail'})
00150 
00151 # The nonce of the private channel.  The parent and the broker agree on
00152 # one closed form: 32 hexadecimal characters.  A nonce identifies the
00153 # channel of one round; it grants no custody over any process.
00154 NONCE_PATTERN = r'[0-9a-f]{32}'
00155 NONCE_BYTES = 16
00156 
00157 # The facts that the close of the broker has to report before the
00158 # parent may forget the original reference of the broker.  Every one of
00159 # them has to be present in the facts of the close and it has to be
00160 # exactly ``True``.  A missing fact, a ``None``, a ``False`` and an
00161 # unknown value are all unknown: the original broker is kept and the
00162 # round stays unknown.
00163 #
00164 # The list is **strict**.  The channel, the process and its original
00165 # handle are not enough: the reader of the channel has to have really
00166 # stopped without an error, the two ends of the channel have to be
00167 # closed and the original handles of the workers of the endpoints have
00168 # to be released.  A close that proves only three facts does not prove
00169 # that the resources of the round are released.
00170 REQUIRED_CLOSE_FACTS = ('channel_closed', 'process_exited',
00171                         'process_handle_released', 'channel_reader_finished',
00172                         'read_end_closed', 'write_end_closed',
00173                         'worker_thread_handle_released')
00174 # The facts of the close that have to be present and that have to be
00175 # zero.  A missing fact is a missing fact and not a zero.
00176 REQUIRED_CLOSE_ZERO_FACTS = ('channel_retained_workers',)
00177 # The facts of the reader of the channel.  They are facts of the close
00178 # and not of the round: they are checked when they are present.
00179 READER_CLOSE_FACTS = ('channel_reader_finished', 'reader_finished')
00180 
00181 # The three states of the close of one round.  NOT_STARTED is the state
00182 # of a round whose close never began.  IN_PROGRESS is the state of a
00183 # close that is running right now.  FINISHED is the state of a close
00184 # whose **attempt** ended: it never means that the resources of the
00185 # round are released, only the facts of the close prove that.
00186 CLOSE_STATES = ('NOT_STARTED', 'IN_PROGRESS', 'FINISHED')
00187 
00188 # The slice of the wait of a competitor of the close.  The wait is
00189 # sliced so that the shared deadline of the cleanup is honoured by the
00190 # clock of the round and not only by the clock of the host: a clock
00191 # that the round controls can make the deadline arrive at once.
00192 CLOSE_WAIT_SLICE_SECONDS = 0.05

00194 
00195 class CustodyError(RuntimeError):
00196     """A refused custody step with a stable, machine readable code."""
00197 
00198     def __init__(self, code, detail=None):
00199         super().__init__(code)
00200         self.code = code
00201         self.detail = detail
00202 
00203 
00204 # -- the one context of a round ---------------------------------------------
00205 
00206 class _CustodyContext:
00207     """The ONE finite startup and cleanup context of one round.
00208 
00209     The context holds the four things that every layer of the round has
00210     to share and that may never be copied from one layer into the next
00211     one.
00212 
00213     * The **clock** of the round, so that every deadline of the round is
00214       expressed in one domain.
00215 
00216     * The **absolute deadline** of the round.  It is fixed once and it
00217       may only be shortened.
00218 
00219     * The **one cleanup deadline**.  It is published **once**, by the
00220       first caller that enters the cleanup, and every later caller
00221       reuses that very value.  The publication is one atomic step under
00222       the lock of the context: two callers that entered the cleanup at
00223       the same moment always see the same number and the deadline is
00224       never generated twice.
00225 
00226     * The three **one way latches** of the round: the cancel, the stop
00227       and the unknown.  A latch that was set is never cleared, and a
00228       late event may never overwrite the unknown of the round.
00229 
00230     The session, the send of the round, the stop of the background
00231     threads and the close of the broker all hold this **one object**.
00232     A layer that only copied the number of a deadline would be a layer
00233     with a deadline of its own.
00234     """
00235 
00236     def __init__(self, *, clock=None, wall=None):
00237         self.clock = clock or time.monotonic
00238         self.wall = wall or time.time
00239         self._lock = threading.RLock()
00240         self._absolute_deadline = None
00241         self._cleanup_deadline = None
00242         # The one way latches of the round.
00243         self.cancel = threading.Event()
00244         self.stopping = threading.Event()
00245         self.unknown = threading.Event()
00246 
00247     # -- the deadlines ----------------------------------------------------
00248     def bind_absolute_deadline(self, deadline):
00249         """Bind the absolute deadline of the round.  It may only shorten."""
00250         if deadline is None:
00251             return self._absolute_deadline
00252         with self._lock:
00253             if self._absolute_deadline is None or deadline < self._absolute_deadline:
00254                 self._absolute_deadline = deadline
00255             return self._absolute_deadline
00256 
00257     @property
00258     def absolute_deadline(self):
00259         with self._lock:
00260             return self._absolute_deadline
00261 
00262     def cleanup_deadline_value(self):
00263         """The published cleanup deadline, or ``None`` before the first."""
00264         with self._lock:
00265             return self._cleanup_deadline
00266 
00267     def publish_cleanup_deadline(self, value):
00268         """Publish the ONE cleanup deadline of the round, atomically.
00269 
00270         The first caller publishes the value and every later caller
00271         receives the very same value.  A later caller may never extend
00272         the deadline of the cleanup and it may never replace it.
00273         """
00274         with self._lock:
00275             if self._cleanup_deadline is None:
00276                 self._cleanup_deadline = value
00277             return self._cleanup_deadline
00278 
00279     def remaining_cleanup(self):
00280         """The seconds that are left until the cleanup deadline.
00281 
00282         The value is recomputed on every call: a wait that already
00283         consumed part of the budget never receives the whole budget
00284         again.
00285         """
00286         with self._lock:
00287             value = self._cleanup_deadline
00288         if value is None:
00289             return 0.0
00290         return max(0.0, value - self.clock())
00291 
00292     def effective_deadline(self, call=None, bound=None):
00293         """The ONE deadline that a call of a layer really uses.
00294 
00295         The deadline of the call, the deadline that is bound to the
00296         layer, the absolute deadline of the round and the **published**
00297         cleanup deadline of the round are one value: the earliest of the
00298         four.
00299 
00300         The published cleanup deadline is part of it: a round that
00301         entered its cleanup publishes one deadline and every layer that
00302         reads the context afterwards is bound by it.
00303 
00304         The value is **recomputed on every call**: a deadline that was
00305         published after the call started still shortens the call that is
00306         running.
00307         """
00308         value = call
00309         if value is not None and not isinstance(value, (int, float)):
00310             value = None
00311         for candidate in (bound, self.absolute_deadline,
00312                           self.cleanup_deadline_value()):
00313             if candidate is None:
00314                 continue
00315             if value is None or candidate < value:
00316                 value = candidate
00317         return value
00318 
00319     # -- the one way latches ----------------------------------------------
00320     def request_cancel(self):
00321         """Ask for the cancellation of the round.  One way."""
00322         self.cancel.set()
00323 
00324     def request_stop(self):
00325         """Ask for the stop of the round.  One way."""
00326         self.stopping.set()
00327 
00328     def latch_unknown(self):
00329         """Latch the unknown state of the round.  One way."""
00330         if self.unknown.is_set():
00331             return False
00332         self.unknown.set()
00333         return True
00334 
00335     def is_unknown(self):
00336         """Whether the unknown state of the round was latched."""
00337         return self.unknown.is_set()
00338 
00339 
00340 # -- the closed plan -------------------------------------------------------
00341 
00342 _WINDOWS_ABSOLUTE = re.compile(r'^(?:[A-Za-z]:[\\/]|\\\\)[^\x00]+$')
00343 _LOG_REFERENCE = re.compile(r'[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._/-]{0,254}')
00344 _DIRECTORY_NAME = re.compile(r'[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]{0,63}')
00345 DIRECTORY_NAME_MAX_LENGTH = 64
00346 # The device names of Windows.  A directory of the round may not carry
00347 # one of them, with or without an extension.
00348 WINDOWS_DEVICE_NAMES = frozenset(
00349     ['CON', 'PRN', 'AUX', 'NUL']
00350     + ['COM%d' % index for index in range(1, 10)]
00351     + ['LPT%d' % index for index in range(1, 10)])
00352 
00353 
00354 def _require_text(value, pattern, code):
00355     if not isinstance(value, str) or not re.fullmatch(pattern, value):
00356         raise CustodyError(code)
00357     return value
00358 
00359 
00360 def _require_exact_int(value, code, *, minimum=None, maximum=None):
00361     """An exact integer.
00362 
00363     A boolean is not an integer here and a float is not an integer either:
00364     ``True`` would silently become ``1`` and ``1.5`` would silently become
00365     ``1``, and both would hide a malformed plan.
00366     """
00367     if type(value) is not int:
00368         raise CustodyError(code)
00369     if minimum is not None and value < minimum:
00370         raise CustodyError(code)
00371     if maximum is not None and value > maximum:
00372         raise CustodyError(code)
00373     return value
00374 
00375 
00376 def _require_number(value, code):
00377     if isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, (int, float)):
00378         raise CustodyError(code)
00379     if not math.isfinite(value):
00380         raise CustodyError(code)
00381     return value
00382 
00383 
00384 def _windows_parts(path):
00385     return [part for part in re.split(r'[\\/]+', path) if part not in ('', '.')]
00386 
00387 
00388 def _require_absolute_windows_path(value, code):
00389     if not isinstance(value, str) or not _WINDOWS_ABSOLUTE.match(value):
00390         raise CustodyError(code)
00391     if '..' in _windows_parts(value):
00392         raise CustodyError(code)
00393     if len(value) > 512:
00394         raise CustodyError(code)
00395     return value
00396 
00397 
00398 def _require_log_reference(value):
00399     """A relative reference of a log of this run, below the run root.
00400 
00401     The reference is judged with the semantics of a **Windows** path and
00402     not with the semantics of the host that runs the check.  The package
00403     is validated on a POSIX host and the round runs on Windows, so a check
00404     that used the rules of the host would accept a reference that the
00405     broker of the round resolves somewhere else.  ``pathlib`` is not used
00406     here for the same reason: ``Path('C:/Windows/temp/x.log').is_absolute()``
00407     is ``False`` on Linux and ``True`` on Windows.
00408 
00409     A reference is refused when it is
00410 
00411     * empty, not a string or longer than the closed limit;
00412     * absolute in the POSIX sense (``/var/log/comfy.log``);
00413     * absolute in the Windows sense (``C:/Windows/temp/comfy.log``);
00414     * relative to a drive (``C:comfy.log``);
00415     * a UNC reference or a mixed separator reference
00416       (``\\\\server\\share\\comfy.log``, ``temp\\native\\comfy.log``);
00417     * an alternate data stream (``temp/native/comfy.log:stream``);
00418     * a traversal (``../comfy.log``, ``temp/../comfy.log``);
00419     * a reference with an empty or a dot segment
00420       (``temp//comfy.log``, ``temp/./comfy.log``).
00421 
00422     A plain relative reference below the run root is accepted.  The
00423     refusal happens in ``validate_launch_plan``, before any job, any
00424     process and any log of the round exists, and the broker validates the
00425     plan again before it consumes it.
00426     """
00427     if not isinstance(value, str) or not value or len(value) > 255:
00428         raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_PLAN_LOG_INVALID')
00429     # A colon is the separator of a drive and the separator of an
00430     # alternate data stream.  Both are refused: the reference stays a
00431     # plain relative name of the run root.
00432     if ':' in value:
00433         raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_PLAN_LOG_INVALID')
00434     # A backslash is a path separator of Windows.  A UNC reference and a
00435     # reference with a mixed separator are refused with it.
00436     if '\\' in value:
00437         raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_PLAN_LOG_INVALID')
00438     # A leading slash is an absolute reference in POSIX and in Windows.
00439     if value.startswith('/'):
00440         raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_PLAN_LOG_INVALID')
00441     # The closed character set of the reference.
00442     if not _LOG_REFERENCE.fullmatch(value):
00443         raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_PLAN_LOG_INVALID')
00444     parts = value.split('/')
00445     if any(part in ('', '.', '..') for part in parts):
00446         raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_PLAN_LOG_INVALID')
00447     return value
00448 
00449 
00450 def _require_directory_name(value, code):
00451     """One controlled directory name and nothing else.
00452 
00453     The name of the user directory of the ComfyUI role is a **single
00454     segment** below the root of the round.  A prefix match is not a
00455     match: a name that starts with a legal name and continues with a
00456     separator would leave the root of the round, and the round would
00457     then write outside of the root that it sealed.
00458 
00459     The name is therefore refused when it is
00460 
00461     * not a string, empty or longer than 64 characters;
00462     * not exactly one segment of the closed character set;
00463     * a traversal (``..``) or a name that ends with a dot, because
00464       Windows would silently cut that dot and the directory of the plan
00465       would not be the directory that the round creates;
00466     * a device name of Windows (``NUL``, ``CON``, ``COM1`` ...), with
00467       or without an extension.
00468     """
00469     if not isinstance(value, str) or not value:
00470         raise CustodyError(code)
00471     if len(value) > DIRECTORY_NAME_MAX_LENGTH:
00472         raise CustodyError(code)
00473     if not _DIRECTORY_NAME.fullmatch(value):
00474         raise CustodyError(code)
00475     if '..' in value:
00476         raise CustodyError(code)
00477     if value.endswith('.') or value != value.strip():
00478         raise CustodyError(code)
00479     if value.split('.', 1)[0].upper() in WINDOWS_DEVICE_NAMES:
00480         raise CustodyError(code)
00481     return value
00482 
00483 
00484 def _validate_role_data(name, data):
00485     if name == 'COMFY':
00486         _require_directory_name(data['user_directory'],
00487                                 'CUSTODY_PLAN_ROLE_DATA_INVALID')
00488         return
00489     if name == 'SAMPLER':
00490         _require_absolute_windows_path(data['nvidia_exe'],
00491                                        'CUSTODY_PLAN_ROLE_DATA_INVALID')
00492         _require_text(data['nvidia_sha256'], r'[0-9a-f]{64}',
00493                       'CUSTODY_PLAN_ROLE_DATA_INVALID')
00494         _require_exact_int(data['max_seconds'], 'CUSTODY_PLAN_ROLE_DATA_INVALID',
00495                            minimum=1, maximum=MAX_SAMPLER_SECONDS)
00496 
00497 
00498 def _freeze(value):
00499     """A read-only view of one validated document.
00500 
00501     The view is what the command of a role is built from.  A caller that
00502     holds the view cannot change it, and a caller that holds the
00503     presented copy of the plan cannot change the view either.
00504     """
00505     if isinstance(value, dict):
00506         return MappingProxyType({key: _freeze(item) for key, item in value.items()})
00507     if isinstance(value, list):
00508         return tuple(_freeze(item) for item in value)
00509     return value
00510 
00511 
00512 class LaunchPlan:
00513     """The validated and sealed launch plan of one native custody round.
00514 
00515     The plan holds two things that cannot influence each other.
00516 
00517     * The **canonical** document: the validated document itself, deep
00518       copied once, at the moment of the validation.  Its normalized
00519       bytes and their digest are the seal of the plan, and the broker
00520       checks the very same bytes again when it consumes the plan.
00521 
00522     * The **read-only views** of the roles: the commands of the round
00523       are built from these views and never from a presented copy.
00524 
00525     The sealed form that is handed to a caller is a *copy* of the
00526     canonical document.  A caller that changes that copy changes
00527     nothing: the command of the role still comes from the read-only
00528     view of the canonical document.
00529     """
00530 
00531     __slots__ = ('run_id', 'candidate_sha', 'authorization_sha256',
00532                  'lease_expires_at', 'max_runtime_seconds', 'roles',
00533                  '_canonical')
00534 
00535     def __init__(self, run_id, candidate_sha, authorization_sha256,
00536                  lease_expires_at, max_runtime_seconds, roles):
00537         self.run_id = run_id
00538         self.candidate_sha = candidate_sha
00539         self.authorization_sha256 = authorization_sha256
00540         self.lease_expires_at = lease_expires_at
00541         self.max_runtime_seconds = max_runtime_seconds
00542         self._canonical = {
00543             'schema': PLAN_SCHEMA,
00544             'run_id': run_id,
00545             'candidate_sha': candidate_sha,
00546             'authorization_sha256': authorization_sha256,
00547             'lease_expires_at': lease_expires_at,
00548             'max_runtime_seconds': max_runtime_seconds,
00549             'roles': [copy.deepcopy(dict(role)) for role in roles],
00550         }
00551         self.roles = tuple(_freeze(role) for role in self._canonical['roles'])
00552 
00553     @property
00554     def sealed(self):
00555         """A copy of the canonical document of the plan.
00556 
00557         The copy is a copy: a caller that writes into it writes into a
00558         temporary object and the plan of the round does not change.
00559         """
00560         return copy.deepcopy(self._canonical)
00561 
00562     def sealed_bytes(self):
00563         """The normalized bytes of the seal of the plan.
00564 
00565         The bytes are the one definition of the seal: the parent writes
00566         them, the parent hashes them and the broker hashes the very same
00567         bytes when it consumes the plan.
00568         """
00569         return json.dumps(self._canonical, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True,
00570                           indent=2).encode('utf-8')
00571 
00572     def sealed_sha256(self):
00573         """The digest of the normalized bytes of the seal of the plan."""
00574         return hashlib.sha256(self.sealed_bytes()).hexdigest()
00575 
00576 
00577 def validate_launch_plan(plan, *, now=None):
00578     """Validate one closed launch plan and return its sealed form.
00579 
00580     The plan is never repaired.  A plan that carries an unknown key, an
00581     unknown role, an arbitrary argument vector, a shell, a script, an
00582     environment or a PID is refused here, before anything is created.
00583     """
00584     if not isinstance(plan, dict):
00585         raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_PLAN_NOT_OBJECT')
00586     if set(plan) != PLAN_KEYS:
00587         raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_PLAN_KEY_SET_INVALID')
00588     if plan['schema'] != PLAN_SCHEMA:
00589         raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_PLAN_SCHEMA_INVALID')
00590     run_id = _require_text(plan['run_id'], r'[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]{0,79}',
00591                            'CUSTODY_PLAN_RUN_ID_INVALID')
00592     candidate_sha = _require_text(plan['candidate_sha'], r'[0-9a-f]{40}',
00593                                   'CUSTODY_PLAN_CANDIDATE_INVALID')
00594     authorization_sha256 = _require_text(plan['authorization_sha256'],
00595                                          r'[0-9a-f]{64}',
00596                                          'CUSTODY_PLAN_AUTHORIZATION_INVALID')
00597     wall = time.time() if now is None else _require_number(now, 'CUSTODY_PLAN_LEASE_INVALID')
00598     lease = _require_number(plan['lease_expires_at'], 'CUSTODY_PLAN_LEASE_INVALID')
00599     if lease <= wall:
00600         raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_PLAN_LEASE_EXPIRED')
00601     runtime = _require_exact_int(plan['max_runtime_seconds'],
00602                                  'CUSTODY_PLAN_RUNTIME_INVALID',
00603                                  minimum=1, maximum=MAX_RUNTIME_SECONDS)
00604     roles = plan['roles']
00605     if not isinstance(roles, list) or not roles or len(roles) > len(ROLE_ORDER):
00606         raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_PLAN_ROLES_INVALID')
00607     sealed_roles = {}
00608     logs = set()
00609     for item in roles:
00610         if not isinstance(item, dict) or set(item) != ROLE_KEYS:
00611             raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_PLAN_ROLE_INVALID')
00612         name = item['role']
00613         if not isinstance(name, str) or name not in ROLE_ORDER or name in sealed_roles:
00614             raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_PLAN_ROLE_INVALID')
00615         tools = item['tools']
00616         if not isinstance(tools, list) or len(tools) != ROLE_TOOL_COUNTS[name]:
00617             raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_PLAN_TOOL_INVALID')
00618         sealed_tools = []
00619         paths = set()
00620         for tool in tools:
00621             if not isinstance(tool, dict) or set(tool) != TOOL_KEYS:
00622                 raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_PLAN_TOOL_INVALID')
00623             path = _require_absolute_windows_path(tool['path'], 'CUSTODY_PLAN_TOOL_INVALID')
00624             _require_text(tool['sha256'], r'[0-9a-f]{64}', 'CUSTODY_PLAN_TOOL_INVALID')
00625             if path.casefold() in paths:
00626                 raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_PLAN_TOOL_INVALID')
00627             paths.add(path.casefold())
00628             sealed_tools.append({'path': path, 'sha256': tool['sha256']})
00629         data = item['data']
00630         if not isinstance(data, dict) or set(data) != ROLE_DATA_KEYS[name]:
00631             raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_PLAN_ROLE_DATA_INVALID')
00632         _validate_role_data(name, data)
00633         log = _require_log_reference(item['log'])
00634         if log.casefold() in logs:
00635             raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_PLAN_LOG_INVALID')
00636         logs.add(log.casefold())
00637         sealed_roles[name] = {'role': name, 'tools': sealed_tools,
00638                               'data': dict(data), 'log': log}
00639     if any(name not in sealed_roles for name in REQUIRED_ROLES):
00640         raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_PLAN_ROLES_INVALID')
00641     ordered = [sealed_roles[name] for name in ROLE_ORDER if name in sealed_roles]
00642     return LaunchPlan(run_id, candidate_sha, authorization_sha256, float(lease),
00643                       runtime, ordered)
00644 
00645 
00646 def enforce_child_budget(plan, *, parent_child_remaining_seconds):
00647     """Bind the runtime of the round to the finite budget of the parent.
00648 
00649     The remaining budget of the parent child is an input of the parent.  It
00650     is never read from a ledger here and it is never invented: a parent that
00651     has no remaining budget refuses the round instead of starting one.
00652     """
00653     remaining = _require_exact_int(parent_child_remaining_seconds,
00654                                    'CUSTODY_NO_CHILD_BUDGET_REMAINING', minimum=1)
00655     if plan.max_runtime_seconds > remaining:
00656         raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_CHILD_BUDGET_TOO_SMALL')
00657     return remaining
00658 
00659 
00660 # -- the private control channel -------------------------------------------
00661 
00662 def _strict_pairs(items):
00663     value = {}
00664     for key, item in items:
00665         if key in value:
00666             raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_FRAME_INVALID')
00667         value[key] = item
00668     return value
00669 
00670 
00671 def _reject_constant(_value):
00672     raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_FRAME_INVALID')
00673 
00674 
00675 def decode_frame(raw, *, limit=MAX_FRAME_BYTES):
00676     """Decode one frame of the private channel with the strict rules."""
00677     if not isinstance(raw, (bytes, bytearray)):
00678         raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_FRAME_INVALID')
00679     if len(raw) > limit:
00680         raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_FRAME_TOO_LARGE')
00681     try:
00682         text = bytes(raw).decode('utf-8')
00683     except UnicodeDecodeError as error:
00684         raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_FRAME_INVALID') from error
00685     try:
00686         value = json.loads(text, object_pairs_hook=_strict_pairs,
00687                            parse_constant=_reject_constant)
00688     except CustodyError:
00689         raise
00690     except (ValueError, TypeError) as error:
00691         raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_FRAME_INVALID') from error
00692     if not isinstance(value, dict):
00693         raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_FRAME_INVALID')
00694     return value
00695 
00696 
00697 def encode_command(command, *, run_id, candidate_sha, authorization_sha256,
00698                    nonce, sequence):
00699     """Encode one command of the parent for the private channel."""
00700     if command not in COMMANDS:
00701         raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_FRAME_INVALID')
00702     _require_exact_int(sequence, 'CUSTODY_FRAME_SEQUENCE_INVALID', minimum=1)
00703     frame = {'schema': CHANNEL_SCHEMA, 'command': command, 'run_id': run_id,
00704              'candidate_sha': candidate_sha,
00705              'authorization_sha256': authorization_sha256,
00706              'nonce': nonce, 'sequence': sequence}
00707     raw = json.dumps(frame, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True,
00708                      separators=(',', ':'), allow_nan=False).encode('utf-8')
00709     if len(raw) > MAX_FRAME_BYTES:
00710         raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_FRAME_TOO_LARGE')
00711     return raw + b'\n'
00712 
00713 
00714 def decode_command(raw, *, run_id, candidate_sha, authorization_sha256,
00715                    nonce, last_sequence):
00716     """Decode and check one command that the parent sent to the broker."""
00717     value = decode_frame(raw)
00718     if set(value) != COMMAND_KEYS:
00719         raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_FRAME_INVALID')
00720     if value['schema'] != CHANNEL_SCHEMA:
00721         raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_FRAME_INVALID')
00722     if value['command'] not in COMMANDS:
00723         raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_FRAME_INVALID')
00724     if (value['run_id'] != run_id or value['candidate_sha'] != candidate_sha
00725             or value['authorization_sha256'] != authorization_sha256
00726             or value['nonce'] != nonce):
00727         raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_FRAME_IDENTITY_MISMATCH')
00728     sequence = value['sequence']
00729     if type(sequence) is not int or sequence < 1:
00730         raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_FRAME_SEQUENCE_INVALID')
00731     if last_sequence is not None and sequence <= last_sequence:
00732         raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_FRAME_SEQUENCE_INVALID')
00733     return value
00734 
00735 
00736 def decode_reply(raw, *, run_id, candidate_sha, authorization_sha256,
00737                  nonce, last_sequence):
00738     """Decode and check one reply that the broker sent to the parent."""
00739     value = decode_frame(raw)
00740     if set(value) != REPLY_KEYS:
00741         raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_FRAME_INVALID')
00742     if value['schema'] != CHANNEL_SCHEMA:
00743         raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_FRAME_INVALID')
00744     if value['kind'] not in REPLIES:
00745         raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_FRAME_INVALID')
00746     if (value['run_id'] != run_id or value['candidate_sha'] != candidate_sha
00747             or value['authorization_sha256'] != authorization_sha256
00748             or value['nonce'] != nonce):
00749         raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_FRAME_IDENTITY_MISMATCH')
00750     sequence = value['sequence']
00751     if type(sequence) is not int or sequence < 1:
00752         raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_FRAME_SEQUENCE_INVALID')
00753     if last_sequence is not None and sequence <= last_sequence:
00754         raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_FRAME_SEQUENCE_INVALID')
00755     if value['state'] not in STATES:
00756         raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_FRAME_INVALID')
00757     if not isinstance(value['detail'], dict):
00758         raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_FRAME_INVALID')
00759     return value
00760 
00761 
00762 # -- the one reader of the private channel --------------------------------
00763 
00764 class _ReplyWaiter:
00765     """One waiting owner of one kind of reply."""
00766 
00767     __slots__ = ('kinds', 'event', 'reply')
00768 
00769     def __init__(self, kinds):
00770         self.kinds = kinds
00771         self.event = threading.Event()
00772         self.reply = None
00773 
00774 
00775 class _ChannelPump(threading.Thread):
00776     """The one reader of the private control channel of one round.
00777 
00778     The pump is the single owner of the read side of the channel.  It is
00779     one thread, it is joined by the owner of the round and it is never a
00780     pool of workers.  Its failure is visible: the waiters of the round
00781     are woken and the round keeps the failure instead of waiting for a
00782     reply that will never arrive.
00783 
00784     A reply that nobody waits for is kept, but only the last status
00785     reply is kept: a round that runs for hours would otherwise fill the
00786     channel with the answers of its own heartbeat.
00787     """
00788 
00789     def __init__(self, broker, *, decode, clock=None, on_error=None,
00790                  context=None):
00791         super().__init__(daemon=True, name='lvw-native-custody-channel-pump')
00792         self._broker = broker
00793         self._decode = decode
00794         self._clock = clock or time.monotonic
00795         # The ONE context of the round.  The pump reads the **current**
00796         # absolute deadline and the **published** cleanup deadline of
00797         # the round from it and it never keeps a copy of a deadline of
00798         # its own.
00799         self._context = context
00800         # The owner of the round is told about a failure of the pump.
00801         # A pump that died is not a silent event: the round latches its
00802         # unknown state and closes itself in a bounded way.
00803         self._on_error = on_error
00804         self._lock = threading.Lock()
00805         self._waiters = []
00806         self._pending = []
00807         self._latest_status = None
00808         self._error = None
00809         self._eof = False
00810         self._frames = 0
00811         # The name of the cancel flag is not ``_stop``: the base class of
00812         # the thread uses that name for its own method and a shadow of it
00813         # would break the join of the thread.
00814         self._stop_event = threading.Event()
00815         self._finished = threading.Event()
00816 
00817     # -- the reading ------------------------------------------------------
00818     def _remaining(self):
00819         """The seconds that are left until the current bound of the round.
00820 
00821         The value is read **again** at every call: the absolute deadline
00822         of the round and the **published** cleanup deadline of the round
00823         are one minimum and the pump never keeps a copy of it.  ``None``
00824         means that the round did not bind a deadline at all.
00825         """
00826         context = self._context
00827         if context is None:
00828             return None
00829         value = context.effective_deadline()
00830         if value is None:
00831             return None
00832         clock = getattr(context, 'clock', None) or self._clock
00833         return max(0.0, value - clock())
00834 
00835     def run(self):
00836         try:
00837             while not self._stop_event.is_set():
00838                 remaining = self._remaining()
00839                 if remaining is not None and remaining <= 0:
00840                     # The bound of the round arrived: the pump asks for
00841                     # nothing more and it ends.  It never waits with a
00842                     # lease of its own and it never spins on a round
00843                     # that is over.
00844                     self._eof = True
00845                     break
00846                 raw = self._broker.read(CHANNEL_POLL_SECONDS)
00847                 if raw is None:
00848                     continue
00849                 if raw == b'':
00850                     self._eof = True
00851                     break
00852                 reply = self._decode(raw)
00853                 self._frames += 1
00854                 self._deliver(reply)
00855         except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
00856             self._error = error
00857             if self._on_error is not None:
00858                 try:
00859                     self._on_error(error)
00860                 except BaseException:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
00861                     pass
00862         finally:
00863             with self._lock:
00864                 for waiter in self._waiters:
00865                     waiter.event.set()
00866                 self._waiters = []
00867             self._finished.set()
00868 
00869     def _deliver(self, reply):
00870         with self._lock:
00871             for waiter in list(self._waiters):
00872                 if reply['kind'] in waiter.kinds:
00873                     waiter.reply = reply
00874                     waiter.event.set()
00875                     self._waiters.remove(waiter)
00876                     return
00877             if reply['kind'] == 'STATUS':
00878                 # The status of the round is a snapshot, not a queue: only
00879                 # the last one is kept.
00880                 self._latest_status = reply
00881                 return
00882             if len(self._pending) >= MAX_FRAMES:
00883                 raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_FRAME_COUNT_EXCEEDED')
00884             self._pending.append(reply)
00885 
00886     # -- the waiting ------------------------------------------------------
00887     def take(self, kinds, deadline):
00888         """Wait for one reply of the wanted kinds, bounded by the deadline.
00889 
00890         The **original** context of the round is read **again** at the
00891         moment of the wait: a deadline that was published after the
00892         caller built its number shortens this wait.  A number that was
00893         constructed earlier is never the authority of a wait.
00894         """
00895         waiter = _ReplyWaiter(kinds)
00896         with self._lock:
00897             for index, reply in enumerate(self._pending):
00898                 if reply['kind'] in kinds:
00899                     del self._pending[index]
00900                     return reply
00901             if 'STATUS' in kinds and self._latest_status is not None:
00902                 reply = self._latest_status
00903                 self._latest_status = None
00904                 return reply
00905             if self._error is not None:
00906                 raise self._error
00907             if self._eof:
00908                 raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_CHANNEL_EOF')
00909             self._waiters.append(waiter)
00910         limit = deadline
00911         context = self._context
00912         if context is not None:
00913             current = context.effective_deadline()
00914             if current is not None:
00915                 limit = current if limit is None else min(limit, current)
00916         remaining = limit - self._clock()
00917         if not waiter.event.wait(max(0.0, remaining)):
00918             with self._lock:
00919                 if waiter in self._waiters:
00920                     self._waiters.remove(waiter)
00921             raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_CHANNEL_TIMEOUT')
00922         if waiter.reply is not None:
00923             return waiter.reply
00924         with self._lock:
00925             failure = self._error
00926         if failure is not None:
00927             raise failure
00928         raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_CHANNEL_EOF')
00929 
00930     # -- the end ----------------------------------------------------------
00931     def stop(self):
00932         """Ask the pump to stop.  The pump is joined by its owner."""
00933         self._stop_event.set()
00934 
00935     def join_bounded(self, seconds):
00936         """Join the pump and report whether it really stopped."""
00937         self.join(seconds)
00938         stopped = not self.is_alive()
00939         return stopped
00940 
00941     @property
00942     def frames(self):
00943         return self._frames
00944 
00945     @property
00946     def error(self):
00947         return self._error
00948 
00949     @property
00950     def finished(self):
00951         return self._finished.is_set()
00952 
00953     @property
00954     def alive(self):
00955         return self.is_alive()
00956 
00957 
00958 # -- the parent side session -----------------------------------------------
00959 
00960 class NativeCustodySession:
00961     """The parent side custody of one native custody round.
00962 
00963     The session owns the validated plan, the original handle of the broker
00964     process of this round and the private control channel to it.  It never
00965     stops a native service by name, by PID or by a record of another owner.
00966 
00967     The session is deliberately *not* a service platform.  It launches one
00968     broker process, it exchanges the closed frames of the private channel
00969     with it and it closes it again.  It never loads a caller supplied
00970     module, it never builds an argument vector of its own and it never
00971     starts a native service itself: the broker is the only creator of the
00972     processes of a round.
00973 
00974     Every failure of the start of the round ends inside ``start``: the
00975     original handle of the broker and the private channel are released
00976     there, and the caller never has to remember to stop a round that never
00977     started.
00978     """
00979 
00980     def __init__(self, *, plan, run_root, parent_child_remaining_seconds,
00981                  launcher=None, clock=None, wall=None, nonce=None):
00982         self._document = plan
00983         self._run_root = Path(run_root)
00984         self._remaining = parent_child_remaining_seconds
00985         self._launcher = launcher
00986         self._clock = clock or time.monotonic
00987         self._wall = wall or time.time
00988         self._nonce = self._resolve_nonce(nonce)
00989         self.state = 'NEW'
00990         self.plan = None
00991         self.broker = None
00992         self._sequence = 0
00993         self._broker_sequence = 0
00994         self._frames_sent = 0
00995         self._frames_received = 0
00996         self._deadline_wall = None
00997         self._deadline_mono = None
00998         self._stopped = False
00999         self._failures = []
01000         self._terminal = None
01001         self._broker_identity = None
01002         self._broker_reader_finished = None
01003         self._broker_close_facts = None
01004         self._pump = None
01005         # The failure that the reader of the channel really saw.  It is
01006         # kept with the round, because the reader may be gone when the
01007         # owner of the round reads the first reply: a cancellation that
01008         # the reader saw is the failure of the round and it continues to
01009         # propagate.
01010         self._pump_error = None
01011         self._heartbeat = None
01012         # The stop event of the heartbeat is created **once**, here, and
01013         # it is never rebuilt: a rebuilt event would let a heartbeat
01014         # that starts after the stop of the round wait for a stop that
01015         # will never come.
01016         self._heartbeat_stop = threading.Event()
01017         self._heartbeat_failure = None
01018         self._last_heartbeat = None
01019         self._last_alive_wall = None
01020         self._write_lock = threading.Lock()
01021         # The one owner of the frame budget of the round.  The frames
01022         # that the parent sends and the frames that it receives share
01023         # this one lock, so that the check and the commit of the budget
01024         # are one atomic step in both directions.
01025         self._budget_lock = threading.Lock()
01026         # The one owner of the state of the round.  The latch of the
01027         # unknown state and the application of a state are one atomic
01028         # step: a late reply never turns an unknown round into a
01029         # running one.
01030         self._state_lock = threading.RLock()
01031         self._unknown_latched = False
01032         self._close_lock = threading.Lock()
01033         self._broker_close_failed = False
01034         self._cleanup_deadline = None
01035         # The ONE context of the round.  Every layer of the session
01036         # holds this very object: the send, the stop of the background
01037         # threads, the close of the broker and the recovery of the close
01038         # share one deadline, one cancel and one unknown.
01039         self._context = _CustodyContext(clock=self._clock, wall=self._wall)
01040         # -- the coordination of the close ----------------------------------
01041         # One session keeps ONE cleanup owner and ONE absolute cleanup
01042         # deadline.  The state of the close is explicit: a competitor
01043         # never assumes that a close in progress is a close that is
01044         # finished and it waits inside the same absolute deadline.
01045         self._close_state = 'NOT_STARTED'
01046         self._close_done = threading.Event()
01047         # The generation of the close.  Every attempt of the close owns
01048         # **its own** event: the end of an older attempt never proves
01049         # that the attempt that is running now ended.  A waiter always
01050         # waits for the event of the generation that it found.
01051         self._close_generation = 0
01052         self._close_owner = None
01053         self._close_waiters = 0
01054         self._close_waiter_arrived = threading.Event()
01055         # The event of the **real** end of the closer.  The end of the
01056         # attempt of the close and the end of the thread that performs
01057         # it are two different facts.
01058         self._closer_exited = threading.Event()
01059         # -- the latch of the background of the round ------------------------
01060         # The permission to start a background thread is closed before
01061         # the close takes its snapshot of the threads, so that a thread
01062         # that starts after the close began is refused and never leaks.
01063         self._background_lock = threading.Lock()
01064         self._background_cv = threading.Condition(self._background_lock)
01065         self._background_open = True
01066         self._background_starting = 0
01067         # The state of every background thread of the round.  The state
01068         # tells whether the start of the thread returned, whether it
01069         # failed and whether the thread really runs: a thread whose
01070         # start did not return keeps its owner and it is never taken for
01071         # a thread that ended.
01072         self._background_threads = {}
01073         # The flag of the stop of the round.  A stop blocks every later
01074         # heartbeat: the heartbeat of a round that is stopping must not
01075         # write into a channel that is being closed.
01076         self._stopping = False
01077         # The latch of the bounded close that a failure of the
01078         # background threads started.  The close runs in its own thread:
01079         # a thread never joins itself.
01080         self._closer_started = False
01081         self._closer = None
01082         # Every closer thread that this session ever built.  A closer
01083         # whose start did not return may still come up: it keeps its
01084         # owner in this list and it is never forgotten.
01085         self._closer_threads = []
01086         # The state of the **start** of the closer.  A closer whose
01087         # start did not return may still come up: it is never taken for
01088         # a thread that ended.
01089         self._closer_start_state = 'NOT_STARTED'
01090         # Whether the context of the round was handed over to the
01091         # launcher.  The production launcher accepts it and it holds the
01092         # deadline of the round before the first side effect of the
01093         # launch.  A launcher of the old three argument shape is the
01094         # compatibility boundary of the inspection facades.
01095         self._launcher_context_propagated = None
01096 
01097     # -- the nonce of the round -------------------------------------------
01098     @staticmethod
01099     def _resolve_nonce(nonce):
01100         """The nonce of the private channel of one round.
01101 
01102         The parent and the broker agree on one closed form: 32
01103         hexadecimal characters.  A nonce that does not have that form is
01104         refused here, before any directory, before any file and before
01105         any process.  A nonce identifies the channel of one round and it
01106         grants no custody over any process.
01107         """
01108         if nonce is None:
01109             return os.urandom(NONCE_BYTES).hex()
01110         if not isinstance(nonce, str) or not re.fullmatch(NONCE_PATTERN, nonce):
01111             raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_NONCE_INVALID')
01112         return nonce
01113 
01114     # -- the deadline ----------------------------------------------------
01115     def _fix_deadline(self):
01116         """Fix the absolute deadline of the round, once.
01117 
01118         The deadline is the earlier of the absolute lease of the plan, the
01119         runtime of the plan and the remaining budget of the parent.  It is
01120         fixed here, before the broker is launched and before the first
01121         reply of the round, and it is never moved again: a status request,
01122         a heartbeat or a stop command may not extend it.
01123         """
01124         if self._deadline_wall is not None:
01125             return self._deadline_wall
01126         wall_now = self._wall()
01127         self._deadline_wall = min(self.plan.lease_expires_at,
01128                                   wall_now + self.plan.max_runtime_seconds,
01129                                   wall_now + self._remaining)
01130         self._deadline_mono = self._clock() + max(0.0, self._deadline_wall - wall_now)
01131         # The context of the round carries the same absolute deadline:
01132         # every layer of the round reads it there and it is never
01133         # copied into a deadline of its own.
01134         self._context.bind_absolute_deadline(self._deadline_mono)
01135         return self._deadline_wall
01136 
01137     def remaining_seconds(self):
01138         if self._deadline_mono is None:
01139             return float(self._remaining)
01140         return max(0.0, self._deadline_mono - self._clock())
01141 
01142     # -- the plan --------------------------------------------------------
01143     def validate(self):
01144         """Validate the plan of the round and bind the finite budget."""
01145         if self.state != 'NEW':
01146             raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_STATE_INVALID')
01147         self.plan = validate_launch_plan(self._document, now=self._wall())
01148         enforce_child_budget(self.plan,
01149                              parent_child_remaining_seconds=self._remaining)
01150         self.state = 'VALIDATED'
01151         return self.plan
01152 
01153     # -- the channel -----------------------------------------------------
01154     def _send(self, command, *, terminal=False):
01155         """Send one command of the parent to the broker.
01156 
01157         The write is bounded three times: by the absolute deadline of
01158         the round, by the remaining time of the round and by a fixed
01159         short budget of its own.  A broker that never reads can
01160         therefore never hold the supervisor of the round forever, and
01161         the failure of the write is a named failure of the round.
01162 
01163         The frame budget of the channel is shared by the frames that the
01164         parent sends and by the frames that it receives.  The frames of
01165         the terminal exchange are reserved and the budget is never
01166         widened and never renewed.
01167 
01168         The sequence of a frame is chosen **inside** the write lock and
01169         the frame is encoded there as well.  The parent, the heartbeat
01170         and the stop of the round share ONE sequence owner: two frames
01171         with the same sequence can never be built and no frame is ever
01172         built outside of the lock.
01173 
01174         A round that is stopping sends no further frame: the stop
01175         blocks the heartbeat that would follow it.
01176         """
01177         with self._write_lock:
01178             if self._stopping and not terminal:
01179                 return False
01180             self._reserve_frame(terminal=terminal)
01181             self._sequence += 1
01182             raw = encode_command(command, run_id=self.plan.run_id,
01183                                  candidate_sha=self.plan.candidate_sha,
01184                                  authorization_sha256=self.plan.authorization_sha256,
01185                                  nonce=self._nonce, sequence=self._sequence)
01186             budget = max(0.0, min(self.remaining_seconds(), WRITE_BUDGET_SECONDS))
01187             # The write of the round never outlives the shared cleanup
01188             # deadline of the round: a stop that is in flight does not
01189             # give the write a budget of its own.
01190             cleanup = self._context.cleanup_deadline_value()
01191             if cleanup is not None:
01192                 budget = min(budget, max(0.0, cleanup - self._clock()))
01193             deadline = self._clock() + budget
01194             self._write_to_broker(raw, deadline)
01195             return True
01196 
01197     def _reserve_frame(self, *, terminal=False, received=False):
01198         """One atomic check and commit of the shared frame budget.
01199 
01200         The frames that the parent sends and the frames that it receives
01201         share **one** counter and one owner.  The check and the commit
01202         are one atomic step under the lock of the budget: two
01203         directions that check the budget at the same moment may never
01204         take the last frame of the budget together.
01205 
01206         The reservation of the terminal exchange is **consumed** by the
01207         terminal frame itself: a frame of the terminal exchange is
01208         never refused because of the reservation that was kept for it.
01209         A frame that is not terminal never borrows the reservation and
01210         it fails closed when the total does not fit.
01211         """
01212         with self._budget_lock:
01213             reserved = 0 if terminal else RESERVED_TERMINAL_FRAMES
01214             if self._frames_sent + self._frames_received + reserved + 1 > MAX_FRAMES:
01215                 raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_FRAME_BUDGET_EXCEEDED')
01216             if received:
01217                 self._frames_received += 1
01218             else:
01219                 self._frames_sent += 1
01220             return True
01221 
01222     def _check_frame_budget(self, *, terminal=False):
01223         """The frame budget of the channel, in both directions.
01224 
01225         The round of six hours with its heartbeat stays inside the
01226         budget of the channel, and the frames of the terminal exchange
01227         are reserved.  A round that reached its budget fails closed: it
01228         never reconnects and it never renews its lease.
01229 
01230         The budget is the **total** of the frames that the parent sent
01231         and of the frames that it received.  One direction is never a
01232         substitute for the total: a reply is refused before it is
01233         accepted when the total does not fit.
01234         """
01235         with self._budget_lock:
01236             reserved = 0 if terminal else RESERVED_TERMINAL_FRAMES
01237             if self._frames_sent + self._frames_received + reserved + 1 > MAX_FRAMES:
01238                 raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_FRAME_BUDGET_EXCEEDED')
01239             return True
01240 
01241     def _write_to_broker(self, raw, deadline):
01242         """Write one frame through the deadline aware interface.
01243 
01244         The handle of the broker has to say that it understands an
01245         absolute deadline.  A handle that does not say so is **never**
01246         assumed to be deadline aware: the parent refuses to write into
01247         it instead of blocking on a write that nobody bounds.
01248         """
01249         writer = getattr(self.broker, 'write', None)
01250         if not callable(writer):
01251             raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_BROKER_WRITE_INTERFACE_MISSING')
01252         if getattr(self.broker, 'channel_deadline_aware', False) is not True:
01253             raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_BROKER_NOT_DEADLINE_AWARE')
01254         writer(raw, deadline)
01255 
01256     def _bind_deadline(self, deadline):
01257         """Bind the absolute deadline of the round to the broker handle.
01258 
01259         The deadline may only shorten the deadline that is already
01260         bound.  A handle that does not offer the interface is left
01261         alone: the session still bounds its own waits.
01262         """
01263         if self.broker is None:
01264             return
01265         binder = getattr(self.broker, 'bind_channel_deadline', None)
01266         if not callable(binder):
01267             return
01268         binder(deadline)
01269 
01270     def _read_reply(self, kinds, *, timeout=None):
01271         """Read the replies of the broker until one of them is wanted.
01272 
01273         The read never blocks the deadline of the round: the transport of
01274         the channel is bounded and the wait is clipped to the remaining
01275         deadline of the round.
01276         """
01277         if self._heartbeat_failure is not None:
01278             raise CustodyError(self._heartbeat_failure)
01279         limit = self.remaining_seconds() if timeout is None else timeout
01280         limit = max(0.0, min(limit, self.remaining_seconds()))
01281         deadline = self._clock() + limit
01282         while True:
01283             pump = self._pump
01284             if pump is None:
01285                 # The reader of the channel is gone.  A cancellation that
01286                 # the reader saw is the failure of the round and it
01287                 # continues to propagate; an ordinary failure of the
01288                 # reader is already named in the failures of the round.
01289                 if isinstance(self._pump_error, (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit)):
01290                     raise self._pump_error
01291                 raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_CHANNEL_CLOSED')
01292             reply = pump.take(kinds, deadline)
01293             if reply['kind'] in kinds:
01294                 return reply
01295 
01296     def _decode_reply(self, raw):
01297         """Decode one reply of the broker and check the identity of it."""
01298         reply = decode_reply(raw, run_id=self.plan.run_id,
01299                              candidate_sha=self.plan.candidate_sha,
01300                              authorization_sha256=self.plan.authorization_sha256,
01301                              nonce=self._nonce,
01302                              last_sequence=self._broker_sequence)
01303         # The budget of the channel is the total of both directions.  A
01304         # reply that does not fit into the total is refused before it
01305         # is accepted and before it is counted.  The reply of the
01306         # terminal exchange consumes the reservation that was kept for
01307         # it: it is never refused because of its own reservation.
01308         terminal = reply['kind'] in ('STOPPED', 'REFUSED')
01309         self._reserve_frame(terminal=terminal, received=True)
01310         self._broker_sequence = reply['sequence']
01311         # Only a reply that was decoded and that carries the identity of
01312         # the round refreshes the liveness of the round.
01313         self._last_alive_wall = self._wall()
01314         return reply
01315 
01316     # -- the heartbeat ----------------------------------------------------
01317     def _heartbeat_step(self):
01318         """One step of the status heartbeat of the round.
01319 
01320         The heartbeat is the explicit owner of the keep alive of the
01321         round: it sends one status command of the parent every 60
01322         seconds, so that a long round is not stopped for silence.  It
01323         never extends the absolute deadline of the round: it is a frame
01324         of the closed protocol and nothing else.
01325 
01326         The heartbeat is never sent **after** the absolute deadline of
01327         the round arrived and it is never sent after the round started
01328         to stop.
01329 
01330         The step is a method of its own and it reads the clock of the
01331         session, so that the whole schedule of a round of six hours can
01332         be checked with a clock that only moves when the check moves it.
01333         """
01334         now = self._clock()
01335         if self._last_heartbeat is None:
01336             self._last_heartbeat = now
01337             return False
01338         if now - self._last_heartbeat < HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL_SECONDS:
01339             return False
01340         if self._stopping:
01341             # A round that is stopping sends no further heartbeat.
01342             return False
01343         if self._deadline_mono is not None and now >= self._deadline_mono:
01344             # The absolute lease of the round arrived: the heartbeat is
01345             # not sent any more and it never extends the lease.
01346             return False
01347         self._last_heartbeat = now
01348         try:
01349             sent = self._send('STATUS')
01350         except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
01351             self._heartbeat_failure = ('CUSTODY_HEARTBEAT_FAILED:'
01352                                        + type(error).__name__)
01353             self._latch_background_failure(self._heartbeat_failure)
01354             return False
01355         return bool(sent)
01356 
01357     def _start_heartbeat(self):
01358         """Start the status heartbeat of the round.
01359 
01360         There is exactly one heartbeat thread per round and it is joined
01361         by the owner of the round.  A failure of the heartbeat is a
01362         named failure of the round: the round becomes unknown and it is
01363         never stopped silently in the background.
01364 
01365         The stop event of the heartbeat is created **once**, in the
01366         constructor of the session, and it is never rebuilt.  A rebuilt
01367         event would let a heartbeat that starts after the stop of the
01368         round wait for a stop that will never come.
01369         """
01370         if self._heartbeat is not None:
01371             # There is exactly one heartbeat per round.
01372             return True
01373         if self._heartbeat_stop.is_set():
01374             # The round is already stopping: a heartbeat that starts
01375             # now would be a heartbeat that nobody stops any more.
01376             self._failures.append('CUSTODY_HEARTBEAT_NOT_STARTED')
01377             return False
01378 
01379         def beat():
01380             while not self._heartbeat_stop.wait(HEARTBEAT_SLICE_SECONDS):
01381                 try:
01382                     self._heartbeat_step()
01383                 except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
01384                     self._heartbeat_failure = ('CUSTODY_HEARTBEAT_FAILED:'
01385                                                + type(error).__name__)
01386                     self._latch_background_failure(self._heartbeat_failure)
01387                     return
01388 
01389         thread = threading.Thread(target=beat, daemon=True,
01390                                   name='lvw-native-custody-heartbeat')
01391         return self._start_background_thread('heartbeat', thread)
01392 
01393     def _start_background_thread(self, kind, thread):
01394         """Start one background thread of the round under the latch.
01395 
01396         The registration of the thread, the permission to start it and
01397         the count of the threads that are in the middle of their start
01398         are **one** atomic step under the lock of the background of the
01399         round.  A thread that is registered may be started and a thread
01400         that may not be started any more is never registered.
01401 
01402         The close of the round closes the permission **before** it takes
01403         its snapshot of the threads, so a thread that is started after
01404         the close began is either refused or stopped by the very close
01405         that refused it.
01406 
01407         The state of the thread is recorded **while** it is started:
01408 
01409         * ``STARTING``  the start of the thread did not return yet.  The
01410           thread keeps its owner and it may only clean up: it is never
01411           taken for a thread that ended.
01412         * ``RUNNING``   the thread started and it runs.
01413         * ``FAILED``    the start of the thread raised.  The record says
01414           whether the thread really runs anyway: a thread that runs
01415           keeps the reference of the session and the round becomes
01416           unknown, and a thread that never started is not a thread of
01417           the round.
01418         """
01419         with self._background_lock:
01420             if not self._background_open:
01421                 self._failures.append('CUSTODY_BACKGROUND_START_REFUSED:' + kind)
01422                 return False
01423             record = {'kind': kind, 'thread': thread, 'state': 'STARTING',
01424                       'started': False, 'error': None}
01425             self._background_threads[kind] = record
01426             setattr(self, '_' + kind, thread)
01427             self._background_starting += 1
01428         try:
01429             thread.start()
01430         except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
01431             alive = bool(getattr(thread, 'is_alive', lambda: False)())
01432             ident = getattr(thread, 'ident', None)
01433             with self._background_lock:
01434                 record['error'] = error
01435                 record['state'] = 'FAILED'
01436                 record['started'] = bool(alive or ident is not None)
01437                 self._background_starting -= 1
01438                 self._background_cv.notify_all()
01439             if record['started']:
01440                 # The thread really runs: it keeps the reference of the
01441                 # session and the round becomes unknown.  A thread that
01442                 # runs is never taken for a thread that never started.
01443                 self._failures.append('CUSTODY_BACKGROUND_START_FAILED:' + kind)
01444                 self._latch_unknown_state()
01445             else:
01446                 # The thread never started: its reference is dropped and
01447                 # its failure stays visible.
01448                 with self._background_lock:
01449                     if getattr(self, '_' + kind) is thread:
01450                         setattr(self, '_' + kind, None)
01451             raise
01452         with self._background_lock:
01453             record['state'] = 'RUNNING'
01454             record['started'] = True
01455             self._background_starting -= 1
01456             self._background_cv.notify_all()
01457         return True
01458 
01459     def _latch_unknown_state(self):
01460         """Latch the unknown state of the round.  One way.
01461 
01462         The latch and the application of a state share **one** lock: a
01463         reply that arrives late and the failure of a background thread
01464         may never both believe that they are the first one.  The first
01465         one latches the unknown state and every later one is refused.
01466         """
01467         with self._state_lock:
01468             first = not self._unknown_latched
01469             self._unknown_latched = True
01470             self._context.latch_unknown()
