01471             self.state = 'UNKNOWN_RETAINED'
01472         return first
01473 
01474     def _latch_background_failure(self, code):
01475         """Latch the unknown state of the round after a background failure.
01476 
01477         A failure of the heartbeat or of the pump is not a string that
01478         is appended to a list and nothing else.  The round becomes
01479         unknown and a **bounded** close is started.  The close runs in
01480         its own thread: a thread never joins itself.
01481         """
01482         if code not in self._failures:
01483             self._failures.append(code)
01484         self._latch_unknown_state()
01485         self._start_bounded_close()
01486 
01487     def _apply_state(self, state):
01488         """Apply the state of the round, never overwriting an unknown one.
01489 
01490         A reply that arrives late - after the pump failed and after the
01491         round latched its unknown state - never turns an unknown round
01492         into a running one.  The late reply stays visible in the
01493         evidence of the round.
01494 
01495         The check and the application are **one** atomic step under the
01496         lock of the state of the round: a reply that passed the check
01497         and a failure that latches the unknown state may never both
01498         believe that they are the first one.
01499         """
01500         with self._state_lock:
01501             if self._unknown_latched and state != 'UNKNOWN_RETAINED':
01502                 self._failures.append('CUSTODY_LATE_STATE_IGNORED:' + state)
01503                 return False
01504             self.state = state
01505             return True
01506 
01507     def _start_bounded_close(self):
01508         """Start the bounded close of the round, once, in its own thread.
01509 
01510         The close runs in its own thread because a thread never joins
01511         itself: the pump and the heartbeat of the round report their
01512         failure to the round and the round closes in a thread of its
01513         own.  The callbacks of the pump and of the heartbeat never wait
01514         for that close, so a closer that joins the pump can never wait
01515         for the pump in turn.
01516 
01517         A closer that could not be **built** and a closer that could not
01518         be **started** must not leave the round in ``IN_PROGRESS`` for
01519         ever: the waiters are woken, the failure is named and the round
01520         stays unknown.  The original broker of the round keeps its
01521         owner, so that the public stop of the round may coordinate it
01522         again.
01523         """
01524         with self._close_lock:
01525             if self._closer_started:
01526                 return False
01527             self._closer_started = True
01528             self._closer_start_state = 'STARTING'
01529         try:
01530             thread = threading.Thread(target=self._closer_main, daemon=True,
01531                                       name='lvw-native-custody-closer')
01532             self._closer = thread
01533             self._closer_threads.append(thread)
01534             thread.start()
01535         except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
01536             self._failures.append('CUSTODY_CLOSER_START_FAILED:'
01537                                    + type(error).__name__)
01538             self._latch_unknown_state()
01539             with self._close_lock:
01540                 # The latch of the closer is not permanent: a later
01541                 # attempt may try again and the public stop of the round
01542                 # is the recovery entry of the resources.
01543                 self._closer_started = False
01544                 unpublished = (self._close_state == 'IN_PROGRESS'
01545                                and self._close_owner is None)
01546                 if unpublished:
01547                     self._close_state = 'FINISHED'
01548             if unpublished:
01549                 self._close_done.set()
01550             return False
01551         # The start of the closer returned: the thread of the closer
01552         # really exists and it may be joined.
01553         self._closer_start_state = 'RUNNING'
01554         return True
01555 
01556     def _closer_main(self):
01557         """The body of the closer of the round.
01558 
01559         The end of the **attempt** of the close and the end of the
01560         **thread** that performs it are two different facts: the
01561         evidence of the round carries both of them.
01562         """
01563         try:
01564             self._close_broker()
01565         finally:
01566             self._closer_exited.set()
01567 
01568     def _on_pump_error(self, error):
01569         """The reader of the channel failed: the round latches its state.
01570 
01571         The failure that the reader really saw is kept with the round
01572         and its own code stays visible: the failure of the reader never
01573         replaces the failure that the reader really saw.
01574         """
01575         self._pump_error = error
01576         if isinstance(error, (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit)):
01577             code = 'CUSTODY_PUMP_CANCELLED:' + type(error).__name__
01578         else:
01579             code = 'CUSTODY_PUMP_FAILED:' + type(error).__name__
01580         original = getattr(error, 'code', None)
01581         if isinstance(original, str) and original and original not in self._failures:
01582             self._failures.append(original)
01583         self._latch_background_failure(code)
01584 
01585     def _stop_background(self, deadline=None):
01586         """Stop the background threads of the round and join them.
01587 
01588         A thread that does not stop within the shared cleanup deadline
01589         is a fact of the round: it is named and the round becomes
01590         unknown.  It is never left behind and it is never discarded, and
01591         its reference is kept so that the owner of the round can try it
01592         again.  A thread that really stopped is not kept any more.
01593 
01594         The latch of the background is closed **before** the snapshot of
01595         the threads is taken.  A thread that is started after this
01596         point is refused, and a thread that is in the middle of its own
01597         start is finished before the snapshot is taken: the close of
01598         the round never leaves behind a thread that it did not know
01599         about.
01600 
01601         The close never joins the thread that runs it: a thread that
01602         joins itself is refused by the product and not by the caller.
01603         """
01604         if deadline is None:
01605             deadline = self._cleanup_deadline_for()
01606         # The stop of the round blocks every later heartbeat, also when
01607         # the close was started by a failure and not by the caller.
01608         self._stopping = True
01609         self._context.request_stop()
01610         self._heartbeat_stop.set()
01611         current = threading.current_thread()
01612         with self._background_cv:
01613             self._background_open = False
01614             while self._background_starting > 0:
01615                 remaining = max(0.0, deadline - self._clock())
01616                 if remaining <= 0.0:
01617                     self._failures.append('CUSTODY_BACKGROUND_START_PENDING')
01618                     break
01619                 self._background_cv.wait(remaining)
01620             heartbeat = self._heartbeat
01621             pump = self._pump
01622             records = dict(self._background_threads)
01623         if heartbeat is not None:
01624             record = records.get('heartbeat')
01625             if record is not None and record['state'] == 'STARTING':
01626                 # The start of this thread did not return: the thread
01627                 # may still come up.  It keeps its owner, it is never
01628                 # joined and it may only clean up.  A thread whose start
01629                 # did not return is never taken for a thread that ended.
01630                 self._failures.append('CUSTODY_BACKGROUND_START_PENDING:heartbeat')
01631                 self._latch_unknown_state()
01632             else:
01633                 if heartbeat is not current and heartbeat.ident is not None:
01634                     heartbeat.join(self._cleanup_remaining(deadline))
01635                 if heartbeat.is_alive():
01636                     self._failures.append('CUSTODY_HEARTBEAT_NOT_STOPPED')
01637                     self._latch_unknown_state()
01638                 else:
01639                     self._heartbeat = None
01640         if pump is not None:
01641             pump.stop()
01642             record = records.get('pump')
01643             if record is not None and record['state'] == 'STARTING':
01644                 # The start of the reader did not return: the thread may
01645                 # still come up.  It keeps its owner and it is never
01646                 # joined: a thread that was not started is not joinable.
01647                 self._failures.append('CUSTODY_BACKGROUND_START_PENDING:pump')
01648                 self._latch_unknown_state()
01649             elif pump is current:
01650                 # The close never joins the thread that runs it: a pump
01651                 # that is the current thread stays alive and keeps its
01652                 # reference with its owner.
01653                 self._failures.append('CUSTODY_PUMP_NOT_STOPPED')
01654                 self._latch_unknown_state()
01655             elif pump.ident is None and not pump.is_alive():
01656                 # The reader never started: it is not a thread of the
01657                 # round and it is never joined.
01658                 self._failures.append('CUSTODY_PUMP_NOT_STARTED')
01659                 self._pump = None
01660             elif not pump.join_bounded(self._cleanup_remaining(deadline)):
01661                 self._failures.append('CUSTODY_PUMP_NOT_STOPPED')
01662                 self._latch_unknown_state()
01663             else:
01664                 self._pump = None
01665 
01666     def _cleanup_remaining(self, deadline=None):
01667         """The seconds that are left until the deadline of the cleanup.
01668 
01669         The name of this helper is **not** ``_remaining``: the session
01670         owns ``_remaining`` as the remaining budget of the parent child
01671         and an instance attribute of the same name would shadow this
01672         method.  A shadowed helper is a ``TypeError`` in every close
01673         path of the round.
01674         """
01675         if deadline is None:
01676             return THREAD_JOIN_SECONDS
01677         return max(0.0, deadline - self._clock())
01678 
01679     def _cleanup_deadline_for(self):
01680         """The ONE absolute deadline of the cleanup of the round.
01681 
01682         The deadline is generated at the first stop or at the first
01683         failure of the cleanup.  It is the earlier of the absolute
01684         deadline of the round and the fixed cleanup budget of the round
01685         and it is the same value in every layer of the cleanup: the
01686         send, the stop of the background threads, the close of the
01687         broker, the close of the channel and the close of the endpoints
01688         all use this very value.
01689 
01690         The generation is **one atomic step** under the lock of the
01691         context of the round.  Two callers that entered the cleanup at
01692         the same moment therefore see the very same value: the
01693         deadline is never generated twice and it is never extended by a
01694         caller that came later.
01695         """
01696         published = self._context.cleanup_deadline_value()
01697         if published is not None:
01698             self._cleanup_deadline = published
01699             return published
01700         budget = max(0.0, min(STOP_CLEANUP_SECONDS, self.remaining_seconds()))
01701         value = self._clock() + budget
01702         if self._deadline_mono is not None:
01703             value = min(value, self._deadline_mono)
01704         published = self._context.publish_cleanup_deadline(value)
01705         self._cleanup_deadline = published
01706         return published
01707 
01708     # -- the lifecycle ----------------------------------------------------
01709     def start(self):
01710         """Start the broker of the round and take the custody of it.
01711 
01712         The parent keeps the *original* handle of the broker.  The identity
01713         of the broker is therefore read from that handle and never from a
01714         claim of the broker itself: a child that reports its own pid is a
01715         claim and not a proof.
01716 
01717         The **whole** lifecycle of the start is protected: the launcher,
01718         the reading of the identity of the broker, the construction and
01719         the start of the pump and the start of the heartbeat are all
01720         inside one guard.  Every failure of this method releases the
01721         original handle of the broker and the private channel before it
01722         leaves the method: the caller never has to stop a round that
01723         never started.
01724 
01725         A refusal of the launcher, a timeout, a parse error and an
01726         ordinary exception end the same way: the round is closed here
01727         and it ends in a **structured** terminal state that carries the
01728         named code of the failure.  The code of the original error is
01729         kept: a refusal of the broker is never replaced by a generic
01730         error of the parent.  A cancellation is cleaned up and it
01731         continues to propagate.
01732         """
01733         if self.state == 'NEW':
01734             self.validate()
01735         if self.state != 'VALIDATED':
01736             raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_STATE_INVALID')
01737         self._fix_deadline()
01738         launcher = self._launcher or _default_launcher
01739         try:
01740             try:
01741                 self.broker = self._call_launcher(launcher)
01742             except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit):
01743                 raise
01744             except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
01745                 # The refusal of the launcher is a structured refusal of
01746                 # the round.  The code of the original error is kept and
01747                 # the error is not raised as it is.
01748                 self._latch_unknown_state()
01749                 self._failures.append(self._launch_failure_code(error))
01750                 return self.evidence()
01751             if self.broker is None:
01752                 self._latch_unknown_state()
01753                 self._failures.append('CUSTODY_BROKER_LAUNCH_UNAVAILABLE')
01754                 return self.evidence()
01755             self._broker_identity = {
01756                 'pid': getattr(self.broker, 'pid', None),
01757                 'executable': getattr(self.broker, 'executable', None),
01758                 'executable_sha256': getattr(self.broker, 'executable_sha256', None),
01759                 'identity_source': 'PARENT_ORIGINAL_HANDLE',
01760                 'reported_by_the_broker': False,
01761             }
01762             # The absolute deadline of the round is bound to the handle of
01763             # the broker before the first frame of the round is read: the
01764             # channel of the round can never outlive the round.
01765             try:
01766                 self._bind_deadline(self._deadline_mono)
01767             except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
01768                 self._failures.append(self._failure_code(error))
01769                 self._latch_unknown_state()
01770                 self._close_broker()
01771                 raise
01772             pump = _ChannelPump(self.broker, decode=self._decode_reply,
01773                                 clock=self._clock,
01774                                 on_error=self._on_pump_error,
01775                                 context=self._context)
01776             # The start of the background of the round is refused when
01777             # the close of the round began already.  The start does not
01778             # raise on that refusal: it continues to the exchange of the
01779             # start, and the failure that leaves this method is the
01780             # failure of the reader of the channel - a cancellation
01781             # among them.
01782             self._start_background_thread('pump', pump)
01783             self._start_heartbeat()
01784             ready = self._read_reply(('READY', 'REFUSED'))
01785             if ready['kind'] == 'REFUSED':
01786                 self._latch_unknown_state()
01787                 self._failures.append(ready['detail'].get('code', 'BROKER_REFUSED'))
01788                 self._close_broker()
01789                 return self.evidence()
01790             self._apply_state(ready['state'])
01791             if ready['state'] != 'ARMED':
01792                 raise CustodyError('CUSTODY_STATE_INVALID')
01793             status = self._read_reply(('STATUS', 'REFUSED'))
01794             if status['kind'] == 'REFUSED':
01795                 self._latch_unknown_state()
01796                 self._failures.append(status['detail'].get('code', 'BROKER_REFUSED'))
01797                 self._close_broker()
01798                 return self.evidence()
01799             self._apply_state(status['state'])
01800         except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit):
01801             # A cancellation is cleaned up and it continues to propagate.
01802             self._failures.append('CUSTODY_SESSION_CANCELLED')
01803             self._latch_unknown_state()
01804             self._close_broker()
01805             raise
01806         except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
01807             # An ordinary failure of the start is a structured terminal
01808             # state of the round.  The handle of the broker is released
01809             # even when the exchange of the start failed: the resource of
01810             # this round is never left open on purpose and the caller
01811             # never has to stop it later.
01812             code = self._failure_code(error)
01813             if code not in self._failures:
01814                 self._failures.append(code)
01815             self._latch_unknown_state()
01816             self._close_broker()
01817             return self.evidence()
01818         return self.evidence()
01819 
01820     def _call_launcher(self, launcher):
01821         """Call the launcher of the round with the context of the round.
01822 
01823         The **context** of the round is handed to the launcher when the
01824         launcher accepts it.  The launcher holds the absolute deadline
01825         of the round **before** the first side effect of the launch: the
01826         creation of the process, the reading of the executable, the
01827         channel and its endpoints all read the deadline of the round
01828         from that context.
01829 
01830         A launcher of the **old three argument shape** is called with
01831         three arguments and the round keeps the fact that the context
01832         could not be handed over.  That shape is the compatibility
01833         boundary of the inspection facades of the tests: it may not be
01834         used to enter the production path of the round, because the
01835         deadline of the round would not be bound before the process of
01836         the round exists.
01837         """
01838         accepts = False
01839         try:
01840             signature = inspect.signature(launcher)
01841         except (TypeError, ValueError):  # pragma: no cover - a C callable
01842             signature = None
01843         if signature is not None:
01844             for parameter in signature.parameters.values():
01845                 if parameter.kind == parameter.VAR_KEYWORD:
01846                     accepts = True
01847                     break
01848                 if parameter.name == 'context' and parameter.kind in (
01849                         parameter.KEYWORD_ONLY, parameter.POSITIONAL_OR_KEYWORD):
01850                     accepts = True
01851                     break
01852         if accepts:
01853             self._launcher_context_propagated = True
01854             return launcher(self.plan, self._run_root, self._nonce,
01855                             context=self._context)
01856         self._launcher_context_propagated = False
01857         return launcher(self.plan, self._run_root, self._nonce)
01858 
01859     @staticmethod
01860     def _launch_failure_code(error):
01861         """The named code of a refusal of the launcher.
01862 
01863         The code of the original error is kept.  A refusal of the
01864         broker that names its own code - the unsupported platform of the
01865         broker for instance - is never replaced by a generic code of the
01866         parent.
01867         """
01868         code = getattr(error, 'code', None)
01869         if isinstance(code, str) and code:
01870             return code
01871         return 'CUSTODY_BROKER_LAUNCH_FAILED:' + type(error).__name__
01872 
01873     @staticmethod
01874     def _failure_code(error):
01875         """The named code of one failure of the round."""
01876         code = getattr(error, 'code', None)
01877         if isinstance(code, str) and code:
01878             return code
01879         return 'CUSTODY_SESSION_FAILED:' + type(error).__name__
01880 
01881     def stop(self):
01882         """Stop the round and close the original handle of the broker.
01883 
01884         The stop is idempotent: a repeated stop does not send a second
01885         command, does not create anything and does not change the evidence
01886         of the round.  The closure of the broker happens in a ``finally``,
01887         so it also happens when the exchange of the stop failed.
01888 
01889         The stop and the cleanup share **one** absolute deadline: the
01890         earlier of the absolute deadline of the round and the fixed
01891         cleanup budget of the round.  That one value is used by the send
01892         of the stop, by the stop of the background threads, by the close
01893         of the broker, by the close of the channel and by the close of
01894         the endpoints of the channel.  No layer of the cleanup adds a
01895         budget of its own and a round that is stopped does not get a new
01896         and unauthorised grace period of hours.
01897 
01898         The stop blocks the heartbeat that would follow it.
01899 
01900         A repeated stop does not send a second command and it does not
01901         close a second time.  It waits for the close that is in flight
01902         inside the same absolute deadline of the cleanup, so that it
01903         never returns a success before the cleanup could finish.
01904         """
01905         if self._stopped:
01906             # A repeated stop sends no second command and it closes no
01907             # second time.  It waits for the close that is in flight and
01908             # it coordinates the items that a previous attempt did not
01909             # confirm, inside the **original** deadline of the cleanup.
01910             cleanup_deadline = self._cleanup_deadline_for()
01911             try:
01912                 status = self._close_broker()
01913                 if status != 'PERFORMED':
01914                     self._public_stop_recovery(cleanup_deadline)
01915             finally:
01916                 self._await_close()
01917             return self.evidence()
01918         self._stopped = True
01919         self._stopping = True
01920         self._context.request_stop()
01921         cleanup_deadline = self._cleanup_deadline_for()
01922         status = 'PERFORMED'
01923         try:
01924             if self.broker is not None and self.state in ('ARMED', 'RUNNING'):
01925                 self._bind_deadline(cleanup_deadline)
01926                 self._send('STOP', terminal=True)
01927                 reply = self._read_reply(
01928                     ('STOPPED', 'REFUSED'),
01929                     timeout=max(0.0, cleanup_deadline - self._clock()))
01930                 self._apply_state(reply['state'])
01931                 self._terminal = reply['detail']
01932                 if reply['kind'] == 'REFUSED':
01933                     self._failures.append(reply['detail'].get('code', 'BROKER_REFUSED'))
01934             elif self.broker is None:
01935                 self._failures.append('CUSTODY_BROKER_NOT_STARTED')
01936                 self._latch_unknown_state()
01937         except CustodyError as error:
01938             self._failures.append(error.code)
01939             self._latch_unknown_state()
01940         except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
01941             # An ordinary failure of the stop is a named failure of the
01942             # round: the evidence of the round keeps it and the stop
01943             # does not raise over the evidence.
01944             self._failures.append(self._failure_code(error))
01945             self._latch_unknown_state()
01946         except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
01947             # A cancellation is cleaned up and it continues to propagate.
01948             self._failures.append(self._failure_code(error))
01949             self._latch_unknown_state()
01950             raise
01951         finally:
01952             status = self._close_broker()
01953             if status != 'PERFORMED':
01954                 # An **explicit public stop** coordinates the items that
01955                 # a previous attempt did not confirm.  The internal close
01956                 # of the round never does that: a close that follows the
01957                 # attempt of a background thread - the close of a start
01958                 # that failed, for instance - would close the same peer a
01959                 # second time.
01960                 self._public_stop_recovery(cleanup_deadline)
01961             # The **real** closer of the round is coordinated on every
01962             # exit of the public stop: the normal one, the cancelled one
01963             # and the ordinary failure.  The end of the attempt of the
01964             # close is not the end of the thread that performs it.
01965             self._await_close()
01966         return self.evidence()
01967 
01968     def _close_broker(self):
01969         """Close the original handle of the broker, exactly once.
01970 
01971         One session keeps ONE cleanup owner and ONE absolute cleanup
01972         deadline.  The first caller becomes the owner of the close and
01973         it performs it; every other caller is a competitor and it waits
01974         for the close that is in flight **inside the same absolute
01975         deadline** of the cleanup.  A competitor never assumes that a
01976         close in progress is a close that is finished and it never
01977         renews the deadline of the cleanup.
01978 
01979         The owner publishes the end of its attempt in a ``finally``: a
01980         success, an ordinary exception, a cancellation and a
01981         ``SystemExit`` all wake the waiters.  A waiter never holds the
01982         lock of the owner while it waits and the owner never waits for
01983         itself.
01984 
01985         A handle that could not be closed is *kept*: the reference of the
01986         original handle stays in the evidence of the round and the round
01987         stays unknown.  A failed close is never swallowed and never
01988         replaced by a success.
01989 
01990         The internal close of the round never coordinates the items
01991         that a previous attempt did not confirm.  The recovery of the
01992         unconfirmed items belongs to the **public stop** of the round:
01993         a close that follows the attempt of a background thread - the
01994         close of a start that failed, for instance - would close the
01995         same peer a second time.
01996 
01997         The return value names the role of the caller: ``PERFORMED``
01998         when this call was the owner of the close, ``ALREADY_FINISHED``
01999         when an attempt ended before, ``WAITED`` when another owner was
02000         closing the round and ``OWNER`` when the caller is the owner
02001         itself.
02002         """
02003         deadline = self._cleanup_deadline_for()
02004         with self._close_lock:
02005             state = self._close_state
02006             if state == 'NOT_STARTED':
02007                 if self.broker is None:
02008                     # Nothing was acquired: there is nothing to close
02009                     # and no owner of the close is claimed.
02010                     return 'NOTHING_TO_CLOSE'
02011                 self._close_state = 'IN_PROGRESS'
02012                 self._close_owner = threading.current_thread()
02013                 owner = True
02014             else:
02015                 owner = False
02016         if owner:
02017             try:
02018                 self._perform_close(deadline)
02019             finally:
02020                 # The end of the attempt is published on every path: a
02021                 # success, an ordinary exception, a cancellation and a
02022                 # SystemExit all wake the waiters.
02023                 self._finish_close()
02024             return 'PERFORMED'
02025         if state == 'FINISHED':
02026             # The attempt that was in flight ended.  An attempt that
02027             # ended is **not** a release, but the internal close does
02028             # not coordinate the unconfirmed items again: the public
02029             # stop of the round does that, and only that.
02030             return 'ALREADY_FINISHED'
02031         if self._close_owner is threading.current_thread():
02032             # The owner of the close never waits for itself.
02033             return 'OWNER'
02034         self._await_close()
02035         return 'WAITED'
02036 
02037     def _public_stop_recovery(self, deadline):
02038         """Coordinate the items that a previous attempt did not confirm.
02039 
02040         Only an **explicit public stop** of the round reaches this
02041         method.  The internal close of the round never coordinates the
02042         unconfirmed items again: a close that follows the attempt of a
02043         background thread would close the same peer a second time.
02044 
02045         The recovery is a **take over** of the unconfirmed items by the
02046         owner of the public stop and it uses the **original** deadline
02047         of the cleanup: the deadline is never renewed.  A recovery that
02048         arrives after the deadline keeps the resources of the round with
02049         their owner and the round stays unknown.
02050 
02051         A competitor never opens a second close at the same time: a
02052         close that is in flight is waited for and the items that it did
02053         not confirm may be coordinated by a **later** public stop.  The
02054         recovery is therefore never locked out.
02055         """
02056         if self.broker is None:
02057             # Everything that the round owned was released: there is
02058             # nothing left to coordinate.
02059             return 'NOTHING_TO_RECOVER'
02060         with self._close_lock:
02061             if self._close_state == 'IN_PROGRESS':
02062                 # Another owner is closing the round right now: the
02063                 # competitor waits for that owner and it never opens a
02064                 # second close at the same time.
02065                 return 'IN_PROGRESS'
02066             if self._close_state != 'FINISHED':
02067                 # No attempt of the close ever ended: there is nothing
02068                 # that could be coordinated again.
02069                 return 'NOT_STARTED'
02070         self._recover_close(deadline)
02071         return 'RECOVERED'
02072 
02073     def _recover_close(self, deadline):
02074         """Coordinate the items that a previous attempt did not confirm.
02075 
02076         The recovery is the **public** entry of the round: it calls the
02077         product entry of the handle of the broker and it never calls a
02078         method of a stand-in.  Only an explicit public stop reaches it
02079         and the internal close of the round never does.  The original
02080         deadline of the cleanup is never renewed: a recovery that
02081         arrives after it keeps the resources of the round with their
02082         owner and the round stays unknown.
02083 
02084         The recovery opens a **new generation** of the close and it
02085         publishes its own event.  The end of the previous attempt never
02086         proves that the attempt that is running now ended: a waiter
02087         that arrives during this generation waits for this generation.
02088         """
02089         if self.broker is None:
02090             # Everything that the round owned was released: there is
02091             # nothing left to coordinate.
02092             return
02093         if max(0.0, deadline - self._clock()) <= 0.0:
02094             self._failures.append('CUSTODY_CLOSE_RECOVERY_EXPIRED')
02095             self._latch_unknown_state()
02096             return
02097         with self._close_lock:
02098             if self._close_state == 'IN_PROGRESS':
02099                 # Another owner is closing the round right now.
02100                 return
02101             self._close_state = 'IN_PROGRESS'
02102             self._close_owner = threading.current_thread()
02103             self._close_generation += 1
02104             self._close_done = threading.Event()
02105         try:
02106             self._perform_close(deadline)
02107         finally:
02108             self._finish_close()
02109 
02110     def _perform_close(self, deadline):
02111         """The close of the original handle of the broker, by its owner.
02112 
02113         The steps of the close are independent of each other: a failure
02114         of one of them never skips the ones that are safe to run.
02115 
02116         The release of the reference needs the **facts** of the close:
02117         the channel, the process and its original handle and the reader
02118         of the channel have to be closed and the reader has to have
02119         stopped without an error.  A missing fact, a ``None``, a
02120         ``False``, an unknown value and an exception are all unknown: the
02121         original broker is kept and the round stays unknown.
02122         """
02123         try:
02124             self._stop_background(deadline)
02125         except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
02126             # The background threads of the round could not be stopped.
02127             # The close of the original handle of the broker still runs:
02128             # the steps of the close are independent of each other.
02129             self._failures.append('CUSTODY_BACKGROUND_STOP_FAILED:'
02130                                    + type(error).__name__)
02131             self._latch_unknown_state()
02132         try:
02133             self._bind_deadline(deadline)
02134         except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
02135             self._failures.append(self._failure_code(error))
02136         broker = self.broker
02137         if broker is None:
02138             return
02139         try:
02140             facts = broker.close()
02141         except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
02142             self._failures.append('CUSTODY_BROKER_CLOSE_FAILED:'
02143                                    + type(error).__name__)
02144             self._broker_close_failed = True
02145             self._latch_unknown_state()
02146             self._broker_reader_finished = getattr(broker, 'reader_finished', None)
02147             return
02148         self._broker_reader_finished = getattr(broker, 'reader_finished', None)
02149         self._broker_close_facts = facts if isinstance(facts, dict) else None
02150         if not isinstance(facts, dict):
02151             # A close that returned no facts at all is not a proof.
02152             self._failures.append('CUSTODY_BROKER_CLOSE_INCOMPLETE:facts')
02153             self._broker_close_failed = True
02154             self._latch_unknown_state()
02155             return
02156         incomplete = []
02157         for key in REQUIRED_CLOSE_FACTS:
02158             if key not in facts or facts[key] is not True:
02159                 incomplete.append(key)
02160         for key in REQUIRED_CLOSE_ZERO_FACTS:
02161             if key not in facts or facts[key] != 0:
02162                 incomplete.append(key)
02163         for key in READER_CLOSE_FACTS:
02164             if key in facts and facts[key] is not True:
02165                 incomplete.append(key)
02166         if getattr(broker, 'reader_finished', None) is not True:
02167             # The reader of the channel has to have really stopped.
02168             # A missing fact and an unknown fact are both unknown.
02169             incomplete.append('reader_finished')
02170         if facts.get('channel_reader_error'):
02171             incomplete.append('channel_reader_error')
02172         if facts.get('channel_retained_workers'):
02173             incomplete.append('channel_retained_workers')
02174         if incomplete:
02175             for key in incomplete:
02176                 self._failures.append('CUSTODY_BROKER_CLOSE_INCOMPLETE:' + key)
02177             self._broker_close_failed = True
02178             self._latch_unknown_state()
02179             # The original broker is kept: the reference of the
02180             # resource of the round is never dropped and the round
02181             # never claims a release that it cannot prove.
02182             return
02183         self.broker = None
02184 
02185     def _finish_close(self):
02186         """Publish the end of the attempt of the close of the round.
02187 
02188         FINISHED means that the attempt ended and it never means that
02189         the resources of the round are released: only the facts of the
02190         close prove that.
02191 
02192         The event that is published is the event of the **current**
02193         generation of the close: the end of an older attempt never
02194         releases a waiter of the attempt that is running now.
02195         """
02196         with self._close_lock:
02197             self._close_state = 'FINISHED'
02198             event = self._close_done
02199         event.set()
02200 
02201     def _await_close(self):
02202         """Wait for the close that is in flight, inside the shared deadline.
02203 
02204         The wait never holds the lock of the owner: the owner may need
02205         it while it stops the background threads of the round.  The
02206         arrival of a waiter is published, so that the round and its
02207         evidence can see that a competitor waits for the close that it
02208         owns.
02209 
02210         The end of the **attempt** of the close and the end of the
02211         **thread** that performs it are two different facts.  A waiter
02212         that arrives when the attempt already ended still waits for the
02213         real exit of the closer: only a close that has no closer thread
02214         of its own and no cleanup in flight may be confirmed at once.
02215         """
02216         with self._close_lock:
02217             state = self._close_state
02218             if state == 'IN_PROGRESS' and self._close_owner is threading.current_thread():
02219                 # The owner of the close never waits for itself.
02220                 return True
02221             waiting = state == 'IN_PROGRESS'
02222             if waiting:
02223                 self._close_waiters += 1
02224             # The waiter waits for the event of the **generation** that
02225             # it found.  The end of an older attempt never releases a
02226             # waiter of the attempt that is running now.
02227             event = self._close_done
02228         if waiting:
02229             self._close_waiter_arrived.set()
02230         try:
02231             deadline = self._cleanup_deadline_for()
02232             if waiting:
02233                 while True:
02234                     remaining = max(0.0, deadline - self._clock())
02235                     if remaining <= 0.0:
02236                         # The shared deadline of the cleanup arrived.
02237                         # The deadline is never renewed and the original
02238                         # resources of the round are kept.
02239                         self._failures.append('CUSTODY_CLOSE_NOT_FINISHED')
02240                         self._latch_unknown_state()
02241                         return False
02242                     if event.wait(min(remaining, CLOSE_WAIT_SLICE_SECONDS)):
02243                         # The end of the attempt of this generation is
02244                         # published.  A **new** generation may have
02245                         # begun in the meantime: the waiter follows the
02246                         # current owner and it is never released by the
02247                         # end of an older attempt.  The end of the
02248                         # **thread** that performed the attempt is a
02249                         # second fact.
02250                         with self._close_lock:
02251                             if (self._close_state == 'IN_PROGRESS'
02252                                     and self._close_owner is not threading.current_thread()):
02253                                 event = self._close_done
02254                                 continue
02255                         break
02256             return self._await_closer_exit(deadline)
02257         finally:
02258             if waiting:
02259                 with self._close_lock:
02260                     self._close_waiters -= 1
02261 
02262     def _await_closer_exit(self, deadline):
02263         """Wait for the **real** exit of the closer of the round.
02264 
02265         The end of the attempt of the close is published by the owner
02266         of the close and it is published **before** the thread that
02267         performs it returns.  The event of the closer is therefore not
02268         a proof that the thread ended: the waiter joins the thread and
02269         it asks the thread itself whether it is still alive.
02270 
02271         The join uses the **same** absolute deadline of the cleanup and
02272         the remaining budget is recomputed before every single join.
02273         The round never joins itself and a closer never waits for a
02274         waiter: there is no cycle of waits.
02275 
02276         A closer whose **start** did not return is **unknown** and not a
02277         thread that ended: the join of such a thread raises and the
02278         thread may still come up.  Such a closer keeps its owner and
02279         the round stays unknown.
02280 
02281         A closer that is still alive when the deadline arrives keeps
02282         its owner and the round stays unknown.
02283         """
02284         with self._close_lock:
02285             closers = list(self._closer_threads)
02286         if not closers:
02287             # The close was performed by the caller itself: there is no
02288             # thread of its own and there is nothing to join.
02289             return True
02290         current = threading.current_thread()
02291         for closer in closers:
02292             if closer is current:
02293                 # The round never joins itself.  The closer is the
02294                 # thread that runs this wait: it cannot wait for its
02295                 # own exit and it ends as soon as this method returns.
02296                 continue
02297             if not self._join_one_closer(closer, deadline):
02298                 return False
02299         return True
02300 
02301     def _join_one_closer(self, closer, deadline):
02302         """Wait for the real exit of one closer of the round."""
02303         while True:
02304             remaining = max(0.0, deadline - self._clock())
02305             if remaining <= 0.0:
02306                 # The original deadline of the cleanup arrived.  The
02307                 # deadline is never renewed, the closer keeps its owner
02308                 # and the round stays unknown.
02309                 self._failures.append('CUSTODY_CLOSER_NOT_FINISHED')
02310                 self._latch_unknown_state()
02311                 return False
02312             try:
02313                 closer.join(min(remaining, CLOSE_WAIT_SLICE_SECONDS))
02314             except RuntimeError:
02315                 # The start of this closer did not return: the thread
02316                 # was never started and it may still come up.  It is
02317                 # **unknown** and not a thread that ended.
02318                 self._failures.append('CUSTODY_CLOSER_START_UNKNOWN')
02319                 self._latch_unknown_state()
02320                 return False
02321             if not closer.is_alive():
02322                 return True
02323 
02324     def __enter__(self):
02325         return self
02326 
02327     def __exit__(self, _kind, _value, _traceback):
02328         try:
02329             self.stop()
02330         except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the evidence keeps the failure
02331             self._latch_unknown_state()
02332         return False
02333 
02334     # -- the evidence ------------------------------------------------------
02335     def evidence(self):
02336         """The evidence of the round.
02337 
02338         Every native claim of this evidence is ``NOT_PROVEN``.  This package
02339         has never executed a Windows service, so it may not claim that a
02340         service was released, that a GPU was released, that a port was
02341         released or that a parent crash is survived.
02342         """
02343         return {
02344             'kind': 'lvw.s3-native-custody-evidence/1',
02345             'state': self.state,
02346             'run_id': self.plan.run_id if self.plan is not None else None,
02347             'candidate_sha': self.plan.candidate_sha if self.plan is not None else None,
02348             'authorization_sha256': (self.plan.authorization_sha256
02349                                      if self.plan is not None else None),
02350             'grant_authority': 'NOT_VERIFIED',
02351             'plan_sha256_proves': 'PLAN_INTEGRITY_ONLY',
02352             'plan': self.plan.sealed if self.plan is not None else None,
02353             'plan_sha256': (self.plan.sealed_sha256()
02354                             if self.plan is not None else None),
02355             'broker': self._broker_identity,
02356             'broker_channel_reader_finished': self._broker_reader_finished,
02357             # Whether the ONE context of the round was handed over to
02358             # the launcher of the broker.  ``None`` means that no
02359             # launcher was called at all.
02360             'launcher_context_propagated': self._launcher_context_propagated,
02361             'broker_retained': self.broker is not None,
02362             'broker_close_failed': self._broker_close_failed,
02363             'broker_close_facts': self._broker_close_facts,
02364             'broker_close_facts_required': list(REQUIRED_CLOSE_FACTS),
02365             'pump_error': (repr(self._pump.error)
02366                            if self._pump is not None and self._pump.error is not None
02367                            else None),
02368             'heartbeat_alive': bool(self._heartbeat is not None
02369                                     and self._heartbeat.is_alive()),
02370             'pump_alive': bool(self._pump is not None and self._pump.alive),
02371             # The coordination of the close of the round.  ``FINISHED``
02372             # means that the attempt of the close ended and it never
02373             # means that the resources of the round are released: only
02374             # the facts of the close prove that.
02375             'close_state': self._close_state,
02376             'close_finished': self._close_done.is_set(),
02377             'close_generation': self._close_generation,
02378             'close_waiters': self._close_waiters,
02379             'closer_alive': bool(self._closer is not None and self._closer.is_alive()),
02380             # The state of the **start** of the closer and the number of
02381             # the closer threads that this session ever built.  A closer
02382             # whose start did not return may still come up: it keeps its
02383             # owner in the list and it is never forgotten.
02384             'closer_start_state': self._closer_start_state,
02385             'closer_threads': len(self._closer_threads),
02386             # The end of the **thread** of the closer and the end of its
02387             # **attempt** are two facts.  The event of the closer is
02388             # published inside the thread and it is therefore not a
02389             # proof that the thread ended: the evidence asks the thread
02390             # itself.
02391             'closer_exited': bool(self._closer_exited.is_set()
02392                                    and self._closer is not None
02393                                    and not self._closer.is_alive()),
02394             'background_open': self._background_open,
02395             # The state of every background thread of the round.  The
02396             # state tells whether the start of the thread returned and
02397             # whether the thread really runs: a thread whose start did
02398             # not return is never taken for a thread that ended.
02399             'background_threads': [{'kind': record['kind'],
02400                                     'state': record['state'],
02401                                     'started': record['started'],
02402                                     'alive': bool(record['thread'].is_alive()),
02403                                     'error_type': (type(record['error']).__name__
02404                                                    if record['error'] is not None
02405                                                    else None)}
02406                                    for record in self._background_threads.values()],
02407             'cleanup_deadline': self._cleanup_deadline,
02408             'broker_deadline_aware': (getattr(self.broker, 'channel_deadline_aware', False)
02409                                       is True),
02410             'broker_channel_deadline_bound': getattr(self.broker, 'write_deadline', None),
02411             'channel': {
02412                 'schema': CHANNEL_SCHEMA,
02413                 'nonce': self._nonce,
02414                 'frames_sent': self._frames_sent,
02415                 'frames_received': self._frames_received,
02416                 'last_sequence_sent': self._sequence,
02417                 'last_sequence_received': self._broker_sequence,
02418                 'frame_limit': MAX_FRAMES,
02419                 'frame_bytes_limit': MAX_FRAME_BYTES,
02420                 'private_to_the_parent': True,
02421                 'heartbeat_interval_seconds': HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL_SECONDS,
02422                 'heartbeat_missing_seconds': HEARTBEAT_MISSING_SECONDS,
02423                 'heartbeat_failure': self._heartbeat_failure,
02424                 'last_alive_wall': self._last_alive_wall,
02425                 'frame_budget_total': MAX_FRAMES,
02426                 'frame_budget_reserved_terminal': RESERVED_TERMINAL_FRAMES,
02427                 'stop_cleanup_budget_seconds': STOP_CLEANUP_SECONDS,
02428             },
02429             'lease': {
02430                 'lease_expires_at': self.plan.lease_expires_at if self.plan is not None else None,
02431                 'max_runtime_seconds': (self.plan.max_runtime_seconds
02432                                         if self.plan is not None else None),
02433                 'parent_child_remaining_seconds': self._remaining,
02434                 'effective_deadline_wall': self._deadline_wall,
02435                 'deadline_extended': False,
02436                 'absolute': True,
02437             },
02438             'terminal': self._terminal,
02439             'failures': list(self._failures),
02440             # The claims that this package does not make.
02441             'native_execution_proven': False,
02442             'native_cleanup_proven': False,
02443             'gpu_released_proven': False,
02444             'port_released_proven': False,
02445             'sampler_proven': False,
02446             'parent_crash_recovery_proven': False,
02447             'windows_stdin_eof_proven': False,
02448             'admission_produced': False,
02449             'stage_pass_claim': False,
02450             'product_gate0': False,
02451         }
02452 
02453 
02454 def _default_launcher(plan, run_root, nonce, *, context=None):
02455     """The production launcher of the broker of this package.
02456 
02457     The launcher is deliberately inert here.  The broker of this package is
02458     a Windows process and this package has never started one, so the
02459     launcher refuses on every platform instead of pretending that a native
02460     execution exists.  The wiring of the launcher into the parent
02461     controller is a later owner and it is not enabled by this package.
02462 
02463     The **context** of the round is handed over to the launcher of the
02464     broker: the launcher holds the absolute deadline of the round and the
02465     one context of the round **before** the first side effect of the
02466     launch - the creation of the process, the reading of the executable,
02467     the channel and its endpoints.
02468     """
02469     from tools.v0_s3_native_broker import launch_broker
02470     return launch_broker(plan, run_root, nonce, context=context)

### 206a7fe7f21343d89ca83e03a0e4dc7e670cd381:tools/test_v0_s3_native_custody_correction05.py
00001 """The frozen regressions of the N1 correction 05 of the native custody.
00002 
00003 This module carries the **24 frozen oracle groups** of the root package
00004 ``reports/v0-s3/product-gate0/native-custody-correction-05`` (the file
00005 ``regression-obligations.json``).  The obligations are frozen by the
00006 root and they are not written by the implementer: every group below is
00007 mapped to the production owner that has to carry it and to the test
00008 that drives it.
00009 
00010 Every group is written in the same closed form.  The group names
00011 
00012 * the **obligation** and the major that it belongs to,
00013 * the **production entry** that has to carry it,
00014 * the **trigger** that the check drives,
00015 * the **oracle** that the product has to satisfy,
00016 * the **negative** case and the exact boundary,
00017 * the **state that the product returned with**, before any salvage of
00018   the test owner,
00019 * the **salvage** of the test owner, which is explicit, bounded and
00020   which never changes the fact of the product.
00021 
00022 The rules of this module are the rules of the whole N1 package.
00023 
00024 * It is **offline**.  It never starts a process, it never opens a
00025   socket, it never touches a database and it never reads the original
00026   budget ledger or the original shared counter.  The Windows primitives
00027   are driven through an **in-process** table of primitives that is
00028   injected into the production façade: the production code that is
00029   checked here is the code of a real run, only the native library is
00030   missing.
00031 
00032 * Every case drives the **actual production method**: the production
00033   ``WindowsApi`` with an injected primitive table, the production
00034   ``BrokerCore``, the production ``BrokerLoop``, the production
00035   ``BrokerProcess``, the production ``StreamChannel``, the production
00036   ``_WindowsPipeWorker`` and ``_WindowsPipeEndpoint`` and the
00037   production ``NativeCustodySession``.  A façade that only returns the
00038   right story would not prove anything and it is not used for the
00039   failure paths.
00040 
00041 * Every case records the state of the resources **at the moment the
00042   product returned**, before any salvage of the test owner.  The
00043   cleanup of the test owner is explicit and it is never used to
00044   declare that the product returned cleanly: a product that could not
00045   release a resource stays unknown and keeps its original reference.
00046 
00047 * The native claims of this package stay ``NOT_PROVEN``.  The module
00048   proves the behaviour of the Python owners and it never claims that a
00049   Windows API, a job object, a GPU, a port or a service was executed.
00050 
00051 The boundary of the process is not installed here: the guard of the
00052 batch lives in the report directory of the batch and the runner of the
00053 batch is its only authorised entry.

00120 
00121 class SteppingClock:
00122     """A clock that moves by a fixed step on every read.
00123 
00124     A clock that only moves when the check moves it cannot tell an
00125     atomic publication from a lucky one: two threads that read the
00126     same value would see the same result with and without a lock.  A
00127     clock that moves on every read makes the difference visible: the
00128     value that was published is the value that everybody sees.
00129     """
00130 
00131     def __init__(self, value=1000.0, step=0.5):
00132         self.value = float(value)
00133         self.step = float(step)
00134 
00135     def __call__(self):
00136         current = self.value
00137         self.value += self.step
00138         return current
00139 
00140     def advance(self, seconds):
00141         self.value += float(seconds)
00142 
00143 
00144 class _RecordingEvent:
00145     """An event that records the waits and moves the clock."""
00146 
00147     def __init__(self, clock, waits):
00148         self._clock = clock
00149         self._waits = waits
00150         self._set = False
00151 
00152     def is_set(self):
00153         return self._set
00154 
00155     def set(self):
00156         self._set = True
00157 
00158     def wait(self, timeout=None):
00159         self._waits.append(timeout)
00160         # The wait consumes the budget of the round: the clock moves.
00161         self._clock.advance(timeout or 0.0)
00162         return self._set
00163 
00164 
00165 class RecordingReader:
00166     """A stand-in of the reader of a child stream.
00167 
00168     The stand-in records the wait of the reader and the join of the
00169     reader, and it moves the clock of the round while it waits.  It is
00170     the **production** ``BrokerCore._close_readers`` that coordinates it.
00171     """
00172 
00173     def __init__(self, clock, handle, name='lvw-native-custody-stream-fake'):
00174         self.handle = handle
00175         self.name = name
00176         self.error = None
00177         self.ident = 1
00178         self.waits = []
00179         self.joins = []
00180         self._clock = clock
00181         self._alive = True
00182         self.finished = _RecordingEvent(clock, self.waits)
00183 
00184     def is_alive(self):
00185         return self._alive
00186 
00187     def join(self, timeout=None):
00188         self.joins.append(timeout)
00189         self._alive = False
00190 
00191     def stop(self):
00192         self._alive = False

00195 # -- N05-R01: the null device is owned from the moment of its creation ----
00196 
00197 def test_n05_r01_the_null_device_has_an_owner_from_the_moment_of_its_creation():
00198     """N05-R01 (M03): the null device keeps its original owner.
00199 
00200     Obligation: the null device of the child has to be owned from the
00201     moment of its creation.  Entry: ``WindowsApi.create_process_suspended``.
00202     Trigger: the close of the null device fails.  Oracle: the original
00203     handle of the null device stays with its owner, the failure keeps
00204     it recoverable and the other safe cleanups still run.  Negative: a
00205     null device that was closed with a confirmed close leaves the table.
00206     Returned state: the failed creation names the null device and the
00207     table of the façade still holds the original handle.  Salvage: the
00208     recovery of the owner is explicit and it closes the handle.
00209     """
00210     null_handle = 901
00211 
00212     def fail_null_close(kernel32, *args):
00213         handle = getattr(args[0], 'value', args[0])
00214         if handle == null_handle:
00215             # The close of the null device fails the first time only:
00216             # a later recovery of the owner may close it.
00217             attempts = kernel32.__dict__.setdefault('null_close_attempts', {})
00218             attempts[handle] = attempts.get(handle, 0) + 1
00219             if attempts[handle] == 1:
00220                 return 0
00221         kernel32.live.pop(handle, None)
00222         return 1
00223 
00224     kernel32 = SyntheticKernel32(script={'CloseHandle': fail_null_close})
00225     kernel32.null_handle = null_handle
00226     api = WindowsApi(table=synthetic_table(kernel32))
00227     with pytest.raises(BrokerError) as info:
00228         api.create_process_suspended(executable='C:/tools/python.exe', arguments=['-s'],
00229                                      working_directory='C:/round', stdout_handle=0x21,
00230                                      stderr_handle=0x22, environment=None)
00231     # The failure of the close of the null device is the failure of the
00232     # creation: it is named and it is not only a message.
00233     assert info.value.code == 'BROKER_NULL_DEVICE_CLOSE_FAILED'
00234     assert 'CloseHandle' in (info.value.detail or '')
00235     # The null device is with its owner and with its **original**
00236     # reference: the table of the façade is the recovery entry point.
00237     retained = api.retained_handles()
00238     kinds = sorted(entry['kind'] for entry in retained)
00239     assert kinds == ['null_device']
00240     entry = retained[0]
00241     assert entry['handle'] == null_handle
00242     assert entry['owner'] == 'WINDOWS_API'
00243     # The other safe cleanups of the creation still ran.
00244     assert 'TerminateProcess' in kernel32.names()
00245     assert 'WaitForSingleObject' in kernel32.names()
00246     # The recovery of the owner is explicit and it closes the handle.
00247     report = api.release_retained()
00248     assert [item['kind'] for item in report] == ['null_device']
00249     assert api.retained_handles() == []
00250     assert null_handle not in kernel32.live

00253 # -- N05-R02: a cancellation never skips the other finalizers ------------
00254 
00255 def test_n05_r02_a_cancellation_in_the_cleanup_never_skips_the_other_finalizers():
00256     """N05-R02 (M03): the finalizers of a creation are independent.
00257 
00258     Obligation: every safe finalizer of a creation runs on its own, the
00259     primary and the secondary error coexist and the cancellation
00260     propagates after the cleanup.  Entry:
00261     ``WindowsApi.create_process_suspended``.  Trigger:
00262     ``DeleteProcThreadAttributeList`` raises a cancellation.  Oracle:
00263     the null device was closed, the created process was released and
00264     the cancellation is named.  Negative: the cancellation never
00265     replaces the primary error of the creation.  Returned state: the
00266     cancellation is the error that leaves the method.  Salvage: the
00267     retained handles of the façade are released by their owner.
00268     """
00269     def cancel_delete(kernel32, *args):
00270         raise KeyboardInterrupt('delete_cancelled')
00271 
00272     kernel32 = SyntheticKernel32(script={'DeleteProcThreadAttributeList': cancel_delete})
00273     api = WindowsApi(table=synthetic_table(kernel32))
00274     with pytest.raises(KeyboardInterrupt):
00275         api.create_process_suspended(executable='C:/tools/python.exe', arguments=['-s'],
00276                                      working_directory='C:/round', stdout_handle=0x21,
00277                                      stderr_handle=0x22, environment=None)
00278     # The safe steps of the cleanup ran: the deletion of the attribute
00279     # list never skipped the close of the null device.
00280     assert 'CloseHandle' in kernel32.names()
00281     assert kernel32.null_handle not in kernel32.live
00282     # The release of the created process ran as well.
00283     assert 'TerminateProcess' in kernel32.names()
00284     assert 'WaitForSingleObject' in kernel32.names()
00285     assert kernel32.process_handle not in kernel32.live
00286     assert kernel32.thread_handle not in kernel32.live
00287     # Nothing is left without an owner.
00288     assert api.retained_handles() == []
00289 
00290     # The primary error of the creation is never replaced by the
00291     # cancellation of a later finalizer.
00292     def fail_birth(kernel32, *args):
00293         return 0
00294 
00295     def cancel_terminate(kernel32, *args):
00296         raise SystemExit('terminate_cancelled')
00297 
00298     kernel32 = SyntheticKernel32(script={'GetProcessTimes': fail_birth,
00299                                          'TerminateProcess': cancel_terminate})
00300     api = WindowsApi(table=synthetic_table(kernel32))
00301     with pytest.raises(BrokerError) as info:
00302         api.create_process_suspended(executable='C:/tools/python.exe', arguments=['-s'],
00303                                      working_directory='C:/round', stdout_handle=0x21,
00304                                      stderr_handle=0x22, environment=None)
00305     assert info.value.code == 'BROKER_PROCESS_BIRTH_QUERY_FAILED'
00306     detail = info.value.detail or ''
00307     assert 'TerminateProcess:CANCELLED' in detail
00308     # The close of the handles of the process still ran: the
00309     # cancellation never skipped it.
00310     assert 'CloseHandle' in kernel32.names()
00311     assert kernel32.process_handle not in kernel32.live
00312     assert kernel32.thread_handle not in kernel32.live
