02519 # -- N07-R14: the startup owner releases its owner after the real exit -----
02520 
02521 def test_n07_r14_the_startup_owner_releases_its_owner_after_the_real_exit(tmp_path):
02522     """N07-R14 (M08): the attempt of a step is not the exit of its thread.
02523 
02524     Obligation: the end of the attempt of a startup step and the end of
02525     the thread that performs it are two different facts; the owner of the
02526     step is released only after a bounded join of the real thread; a
02527     thread that did not exit keeps its owner and the round never gets a
02528     new budget.  Entry: ``_StartupOwner.settle`` and
02529     ``BrokerLoop._run_startup``.  Trigger: the action of the step ends
02530     and the thread of the step needs one more tick to leave.  Oracle: the
02531     settle reports that the thread did not exit and the owner of the step
02532     is not released.  Negative: the event of the attempt never releases
02533     the owner.  Returned state: the round names the step that is still
02534     in flight.  Salvage: the thread of the step ends and it may be
02535     released.
02536     """
02537     class SlowThreadOwner(_StartupOwner):
02538         """A startup owner whose thread needs one more tick to leave."""
02539 
02540         gate = threading.Event()
02541 
02542         def _run(self, action):
02543             super()._run(action)
02544             self.gate.wait(5.0)
02545 
02546     SlowThreadOwner.gate = threading.Event()
02547     owner = SlowThreadOwner(name='arm', clock=time.monotonic,
02548                              deadline=time.monotonic() + 10.0)
02549     owner.start(lambda: 'done')
02550     try:
02551         assert owner.await_result() == 'done'
02552         # The attempt of the step ended but the thread of the step is
02553         # still inside it.
02554         assert owner._finished.is_set()
02555         assert owner.settle(0.2) is False, \
02556             'the event of the attempt was read as the exit of the thread'
02557         assert owner.in_flight is True
02558         SlowThreadOwner.gate.set()
02559         assert owner.settle(5.0) is True
02560         assert owner.in_flight is False
02561     finally:
02562         SlowThreadOwner.gate.set()
02563 
02564     # The loop releases the owner of a step only after the real exit of
02565     # its thread and it never publishes the cleanup deadline of the round
02566     # for a step that did not fail.
02567     core = BrokerCore(win32=RecordingWin32(), plan=sealed_plan(),
02568                       run_root=tmp_path)
02569     loop = BrokerLoop(core=core, transport=object(), plan=sealed_plan(),
02570                       nonce='a' * 32)
02571     loop._deadline_mono = time.monotonic() + 30.0
02572     SlowThreadOwner.gate = threading.Event()
02573     original = broker_module._StartupOwner
02574     broker_module._StartupOwner = SlowThreadOwner
02575     try:
02576         loop._run_startup('ARM', lambda: None)
02577         assert loop._startup_in_flight is not None, \
02578             'the owner of the step was released before the real exit'
02579         assert any(item.get('code') == 'BROKER_STARTUP_IN_FLIGHT_RETAINED'
02580                    for item in core.failures)
02581         assert loop.context.inflight(), 'the owner of the step was released'
02582         assert core.context.cleanup_deadline_value() is None, \
02583             'the startup published the cleanup deadline of the round'
02584     finally:
02585         broker_module._StartupOwner = original
02586         SlowThreadOwner.gate.set()
02587         for pending in loop.context.inflight():
02588             pending.settle(5.0)
02589 
02590 
02591 # -- N07-R15: the real chain is coordinated again by the same owner -------
02592 
02593 def test_n07_r15_the_real_chain_is_coordinated_again_by_the_same_owner(tmp_path):
02594     """N07-R15 (M09): the real chain of the round is coordinated again.
02595 
02596     Obligation: a call that returned is not a release: the real chain
02597     ``Session`` -> ``BrokerProcess`` -> ``StreamChannel`` -> ``Windows
02598     endpoint`` -> worker is coordinated again by the same owner when the
02599     first attempt did not confirm every item of it; the confirmed items
02600     are never closed twice.  Entry: ``NativeCustodySession.stop``,
02601     ``BrokerProcess.close`` and ``StreamChannel.close``.  Trigger: the
02602     original handle of the thread of a worker of an endpoint cannot be
02603     closed by the first attempt.  Oracle: the channel was closed twice,
02604     the handle of the thread was closed by the second attempt and the
02605     broker was released.  Negative: the high level never skips an
02606     unfinished item of the low level because a close returned.  Returned
02607     state: the evidence of the round names the incomplete facts of the
02608     first attempt.  Salvage: the second public stop of the round is the
02609     recovery entry.
02610 
02611     CORRECTION 08 (M09): the **fault** of the group is deterministic
02612     now.  The old group armed a global counter of two failing closes
02613     and the two workers of the two ends of the channel competed for it:
02614     the group could not name the original handle that the round was not
02615     able to close and the outcome depended on the order in which the
02616     two workers happened to be scheduled.  The fault is bound to **one**
02617     original handle of the thread of **one named** worker and it is
02618     released by an explicit act of the owner of the test, so that no
02619     other handle of the round is affected by it.
02620 
02621     The facts of the first attempt, of the second attempt and of the
02622     salvage are written on the disk **before** every assertion, so that
02623     a group that fails leaves its evidence behind and never depends on
02624     the representation of a failure of pytest.  The real chain is
02625     never replaced by a hard coded set of facts: every fact of the
02626     document is the fact that the production returned.
02627 
02628     The decision of the root of the correction 08 added four **bounded**
02629     sub scenarios of the **production** behaviour of the reader of the
02630     channel to this group.  The node id of the group, the order of the
02631     175 node ids and every original assertion of the group are
02632     unchanged:
02633 
02634     * the cancellation that the close of the channel really initiated is
02635       a named fact of the close, it is not the error of the reader and
02636       the strict facts of the close are the ones that prove the
02637       release;
02638     * a cancellation with the **same code** whose owner is another one
02639       keeps the error of the reader and the round stays unknown;
02640     * an ordinary failure of the read keeps its error and the round
02641       stays unknown;
02642     * a frame that never ends keeps the error of the partial frame and
02643       the round stays unknown.
02644 
02645     CORRECTION 09 (M09): the **order** of the cancellation of another
02646     owner is deterministic now.  The failed batch of the correction 08
02647     proved that the old owner of the test set the flag of the cancel
02648     **while** the main thread was already inside the first public stop:
02649     the close and the owner competed for the flag, the **first** owner
02650     won and the close won it, therefore the premise of the sub scenario
02651     (a cancellation of **another** owner) was never established.  The
02652     raw facts of that batch show the close cancellation as a named fact
02653     of the close and no error of the reader at all; they do **not**
02654     prove that the production swallowed a cancellation.
02655 
02656     The external owner is now a named object of the test: it waits for
02657     the explicit event that the reader of the chain is really inside its
02658     read, it sets the **same** flag of the **same** channel without
02659     naming an owner and it publishes an explicit confirmation.  The
02660     owner of the test waits for that confirmation, it checks that the
02661     flag is really set and that the origin of the cancellation is
02662     really unknown and **only then** it drives the first public stop.
02663     A failure of the owner is reported to the owner of the test and the
02664     exact thread of the owner is joined in the ``finally``: the thread
02665     of the owner never asserts, the main thread never drives the close
02666     after a failed owner and the round is never closed under a live
02667     worker.  There is no sleep, no polling and no rerun.
02668     """
02669     document = _live_plan_document()
02670     plan = validate_launch_plan(document, now=time.time())
02671     clock = time.monotonic
02672     wall = time.time
02673 
02674     # -- THE GROUP: the targeted fault of the original handle of a thread
02675     api = _F5Api()
02676     session, holder, channel_closes, channel_facts = _f5_build_chain(
02677         api, document, plan, clock, wall, tmp_path)
02678     target = None
02679     try:
02680         session.start()
02681         assert session.broker is holder['broker']
02682         # The **one** original handle of the thread that the fault of
02683         # the round is bound to: the handle of the thread of the reader
02684         # of the read end of the channel.  It is the first handle that
02685         # the round allocated and the round really used it: the READY
02686         # reply of the round was delivered through it.
02687         reader = holder['read_endpoint']._reader
02688         assert reader is not None, 'the reader of the channel never started'
02689         target = reader.thread_handle
02690         assert target is not None, 'the reader has no handle of its thread'
02691         assert api.thread_handles == [target], \
02692             'the fault would not be bound to one definite handle'
02693         api.fault_handle = target
02694 
02695         # The first public stop of the round.
02696         first = session.stop()
02697         # The facts of the first attempt are written **before** the
02698         # assertions of the first attempt.
02699         first_document = _f5_snapshot('first', session, api, holder,
02700                                       channel_facts, channel_closes, target)
02701         _f5_write('first', first_document)
02702         deadline = first_document['cleanup_deadline']
02703         # The fault of the round really fired and it fired on the one
02704         # handle that the owner of the test named.
02705         assert api.fault_hits >= 1, 'the targeted fault never fired'
02706         assert all(item['handle'] == target for item in api.close_ledger
02707                    if item['faulted'])
02708         # The unconfirmed item of the first attempt is the original
02709         # handle of the thread of the reader and it kept its owner.
02710         retained = holder['read_endpoint']._retained
02711         assert len(retained) == 1, \
02712             'the read endpoint did not retain exactly the targeted worker'
02713         assert retained[0].thread_handle == target
02714         assert retained[0].thread_handle_released is False
02715         # The broker of the round was not released without a proof.
02716         assert session.broker is holder['broker'], \
02717             'the broker was released without a proof'
02718         assert first['broker_retained'] is True
02719         incomplete = [failure for failure in first['failures']
02720                       if failure.startswith('CUSTODY_BROKER_CLOSE_INCOMPLETE')]
02721         assert incomplete, 'the incomplete facts were not named'
02722         assert len(channel_closes) == 1
02723         # The items that the first attempt confirmed are the items that
02724         # were really closed and they were closed exactly once.
02725         assert len(api.closed) == len(set(api.closed)), \
02726             'a handle was closed twice'
02727 
02728         # The owner of the test releases the fault: only the condition
02729         # of the fault is cleared and nothing else of the round changes.
02730         api.fault_released = True
02731 
02732         # The second public stop coordinates the items that the first
02733         # attempt did not confirm.
02734         second = session.stop()
02735         second_document = _f5_snapshot('second', session, api, holder,
02736                                        channel_facts, channel_closes, target)
02737         _f5_write('second', second_document)
02738         assert len(channel_closes) == 2, \
02739             'the high level skipped the unfinished item of the low level'
02740         # The item that was not confirmed really succeeded.
02741         assert target in api.closed, 'the targeted handle was never closed'
02742         # The items that were confirmed by the first attempt were never
02743         # closed a second time.
02744         assert len(api.closed) == len(set(api.closed)), \
02745             'a handle was closed twice'
02746         assert holder['read_endpoint']._retained == []
02747         # The original deadline of the cleanup was never reset.
02748         assert second_document['cleanup_deadline'] == deadline
02749         # The broker of the round is released by the facts of the close
02750         # and not by a name.
02751         assert session.broker is None
02752         assert second['broker_retained'] is False
02753     finally:
02754         # The salvage of the owner of the test is bounded and its facts
02755         # are written as well.
02756         try:
02757             session.stop()
02758         except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the evidence keeps the failure
02759             pass
02760         _f5_write('salvage', _f5_snapshot('salvage', session, api, holder,
02761                                            channel_facts, channel_closes,
02762                                            target))
02763 
02764     # -- SUB SCENARIO ONE: the cancellation that the close initiated ------
02765     # The reader of the chain is still inside its read when the round is
02766     # closed: the close cancels it and the cancellation is a **named
02767     # fact of the close**, not a failure of the reader.  The strict
02768     # facts of the close are the ones that prove the release.
02769     api = _F5Api(block_the_reader=True)
02770     session, holder, channel_closes, channel_facts = _f5_build_chain(
02771         api, document, plan, clock, wall, tmp_path)
02772     try:
02773         session.start()
02774         first = session.stop()
02775         close_document = _f5_snapshot('close-cancellation', session, api,
02776                                       holder, channel_facts, channel_closes,
02777                                       None)
02778         _f5_write('close-cancellation', close_document)
02779         facts = close_document['broker_close_facts'] or {}
02780         channel_fact = channel_facts[-1] if channel_facts else {}
02781         # The cancellation of the reader was initiated by the close of
02782         # the channel: it is named with its code, its source and its
02783         # order and it is not the error of the reader.
02784         cancellation = facts.get('close_cancellation')
02785         assert cancellation, 'the cancellation of the close was not named'
02786         assert cancellation['code'] == 'BROKER_PIPE_CANCELLED'
02787         assert cancellation['source'] == 'CHANNEL_CLOSE'
02788         assert cancellation['sequence'] == 1
02789         assert channel_fact.get('reader_error') is None, \
02790             'the cancellation of the close was taken for an error of the reader'
02791         assert facts.get('channel_reader_error') is None
02792         # The strict facts of the close are the ones that prove the
02793         # release and they are really confirmed.
02794         assert facts.get('channel_closed') is True
02795         assert facts.get('read_end_closed') is True
02796         assert facts.get('write_end_closed') is True
02797         assert facts.get('channel_retained_workers') == 0
02798         assert facts.get('worker_thread_handle_released') is True
02799         assert facts.get('channel_reader_finished') is True
02800         # The broker of the round is really released.
02801         assert session.broker is None
02802         assert first['broker_retained'] is False
02803     finally:
02804         try:
02805             session.stop()
02806         except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the evidence keeps the failure
02807             pass
02808 
02809     # -- SUB SCENARIO TWO: a cancellation of another owner ----------------
02810     # Another owner of the cancel flag of the channel sets it and it
02811     # names no owner: the cancellation of the close may never be taken
02812     # for it and the error of the reader is kept.
02813     #
02814     # CORRECTION 09: the order of the two facts is now **deterministic**.
02815     # The old owner of the test waited for the reader of the chain to
02816     # be inside its read and it set the flag of the cancel **while** the
02817     # main thread was already inside the first public stop: the close
02818     # and the owner competed for the flag and the **first** owner won.
02819     # The raw facts of the failed batch of the correction 08 show the
02820     # close winning it, therefore the premise of the sub scenario was
02821     # never established and the assertion of the error of the reader
02822     # could not hold.
02823     #
02824     # The owner of the test now waits for the **explicit confirmation**
02825     # of the external owner, it checks that the flag is really set and
02826     # that the origin of the cancellation is really **unknown** and only
02827     # then it drives the first public stop.  The order of the two facts
02828     # is therefore the order of the test and it is not the order of the
02829     # scheduler.
02830     #
02831     # The scenario drives **two** consecutive public stops of the round.
02832     # The second attempt copies the facts of the first one: the error of
02833     # the reader is the **history** of the round and a retry is not an
02834     # amnesty, so the error is still there, the round is still unknown
02835     # and the broker is still kept.
02836     api = _F5Api(block_the_reader=True)
02837     session, holder, channel_closes, channel_facts = _f5_build_chain(
02838         api, document, plan, clock, wall, tmp_path)
02839     external_owner = _F5ExternalCancelOwner(api, holder)
02840     owner_thread = threading.Thread(target=external_owner.run,
02841                                     name='lvw-n07-r15-unrelated-owner')
02842     owner_thread.start()
02843     try:
02844         session.start()
02845         # The owner of the test waits for the **confirmation** of the
02846         # external owner.  The wait is bounded and its return is
02847         # checked: the main thread never drives the close after an
02848         # owner that died without a word.
02849         assert external_owner.published.wait(30.0), \
02850             'the external owner never confirmed its cancellation'
02851         if external_owner.error is not None:
02852             # The failure of the owner is the failure of the test: it is
02853             # never swallowed by a worker thread and the main thread
02854             # never executes the close after a failed owner.
02855             raise external_owner.error
02856         channel = holder['channel']
02857         # The flag of the cancel is really set and the origin of the
02858         # cancellation is really **unknown**: the flag names no owner,
02859         # therefore the close of the channel may never be taken for the
02860         # owner of this cancellation.
02861         assert external_owner.flag_set is True, \
02862             'the external owner did not set the flag of the cancel'
02863         assert channel._cancel.is_set(), \
02864             'the flag of the cancel was not set'
02865         assert channel._cancel.owner() is None, \
02866             'the flag of the cancel named an owner'
02867         assert external_owner.flag_owner is None, \
02868             'the flag of the cancel named an owner'
02869         first = session.stop()
02870         unrelated_document = _f5_snapshot('unrelated-cancellation', session,
02871                                           api, holder, channel_facts,
02872                                           channel_closes, None)
02873         # The confirmation of the external owner is part of the bounded
02874         # facts of the phase: the evidence of the origin of the
02875         # cancellation is on the disk and it is not a claim of the test.
02876         unrelated_document['external_cancel_owner'] = {
02877             'published': bool(external_owner.published.is_set()),
02878             'flag_set': external_owner.flag_set,
02879             'flag_owner': external_owner.flag_owner,
02880             'read_waiting': bool(api.read_waiting.is_set()),
02881             'thread_name': owner_thread.name,
02882             'thread_is_alive': bool(owner_thread.is_alive()),
02883         }
02884         _f5_write('unrelated-cancellation', unrelated_document)
02885         facts = unrelated_document['broker_close_facts'] or {}
02886         # The error of the reader is kept: the cancellation of another
02887         # owner is not a normal fact of the close.
02888         assert facts.get('channel_reader_error') == 'BROKER_PIPE_CANCELLED', \
02889             'the cancellation of another owner was swallowed'
02890         assert facts.get('close_cancellation') is None, \
02891             'the cancellation of another owner was taken for the close'
02892         # The round stays unknown and the original broker is kept.
02893         assert session.broker is not None
02894         assert first['broker_retained'] is True
02895         assert first['state'] == 'UNKNOWN_RETAINED'
02896         incomplete = [failure for failure in first['failures']
02897                       if failure.startswith('CUSTODY_BROKER_CLOSE_INCOMPLETE')]
02898         assert incomplete, 'the incomplete facts were not named'
02899         assert any(failure.endswith('channel_reader_error')
02900                    for failure in incomplete)
02901 
02902         # -- THE SECOND PUBLIC STOP OF THE ROUND -------------------------
02903         # The second attempt of the close copies the facts of the first
02904         # one.  The error of the reader is kept by the copy: it is the
02905         # history of the round and the broker may never be released
02906         # because the second attempt copied the facts of the first one.
02907         second = session.stop()
02908         second_document = _f5_snapshot('unrelated-cancellation-second',
02909                                        session, api, holder, channel_facts,
02910                                        channel_closes, None)
02911         _f5_write('unrelated-cancellation-second', second_document)
02912         second_facts = second_document['broker_close_facts'] or {}
02913         assert second_facts.get('channel_reader_error') == 'BROKER_PIPE_CANCELLED', \
02914             'the second attempt lost the error of the reader'
02915         assert second_facts.get('channel_reader_error_source') == 'REPEATED_CLOSE', \
02916             'the second attempt did not carry the error of the reader'
02917         assert second_facts.get('close_cancellation') is None
02918         assert session.broker is not None, \
02919             'the broker was released by the copy of the facts'
02920         assert second['broker_retained'] is True
02921         assert second['state'] == 'UNKNOWN_RETAINED'
02922         second_incomplete = [failure for failure in second['failures']
02923                              if failure.startswith('CUSTODY_BROKER_CLOSE_INCOMPLETE')]
02924         assert second_incomplete, \
02925             'the second attempt did not name the incomplete facts'
02926         assert any(failure.endswith('channel_reader_error')
02927                    for failure in second_incomplete), \
02928             'the second attempt released the broker with the copy of the facts'
02929     finally:
02930         # The **exact** thread of the owner of the test is joined and it
02931         # is never left behind: the round is not closed under a live
02932         # worker and the exit of the process is never the end of a
02933         # thread.
02934         owner_thread.join(30.0)
02935         assert not owner_thread.is_alive(), \
02936             'the owner of the round was left behind'
02937         try:
02938             session.stop()
02939         except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the evidence keeps the failure
02940             pass
02941 
02942     # -- SUB SCENARIO THREE: an ordinary failure of the read -------------
02943     # A failure of the read is not a cancellation and it is not a close
02944     # of the round: its error is kept and the round stays unknown.  The
02945     # scenario drives **two** consecutive public stops: the error of the
02946     # read survives the second one and the broker is never released by
02947     # the copy of the facts.
02948     api = _F5Api(read_fault=RuntimeError('read_fault'))
02949     session, holder, channel_closes, channel_facts = _f5_build_chain(
02950         api, document, plan, clock, wall, tmp_path)
02951     try:
02952         session.start()
02953         # CORRECTION 10: the ordinary failure of the read is published
02954         # by the **owner of the test** and it is never unlocked by the
02955         # write of the stop.  The owner waits for the reader to be
02956         # really inside its read, it publishes the failure and it waits
02957         # for the failure to be **really** published by the worker of
02958         # the read.  The close of the round may never turn a failure
02959         # that was published first into a benign cancellation.
02960         assert api.read_waiting.wait(15.0), \
02961             'the reader of the chain was never inside its read'
02962         api.publish_read_fault()
02963         assert api.fault_observed.wait(15.0), \
02964             'the ordinary failure of the read never happened'
02965         reader = holder['read_endpoint']._reader
02966         assert reader is not None, 'the reader of the chain never started'
02967         assert reader.outcome_published.wait(15.0), \
02968             'the outcome of the read was never published'
02969         first = session.stop()
02970         fault_document = _f5_snapshot('read-fault', session, api, holder,
02971                                        channel_facts, channel_closes, None)
02972         _f5_write('read-fault', fault_document)
02973         facts = fault_document['broker_close_facts'] or {}
02974         assert facts.get('channel_reader_error') == 'RuntimeError', \
02975             'the failure of the read was swallowed'
02976         assert facts.get('close_cancellation') is None
02977         assert session.broker is not None
02978         assert first['broker_retained'] is True
02979         assert first['state'] == 'UNKNOWN_RETAINED'
02980         incomplete = [failure for failure in first['failures']
02981                       if failure.startswith('CUSTODY_BROKER_CLOSE_INCOMPLETE')]
02982         assert any(failure.endswith('channel_reader_error')
02983                    for failure in incomplete)
02984 
02985         # -- THE SECOND PUBLIC STOP OF THE ROUND -------------------------
02986         second = session.stop()
02987         second_document = _f5_snapshot('read-fault-second', session, api,
02988                                        holder, channel_facts, channel_closes,
02989                                        None)
02990         _f5_write('read-fault-second', second_document)
02991         second_facts = second_document['broker_close_facts'] or {}
02992         assert second_facts.get('channel_reader_error') == 'RuntimeError', \
02993             'the second attempt lost the error of the read'
02994         assert second_facts.get('channel_reader_error_source') == 'REPEATED_CLOSE'
02995         assert second_facts.get('close_cancellation') is None
02996         assert session.broker is not None, \
02997             'the broker was released by the copy of the facts'
02998         assert second['broker_retained'] is True
02999         assert second['state'] == 'UNKNOWN_RETAINED'
03000         second_incomplete = [failure for failure in second['failures']
03001                              if failure.startswith('CUSTODY_BROKER_CLOSE_INCOMPLETE')]
03002         assert any(failure.endswith('channel_reader_error')
03003                    for failure in second_incomplete), \
03004             'the second attempt released the broker with the copy of the facts'
03005     finally:
03006         try:
03007             session.stop()
03008         except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the evidence keeps the failure
03009             pass
03010 
03011     # -- SUB SCENARIO FOUR: a frame that never ends -----------------------
03012     # The stream ends in the middle of a frame: the partial frame is a
03013     # named failure and it is never dropped as a normal end.  The
03014     # scenario drives **two** consecutive public stops: the partial frame
03015     # survives the second one and the broker is never released by the
03016     # copy of the facts.
03017     api = _F5Api(partial_frame=b'{"schema": "lvw.s3-native-custody-channel/1"')
03018     session, holder, channel_closes, channel_facts = _f5_build_chain(
03019         api, document, plan, clock, wall, tmp_path)
03020     try:
03021         session.start()
03022         # CORRECTION 10: the frame that never ends is published by the
03023         # **owner of the test** and the end of the stream follows it.
03024         # The stop of the round never unlocks a frame that should have
03025         # been read before it.
03026         assert api.read_waiting.wait(15.0), \
03027             'the reader of the chain was never inside its read'
03028         api.publish_partial_frame()
03029         assert api.eof_observed.wait(15.0), \
03030             'the end of the stream was never observed'
03031         first = session.stop()
03032         partial_document = _f5_snapshot('partial-frame', session, api,
03033                                         holder, channel_facts, channel_closes,
03034                                         None)
03035         _f5_write('partial-frame', partial_document)
03036         facts = partial_document['broker_close_facts'] or {}
03037         assert facts.get('channel_reader_error') == 'BROKER_CHANNEL_PARTIAL_FRAME', \
03038             'the partial frame was dropped as a normal end'
03039         assert facts.get('close_cancellation') is None
03040         assert session.broker is not None
03041         assert first['broker_retained'] is True
03042         assert first['state'] == 'UNKNOWN_RETAINED'
03043         incomplete = [failure for failure in first['failures']
03044                       if failure.startswith('CUSTODY_BROKER_CLOSE_INCOMPLETE')]
03045         assert any(failure.endswith('channel_reader_error')
03046                    for failure in incomplete)
03047 
03048         # -- THE SECOND PUBLIC STOP OF THE ROUND -------------------------
03049         second = session.stop()
03050         second_document = _f5_snapshot('partial-frame-second', session, api,
03051                                        holder, channel_facts, channel_closes,
03052                                        None)
03053         _f5_write('partial-frame-second', second_document)
03054         second_facts = second_document['broker_close_facts'] or {}
03055         assert second_facts.get('channel_reader_error') == 'BROKER_CHANNEL_PARTIAL_FRAME', \
03056             'the second attempt lost the partial frame'
03057         assert second_facts.get('channel_reader_error_source') == 'REPEATED_CLOSE'
03058         assert second_facts.get('close_cancellation') is None
03059         assert session.broker is not None, \
03060             'the broker was released by the copy of the facts'
03061         assert second['broker_retained'] is True
03062         assert second['state'] == 'UNKNOWN_RETAINED'
03063         second_incomplete = [failure for failure in second['failures']
03064                              if failure.startswith('CUSTODY_BROKER_CLOSE_INCOMPLETE')]
03065         assert any(failure.endswith('channel_reader_error')
03066                    for failure in second_incomplete), \
03067             'the second attempt released the broker with the copy of the facts'
03068     finally:
03069         try:
03070             session.stop()
03071         except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the evidence keeps the failure
03072             pass

03074 
03075 # -- N07-R16: the endpoint recomputes the state of its workers ------------
03076 
03077 def test_n07_r16_the_endpoint_recomputes_the_state_of_its_workers():
03078     """N07-R16 (M09): the state of the workers of an endpoint is real.
03079 
03080     Obligation: the endpoint recomputes the live and the retained state
03081     of its workers on every close; a worker that really ended and whose
03082     handle of its thread was released is not retained any more; a worker
03083     whose handle could not be closed is retained and it may be released
03084     by a later close; a closed file never locks the recovery of the
03085     handle of a thread.  Entry: ``_WindowsPipeEndpoint.close`` and
03086     ``_WindowsPipeWorker.release_thread_handle``.  Trigger: the close of
03087     the handle of the thread of the worker fails twice.  Oracle: the
03088     first close keeps the worker, the second close releases it and the
03089     facts are recomputed and not copied.  Negative: the retained list
03090     never names the same worker twice.  Returned state: the facts of the
03091     endpoint name the state of the workers.  Salvage: the second close
03092     of the owner releases the handle.
03093     """
03094     closed = []
03095     failures = {'left': 2}
03096 
03097     class Api:
03098         def duplicate_current_thread_handle(self):
03099             return 701
03100 
03101         def cancel_synchronous_io(self, handle):
03102             return 'REQUESTED'
03103 
03104         def write_stream(self, handle, data):
03105             return len(data)
03106 
03107         def close_handle(self, handle):
03108             if failures['left'] > 0:
03109                 failures['left'] -= 1
03110                 raise BrokerError('BROKER_HANDLE_CLOSE_FAILED')
03111             closed.append(handle)
03112 
03113     clock = ManualClock(1000.0)
03114     endpoint = _WindowsPipeEndpoint(file_object=_FakeFile('write'), name='inprocess',
03115                                     api=Api(), clock=clock, handle=702)
03116     endpoint.write(b'x', clock.value + 5.0)
03117     assert endpoint._writer is not None
03118     first = endpoint.close()
03119     # The handle of the thread of the worker could not be closed: the
03120     # worker is retained and the file is closed all the same.
03121     assert first['closed'] is True
03122     assert first['retained_workers'] == 1
03123     assert first['worker_thread_handle_released'] is False
03124     assert len(endpoint._retained) == 1
03125     # The second close recomputes the state of the workers: the handle of
03126     # the thread of the worker is released now and the worker is not
03127     # retained any more.
03128     second = endpoint.close()
03129     assert second['retained_workers'] == 0
03130     assert second['worker_thread_handle_released'] is True
03131     assert endpoint._retained == []
03132     assert closed == [701]
03133 
03134 
03135 # -- N07-R17: the second generation of the close has its own event --------
03136 
03137 def test_n07_r17_the_second_generation_of_the_close_has_its_own_event(tmp_path):
03138     """N07-R17 (M09): every attempt of the close owns its own event.
03139 
03140     Obligation: the attempt of the close that is running now owns its
03141     own event and its own generation; the end of an older attempt never
03142     releases a waiter of the attempt that is running now; the waiter
03143     never holds the lock while it waits and the deadline of the cleanup
03144     is never reset.  Entry: ``NativeCustodySession._recover_close`` and
03145     ``NativeCustodySession._await_close``.  Trigger: the first attempt
03146     did not confirm the release and the recovery of the second
03147     generation is in flight when a competitor arrives.  Oracle: the
03148     competitor waits for the second generation and it is released by the
03149     end of that generation.  Negative: the old event never releases the
03150     waiter of the new generation.  Returned state: the generation of the
03151     close is named in the evidence.  Salvage: the release of the second
03152     attempt ends the wait.
03153     """
03154     session, holder, clock = make_session(
03155         tmp_path, close_facts=dict(COMPLETE_CLOSE_FACTS,
03156                                    process_handle_released=False))
03157     session.start()
03158     peer = holder['peer']
03159     deadline = session._cleanup_deadline_for()
03160     session.stop()
03161     assert session.broker is peer
03162     # The condition of the round recovered and the recovery of the second
03163     # generation is in flight.
03164     entered = threading.Event()
03165     release = threading.Event()
03166 
03167     def blocking_close():
03168         entered.set()
03169         release.wait(5.0)
03170         return dict(COMPLETE_CLOSE_FACTS)
03171 
03172     peer.close = blocking_close
03173     recovery = threading.Thread(target=session.stop)
03174     recovery.start()
03175     try:
03176         assert entered.wait(5.0), 'the recovery never reached the close'
03177         outcome = {}
03178 
03179         def competitor():
03180             outcome['result'] = session._await_close()
03181 
03182         waiter = threading.Thread(target=competitor)
03183         waiter.start()
03184         waiter.join(0.3)
03185         assert waiter.is_alive(), \
03186             'the end of the older attempt released the waiter of the new one'
03187         release.set()
03188         waiter.join(5.0)
03189         assert not waiter.is_alive()
03190         assert outcome.get('result') is True
03191     finally:
03192         release.set()
03193         recovery.join(5.0)
03194     # The second generation published its own event and the deadline of
03195     # the cleanup was never reset.
03196     assert session._close_generation == 1
03197     assert session._cleanup_deadline_for() == deadline
03198     assert session.broker is None
03199 
03200 
03201 # -- N07-R18: the first public stop waits for the real closer -------------
03202 
03203 def test_n07_r18_the_first_public_stop_waits_for_the_real_closer(tmp_path):
03204     """N07-R18 (M09): the public stop coordinates the real closer.
03205 
03206     Obligation: every exit of the public stop of the round - the normal
03207     one, the cancelled one and the ordinary failure - coordinates the
03208     current owner of the close and the **real** closer; the end of the
03209     attempt of the close is never the end of the thread that performs
03210     it; a closer that is still alive when the deadline arrives keeps its
03211     owner and the round stays unknown.  Entry:
03212     ``NativeCustodySession.stop`` and
03213     ``NativeCustodySession._await_closer_exit``.  Trigger: the closer
03214     published the end of its attempt and it is still alive when the
03215     first public stop arrives.  Oracle: the public stop waits for the
03216     real exit of the closer.  Negative: only a helper or a repeated
03217     stop never proves the coordination of the closer.  Returned state:
03218     the evidence of the round names both facts.  Salvage: the closer
03219     ends and the round is closed.
03220     """
03221     session, _holder, _clock = make_session(tmp_path,
03222                                             plan=_live_plan_document(),
03223                                             clock=ManualClock(1000.0))
03224     session.start()
03225     # The round really runs: the lease of the plan is live and the check
03226     # of the lease was never relaxed.
03227     assert session.state == 'RUNNING'
03228     entered = threading.Event()
03229     release = threading.Event()
03230     waiting = threading.Event()
03231     original_finish = session._finish_close
03232     original_await = session._await_close
03233 
03234     def slow_finish():
03235         original_finish()
03236         if threading.current_thread() is session._closer:
03237             # The closer published the end of its attempt and it is
03238             # still alive: it waits for the owner of the round.
03239             entered.set()
03240             release.wait(5.0)
03241 
03242     def announced_await():
03243         # The public stop entered the wait for the real closer.
03244         waiting.set()
03245         return original_await()
03246 
03247     session._finish_close = slow_finish
03248     session._await_close = announced_await
03249     try:
03250         assert session._start_bounded_close() is True
03251         assert entered.wait(5.0), 'the closer never ended its attempt'
03252         evidence = session.evidence()
03253         assert evidence['close_state'] == 'FINISHED'
03254         assert evidence['closer_alive'] is True
03255         assert evidence['closer_exited'] is False
03256         outcome = {}
03257 
03258         def stopper():
03259             outcome['evidence'] = session.stop()
03260 
03261         thread = threading.Thread(target=stopper)
03262         thread.start()
03263         # The public stop really waits for the real closer.  The check
03264         # waits for the event that announces the wait and it never uses
03265         # a sleep to guess it.
03266         assert waiting.wait(5.0), 'the public stop never waited for the closer'
03267         assert thread.is_alive(), \
03268             'the public stop returned before the real closer ended'
03269         # The owner of the round releases the closer.
03270         release.set()
03271         thread.join(10.0)
03272         assert not thread.is_alive()
03273         final = outcome.get('evidence')
03274         assert final is not None
03275         assert final['closer_alive'] is False
03276         assert final['closer_exited'] is True
03277         assert final['close_state'] == 'FINISHED'
03278     finally:
03279         release.set()
03280         closer = session._closer
03281         if closer is not None:
03282             closer.join(5.0)
